  ### 关键约束

  **布局（D-T2）：**
  - 主从双栏：左任务列表 flex:1 + 右面板 360px
  - 编辑器全宽对话框 rw-dialog-xl + rw-dialog-scroll-body
  - Tab: 活动 | 历史，keep-alive

  **调度类型（D-T5 — 关键类型变化）：**
  - QSelect 选项：immediate / timer / event
  - schedule.kind 动态渲染：
    - immediate: 无额外配置
    - timer: 间隔 + 次数归口 TaskStopCondition.maxIterations + 无限循环 toggle
    - event: 条件编辑器（ConditionTerm 列表，至少一条）
  - 列定义字段：schedule.kind（不是 schedulingMode）
  - QChip 映射用 scheduleKindMap

  **状态推导（D-T7）：**
  - running + schedule.kind='event' → "等待触发"
  - running + schedule.kind='timer' → "等待调度"
  - taskStatusMap 中定义完整映射含推导状态
  - StatusBadge :status-map="taskStatusMap"

  **Phase 2 功能隐藏：**
  - fieldVariations、exitCondition、step repeat 类型已就绪
  - UI 不渲染编辑器，高级配置区标注"待后续版本"

  **Service API（已修正）：**
  - stopAll(): number
  - retryTask(id): TaskInstanceState | undefined（同步）
  - updateTask(id, definition): TaskInstanceState | undefined（仅 created 态）

  **步骤编辑器：**
  - SendStep: FrameSelector + SendTargetSelector + FieldEditWidget(:values="step.config.userFieldValues") + intervalAfterMs + 可选 repeat
  - WaitConditionStep: ConditionTerm 列表 + timeoutMs + onTimeout(continue/skip/fail)
  - DelayStep: durationMs
  - 步骤用 QExpansionItem 展开/折叠

  **数据流（P4/O3/O4）：**
  - 任务列表 shallowRef + watch(selector 结果) 手动更新
  - 轮询用 usePolling(fn, 1000)
  - 不变映射（statusMap、kindMap、labels）放模块级（O4）
  - 步骤类型标签如出现 2+ 处相同模式提取（O3）

  **异步操作：**
  - start/pause/resume/stop/remove/retry 用 useAsyncAction().execute
  - 通知用 useNotify()

  ### 验证
  - pnpm -C rewrite build
  - pnpm -C rewrite lint

13	---
14	
15	# Rewrite 前端 UI 规范
16	
17	样式 token 规则和 UnoCSS 结构性布局职责划分见 CLAUDE.md "目录与职责"，token 定义见 `rewrite/src/css/tokens/`，本文不重复。代码质量规则见 `rewrite-quality-rules.md`。自检 checklist 见 `rewrite-frontend-checklist.md`。
18	
19	---
20	
21	## 1. Quasar 组件使用
22	
23	### Q1. UI 需求优先从 Quasar 内建方案解决
24	
25	不引入第三方 UI 库（ag-Grid、Lucide、vue-sonner 等）。
26	
27	```typescript
28	// ❌ 第三方 toast
29	import { toast } from 'vue-sonner'; toast.success('ok');
30	// ❌ 第三方图标
31	import { Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next';
32	
33	// ✅ Quasar 内建
34	$q.notify({ type: 'positive', message: 'ok' });
35	<q-icon name="o_delete" />
36	```
37	
38	### Q2. Quasar prop 控制视觉行为，不用 CSS 覆盖
39	
40	```vue
41	<!-- ❌ -->
42	<q-input style="padding: 4px; height: 32px;" />
43	<q-btn style="box-shadow: none; background: transparent;" />
44	
45	<!-- ✅ -->
46	<q-input dense />
47	<q-btn flat color="primary" />
48	```
49	
50	### Q3. 颜色使用 Quasar brand prop，不硬编码
51	
52	```vue
53	<!-- ❌ -->
54	<q-btn style="background: #1f6feb; color: white;" />
55	<q-badge style="color: green;">已连接</q-badge>
56	
57	<!-- ✅ -->
58	<q-btn color="primary" />
59	<q-badge color="positive">已连接</q-badge>
60	```
61	
62	### Q4. 确认和反馈走 Quasar plugin
63	
64	删除确认用 `$q.dialog()`，操作反馈用 `$q.notify()`。不用 `window.confirm/alert`。
65	
66	```typescript
67	// ❌
68	if (window.confirm('确定删除？')) doDelete();
69	
70	// ✅
71	$q.dialog({ title: '确认删除', message: '不可恢复', cancel: true }).onOk(doDelete);
72	```
73	
74	### Q5. 删除操作必须二次确认
75	
76	级联删除用项目封装的二次确认组件（需输入关键词）。批量操作必须显示进度。
77	
78	---
79	
80	## 2. 表格
81	
82	### T1. 表格用 QTable，大列表开虚拟滚动
83	
84	行数可能超 100 必须开 `virtual-scroll` + 设 `virtual-scroll-item-size` + 设容器高度。
85	
86	```vue
87	<!-- ❌ 10000 行全渲染 -->
88	<q-table :rows="allData" />
89	
90	<!-- ✅ -->
91	<q-table
92	  :rows="data" virtual-scroll
93	  :virtual-scroll-item-size="48"
94	  :table-style="{ maxHeight: 'calc(100vh - 200px)' }"
95	  :rows-per-page-options="[0]"
96	  row-key="id"
97	/>
98	```
99	
100	### T2. 列数 ≥ 3 或有自定义渲染时，列定义抽到 `columns.ts`
101	
102	列宽策略：文本列自适应、状态列设 min-width、操作列固定宽度。不用百分比宽度。QTable `headerStyle` / `style` 中使用 px 值是 API 要求，不视为硬编码违规。
103	
104	### T3. 大数据集排序和分页走 service 层
105	
106	数据量 > 100 行时排序由 feature service 处理，分页用 QTable server-side 模式 + `@request`。不前端排序分页混合。
107	
108	### T4. 空状态和 loading 必须显式处理
109	
110	```vue
111	<q-table :loading="isLoading">
112	  <template #no-data>
113	    <div class="text-center q-pa-lg text-muted">暂无数据</div>
114	  </template>
115	</q-table>
116	```
117	
118	### T5. 行选中用 QTable selection，选中色走 token
119	
120	```vue
121	<!-- ❌ 在数据对象上加 _selected 字段，硬编码背景色 -->
122	:style="{ background: selectedRows.includes(row) ? '#e8f1ff' : '' }"
123	
124	<!-- ✅ -->
125	<q-table selection="multiple" v-model:selected="selected" />
126	```
127	
128	---
129	
130	## 3. 表单
131	
132	### F1. 表单用 QForm + Quasar 表单组件
133	
134	不混用 HTML 原生表单元素。校验用 QInput `:rules` prop（简单）或 feature service（复杂）。不用第三方校验库。
135	
136	```typescript
137	// ✅ 简单校验
138	<q-input v-model="name" :rules="[val => !!val || '请输入名称']" />
139	
140	// ✅ 复杂校验在 service 层
141	const result = await taskService.validateConfig(config);
142	```
143	
144	### F2. 校验错误用组件 error slot，不用 toast
145	
146	错误信息走 `:error` + `:error-message`，颜色走 `status-danger` token。
147	
148	### F3. 超过 20 个字段必须拆分子组件
149	
150	每个子组件管理自己的响应式数据，隔离 Vue 依赖追踪范围。动态字段超 50 用 QVirtualScroll 承载。QInput 校验用 `lazy-rules="ondemand"`。
151	
152	### F4. 提交按钮必须 disable/loading 防重复
153	
154	```vue
155	<q-btn label="提交" :loading="isSubmitting" @click="submit" />
156	```
157	
158	---
159	
160	## 4. 弹窗
161	
162	### D1. 弹窗用 QDialog，双向绑定 + @hide 清理
163	
164	```vue
165	<!-- ❌ 单向绑定，无法关闭 -->
166	<q-dialog :model-value="show">
167	
168	<!-- ✅ -->
169	<q-dialog v-model="show" @hide="resetForm">
170	  <q-card>...</q-card>
171	</q-dialog>
172	```
173	
174	### D2. 弹窗宽度用语义 class，不硬编码
175	
176	定义在 `css/tokens/_size.scss` + `css/layers/_utilities.scss`：
177	
178	| class | min-width | 用途 |
179	|-------|-----------|------|
180	| `rw-dialog-sm` | 400px | 单字段确认、简单选择 |
181	| `rw-dialog-md` | 560px | 表单、配置编辑 |
182	| `rw-dialog-lg` | 720px | 多字段编辑、预览 |
183	| `rw-dialog-xl` | 960px | 复合编辑器（任务编辑器、帧编辑器） |
184	
185	所有 class 带 `max-width: 80vw` 响应式下限。禁止在 `<q-card>` 或 scoped SCSS 中硬编码 `min-width`/`max-width` px 值。
186	
187	```vue
188	<!-- ❌ -->
189	<q-card style="width: 600px; max-width: 80vw;">
190	
191	<!-- ✅ -->
192	<q-card class="rw-dialog-md">
193	```
194	
195	---
196	
197	## 5. 页面布局
198	
199	### L1. 页面内边距用 `p-page` / `p-page-compact` UnoCSS class
200	
201	工具栏与内容区间距用 `gap-4`。结构性布局（间距、flex、grid、display、position、sizing）全部走 UnoCSS utility class，不在 `<style>` 中声明。
202	
203	### L2. 侧边栏用 QDrawer，宽度走 `app-drawer` token（232px）
204	
205	### L3. 桌面不做移动端适配
206	
207	不用 xs/sm 断点、不引入 touch 手势库。响应式仅处理窗口缩小，下限 `breakpoint.page-compact`（720px）。
208	
209	---
210	
211	## 6. 颜色与状态色
212	
213	### C1. 颜色只用 semantic-colors token 或 Quasar brand 色
214	
215	禁止在 style/class/SCSS 中硬编码 hex/rgb/hsl。
216	
217	```scss
218	// ✅ SCSS
219	.error-text { color: rw-color('status-danger'); }
220	// ✅ CSS 变量
221	background: var(--rw-color-surface-selected);
222	// ✅ Quasar
223	<q-badge color="negative">
224	```
225	
226	### C2. 状态色固定映射
227	
228	| 语义 | Token | Quasar color |
229	|------|-------|-------------|
230	| 成功/正常/在线 | `status-success` | `positive` |
231	| 警告/注意 | `status-warning` | `warning` |
232	| 错误/失败/离线 | `status-danger` | `negative` |
233	| 信息/提示 | `status-info` | `info` |
234	| 选中/激活 | `surface-selected` | — |
235	| 禁用/静默 | `text-muted` | — |
236	
237	### C3. 文字颜色分级
238	
239	| 层级 | Token | 用途 |
240	|------|-------|------|
241	| 主要 | `text-primary` | 标题、重要数据 |
242	| 默认 | `text-default` | 正文、表格内容 |
243	| 次要 | `text-secondary` | 描述、辅助信息 |
244	| 弱化 | `text-muted` | 时间戳、placeholder |
245	| 最弱 | `text-subtle` | 禁用态、hint |
246	
247	### C4. Token 消费 2+ 次必须提取语义 class
248	
249	禁止在模板 inline style 中直接使用 color/border/background token。同一 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次时，必须提取为语义 class。
250	
251	```vue
252	<!-- ❌ -->
253	<span style="color: var(--rw-color-text-muted)">ID</span>
254	<span style="color: var(--rw-color-text-primary)">{{ value }}</span>
255	<div style="border-bottom: var(--rw-border-width-subtle) solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)">
256	
257	<!-- ✅ -->
258	<span class="rw-text-label">ID</span>
259	<span class="rw-text-value">{{ value }}</span>
260	<div class="rw-divider-b">
261	```
262	
263	---
264	
265	## 7. 数据展示
266	
267	### V1. 数字格式化
268	
269	整数不带小数，大数加千分位，百分比带 `%`，浮点保留有效精度。格式化函数放 `shared/`。
270	
271	```vue
272	<!-- ❌ --> <span>{{ rawValue }}</span>  <!-- 1234567.890000001 -->
273	<!-- ✅ --> <span>{{ formatNumber(rawValue) }}</span>  <!-- 1,234,567.89 -->
274	```
275	
276	### V2. 时间格式化
277	
278	日期时间 `YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss`，仅日期 `YYYY-MM-DD`。相对时间只用于实时日志，不用于历史记录。不直接展示 ISO 字符串或时间戳。
279	
280	### V3. 状态用 QBadge + 状态色，空值显示 `--`
281	
282	不显示 `null` / `undefined` / `NaN`。不裸用文字+颜色替代 badge。
283	
284	状态 badge 组件必须泛型化，不硬编码特定业务状态枚举。通过 `statusMap` 配置映射：
285	
286	```vue
287	<!-- ❌ 硬编码 ConnectionLifecycleStatus -->
288	<StatusBadge :status="conn.lifecycle" />
289	
290	<!-- ✅ 泛型 + 外部映射 -->
291	<StatusBadge :status="conn.lifecycle" :status-map="connectionStatusMap" />
292	<StatusBadge :status="task.state" :status-map="taskStatusMap" />
293	```
294	
295	`statusMap` 类型：`Record<string, { label: string; color: string }>`。各 feature 在自己的 `components/` 或 `types` 中定义对应的映射常量。
296	
297	### V4. 物理量必须标注单位，长文本截断 + QTooltip
298	
299	电压 `12.5 V`，温度 `25.3 ℃`，百分比 `85.3%`（无空格）。超长文本用 CSS 截断，QTooltip 展示全文。
300	
301	---
302	
303	## 8. 性能
304	
305	> 本章规则全部来源于旧系统实际踩坑，是 rewrite 性能底线。
306	
307	### P1. 高频数据必须缓冲后批量刷新
308	
309	串口/网络/SCOE 数据不得逐条触发 Vue 响应式。用 rAF（16ms）或定时器缓冲，每帧批量刷一次。UI 刷新频率与数据源频率解耦。
310	
311	```typescript
312	// ❌ 旧系统：每包数据直接穿透到 store
313	serialPort.on('data', data => store.handleReceivedData(data));
314	
315	// ✅ 缓冲层
316	let pending: Frame[] = [];
317	let rafId: number | null = null;
318	function pushFrame(frame: Frame) {
319	  pending.push(frame);
320	  if (!rafId) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(flush);
321	}
322	function flush() {
323	  displayData.value = [...pending]; pending = [];
324	  rafId = null;
325	}
326	```
327	
328	### P2. 大数组/大对象用 shallowRef，整组替换
329	
330	超 50 元素的数组、嵌套超 2 层的对象用 `shallowRef`。更新时 `arr.value = newArr`，不用 `.push()` / `.splice()`。第三方实例和历史数据用 `markRaw()`。
331	
332	```typescript
333	// ❌
334	const list = reactive<Frame[]>([]); list.push(newFrame);
335	// ✅
336	const list = shallowRef<Frame[]>([]); list.value = [...list.value, newFrame];
337	```
338	
339	### P3. 禁止 deep watch 大对象
340	
341	超 20 属性的对象或超 10 元素的数组禁止 `watch(x, fn, { deep: true })`。改为监听具体 computed、在变更点主动调用。
342	
343	```typescript
344	// ❌
345	watch(() => config, handler, { deep: true });
346	// ✅
347	watch(() => config.groups.length, () => rebuild());
348	// 或在变更点显式调用 rebuild()
349	```
350	
351	### P4. computed 返回对象/数组必须保持引用稳定
352	
353	避免 `.filter()` / `.map()` 直接返回（每次新引用）。Vue 3.4+ 用 computed 的 oldValue 参数比较。
354	
355	推荐模式：表格行数据、筛选结果等派生数组用 `shallowRef` + `watch` 手动更新，不用 `computed` + `.map()`/`.filter()`。
356	
357	```typescript
358	// ❌ 每次 frameList 变化都创建全新数组，触发下游全量 diff
359	const tableRows = computed(() =>
360	  frameList.value.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, _index: i + 1 })),
361	);
362	
363	// ✅ shallowRef + watch，整组替换（与 P2 一致）
364	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
365	watch(frameList, (list) => {
366	  tableRows.value = list.map((f, i) => ({ ...f, _index: i + 1 }));
367	}, { immediate: true });
368	```
369	
370	少量数据（< 50 条）时 computed 也勉强可用，但后续页面数据量可能增大，统一使用 shallowRef 模式减少认知负担。
371	
372	### P5. 定时器用 requestAnimationFrame，不用 setInterval
373	
374	UI 更新和动画驱动用 rAF。长周期轮询（≥1s）可用 setInterval，但必须 onUnmounted 清理。
375	
376	```typescript
377	// ❌ 旧系统：setInterval(fn, 500) 更新表格
378	// ✅ rAF + 节流
379	let last = 0;
380	function loop(now: number) {
381	  if (now - last >= 500) { updateTable(); last = now; }
382	  rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
383	}
384	```
385	
386	### P6. v-for 必须用稳定唯一 key，不用 index
387	
388	```vue
389	<!-- ❌ --> <div v-for="(item, i) in list" :key="i">
390	<!-- ✅ --> <div v-for="item in list" :key="item.id">
391	```
392	
393	### P7. 仪表盘用 v-memo 限制重渲染范围
394	
395	```vue
396	<ConnectionPanel v-memo="[connectionState]" :state="connectionState" />
397	<FrameMetrics v-memo="[frameSnapshot]" :data="frameSnapshot" />
398	```
399	
400	### P8. 模板中禁止内联 .filter() / .map() / .reduce()
401	
402	提取到 computed。
403	
404	---
405	
406	## 9. CSS 动画
407	
408	### A1. transition 禁用 `all`，只列具体属性
409	
410	```css
411	/* ❌ */ transition: all 0.3s;
412	/* ✅ */ transition: transform 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease;
413	```
414	
415	### A2. 动画只用 transform 和 opacity（composite 属性）
416	
417	禁止动画 width/height/top/left/margin 等 layout 属性。状态指示器动画用 CSS @keyframes，不用 JS setInterval 驱动。will-change 只用于 transform/opacity，优先用 `transform: translateZ(0)` 替代。
418	
419	---
420	
421	## 10. Electron UI
422	
423	### E1. IPC 高频数据必须批量化
424	
425	main 进程缓冲串口/网络数据，16ms 批量发送一次。renderer 侧走 P1 缓冲模式。低频操作可直接 IPC。
426	
427	### E2. 提供硬件加速开关
428	
429	应用设置中提供开关，启动时处理 `gpu-process-crashed` 事件。支持 `--disable-gpu` 参数。
430	
431	### E3. 控制单窗口 DOM 节点 ≤ 3000
432	
433	虚拟滚动、懒渲染、v-if（非 v-show）是主要手段。折叠面板折叠时不渲染内容。
434	
435	---
436	
437	## 11. 语义 CSS Class
438	
439	以下语义 class 在 `css/layers/_utilities.scss` 中统一定义，模板中禁止用 inline style 消费对应 token。
440	
441	### 文本色
442	
443	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
444	|-------|------|------|
445	| `rw-text-label` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-muted)` | 字段标签、小标题 |
446	| `rw-text-value` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-primary)` | 字段值、重要数据 |
447	| `rw-text-desc` | `color: var(--rw-color-text-secondary)` | 描述、辅助文字 |
448	| `rw-text-error` | `color: var(--rw-color-status-danger)` | 错误提示 |
449	| `rw-text-warn` | `color: var(--rw-color-status-warning)` | 警告提示 |
450	
451	### 边框
452	
453	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
454	|-------|------|------|
455	| `rw-divider-b` | `border-bottom: var(--rw-border-width-subtle) solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 区域分隔 |
456	| `rw-divider-t` | `border-top: var(--rw-border-width-subtle) solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 区域分隔 |
457	| `rw-divider-l` | `border-left: var(--rw-border-width-subtle) solid var(--rw-color-border-subtle)` | 侧边面板分隔 |
458	
459	### 面板
460	
461	| class | 效果 | 用途 |
462	|-------|------|------|
463	| `rw-panel-base` | `background: var(--rw-color-surface-base)` | 基础面板背景 |
464	
465	不在上表中的 token 消费模式出现 2+ 次时，按同一规则提取并补充到此表。
466	
467	---
468	
469	## 12. 页面 Composable 模式
470	
471	页面中以下三种模式不得手写，必须使用对应 composable。
472	
473	### CM1. 异步操作用 `useAsyncAction()`
474	
475	操作锁（operatingIds）、try-finally 清理、错误通知统一由 composable 管理。
476	
477	```typescript
478	// ❌ 手写 operatingIds Set + try-finally
479	const operatingIds = ref<Set<string>>(new Set());
480	async function handleConnect(s: ConnectionSummary) {
481	  operatingIds.value = new Set(operatingIds.value).add(s.connectionId);
482	  try { /* ... */ }
483	  finally { const next = new Set(operatingIds.value); next.delete(s.connectionId); operatingIds.value = next; }
484	}
485	
486	// ✅ composable
487	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
488	async function handleConnect(s: ConnectionSummary) {
489	  await execute(s.connectionId, async () => {
490	    const result = await service.connect(config);
491	    // composable 负责 finally 清理 + 错误时 $q.notify
492	  });
493	}
494	```
495	
496	composable 返回：
497	- `isOperating(id: string): boolean` — 查询操作锁
498	- `execute<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined>` — 带锁执行，自动清理，失败时 `$q.notify({ type: 'negative' })`
499	
500	### CM2. 页面轮询用 `usePolling()`
501	
502	rAF + 节流 + disposed 检查 + onUnmounted 清理统一由 composable 管理。
503	
504	```typescript
505	// ❌ 手写 rAF + disposed + 节流
506	let rafId = 0; let lastPollTime = 0; const disposed = ref(false);
507	function tick(now: number) { /* ... */ rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }
508	onMounted(() => { rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); });
509	onBeforeUnmount(() => { disposed.value = true; if (rafId) cancelAnimationFrame(rafId); });
510	
511	// ✅ composable
512	const { start } = usePolling(() => refreshSummaries(), 500);
513	onMounted(start);
514	```
515	
516	composable 返回：
517	- `start(): void` — 启动轮询
518	- `stop(): void` — 停止轮询
519	- `restart(): void` — 重启轮询
520	- 内部自动 onUnmounted 清理
521	
522	### CM3. 通知用 `useNotify()`
523	
524	封装 `$q.notify` 的成功/失败/警告模式，统一消息格式。
525	
526	```typescript
527	const notify = useNotify();
528	notify.success('连接已创建');
529	notify.error('连接失败', result.error?.message);
530	notify.info('操作已取消');
531	```
532	
533	---
534	
535	## 13. 页面代码组织
536	
537	### O1. 状态声明按用途分组
538	
539	页面 `<script setup>` 中的 ref/computed/shallowRef 按以下顺序声明，组间空一行：
540	
541	```typescript
542	// ===== Service 引用 =====
543	const service = runtime.features.xxxService;
544	
545	// ===== 业务数据 =====
546	const items = ref<readonly Xxx[]>([]);
547	
548	// ===== 查询/筛选 =====
549	const searchText = ref('');
550	const statusFilter = ref<Status | ''>('');
551	
552	// ===== UI 状态 =====
553	const showDialog = ref(false);
554	const selectedIds = ref<string[]>([]);
555	
556	// ===== 派生数据 =====
557	const tableRows = shallowRef<TableRow[]>([]);
558	
559	// ===== 操作 =====
560	const { execute, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
561	const notify = useNotify();
562	```
563	
564	### O2. runtime tick 错误不可静默吞
565	
566	runtime 层的 `routingTick` 等定期操作，`.catch()` 必须至少 `console.error`，不得写空回调。
567	
568	```typescript
569	// ❌
570	routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch(() => {});
571	
572	// ✅
573	routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
574	```
575	
576	### O3. 2+ 处出现的同一代码模式必须提取
577	
578	标签映射、选项数组、stable key 管理、数组操作样板、默认值工厂等，出现在 2+ 文件/函数中时，提取到 composable、shared 常量或辅助函数。不允许复制粘贴后微调。
579	
580	### O4. 不变数据定义在模块级
581	
582	选项数组、标签映射、枚举→标签 Record 等不随组件实例变化的数据，定义在 `<script setup>` 外（模块级常量）。setup 每次挂载都会执行，不应在其中重建不变数据。
583	
```

1	---
2	doc_type: quality
3	type: rewrite-frontend-checklist
4	status: draft
5	date: 2026-05-11
6	summary: 前端 UI 自检 checklist，review/验收时逐项勾选。与 rewrite-frontend-conventions.md 对应。
7	tags:
8	  - rewrite
9	  - frontend
10	  - checklist
11	  - review
12	---
13	
14	# Rewrite 前端 UI 自检 Checklist
15	
16	对应规范：`codestable/quality/rewrite-frontend-conventions.md`。
17	
18	review 或自检时按分组逐项检查。勾选 `- [x]` 表示通过。
19	
20	---
21	
22	## A. 样式检查
23	
24	- [ ] 无硬编码 hex/rgb/hsl 色值（style、class、SCSS 中搜索 `#`、`rgb(`、`hsl(`）
25	- [ ] 无硬编码 px/rem/em 间距（padding、margin、gap 应走 token 或 Quasar class）
26	- [ ] 无硬编码 z-index（走 `rw-z-*` token）
27	- [ ] transition 无 `all`，只列具体属性
28	- [ ] CSS 动画只用 `transform` / `opacity`，无 layout 属性动画
29	- [ ] `will-change` 只用于 `transform` / `opacity`，无 `will-change: width/height`
30	- [ ] 弹窗宽度用语义 class（`rw-dialog-*`），无硬编码 `width: Npx`
31	- [ ] 无 inline style 直接消费 token（文本色、边框、背景 → 语义 class `rw-text-*`/`rw-divider-*`/`rw-panel-*`）
32	
33	## B. Quasar 组件检查
34	
35	- [ ] 无第三方 UI 组件库引入（ag-Grid、Lucide、vue-sonner 等）
36	- [ ] 颜色用 Quasar brand prop（`color="primary"`），无 CSS 覆盖组件视觉
37	- [ ] 禁用/dense/flat 等通过 prop 设置，无 CSS 模拟
38	- [ ] 删除操作有二次确认（`$q.dialog()` 或项目封装组件）
39	- [ ] 操作反馈用 `$q.notify()`，不用第三方 toast
40	- [ ] 图标用 Material Icons（`o_` 前缀），无第三方图标库
41	
42	## C. 表格检查
43	
44	- [ ] 数据表格用 QTable
45	- [ ] 行数可能 > 100 时启用了 `virtual-scroll` + `virtual-scroll-item-size` + 容器高度
46	- [ ] `row-key` 使用稳定唯一标识符（非 index）
47	- [ ] 列数 ≥ 3 时列定义抽到 `columns.ts`
48	- [ ] 空状态有 `#no-data` slot
49	- [ ] loading 用 QTable `:loading` prop
50	- [ ] 大数据集排序/分页走 service 层，非纯前端
51	
52	## D. 表单检查
53	
54	- [ ] 表单用 QForm + Quasar 表单组件，无原生 HTML 表单元素混用
55	- [ ] 校验用 QInput `:rules`（简单）或 feature service（复杂），无第三方校验库
56	- [ ] 校验错误走 `:error` + `:error-message`，不用 toast/q-tooltip 显示校验错误
57	- [ ] 禁用态用 `disable` / `readonly` prop，不用 CSS opacity/pointer-events
58	- [ ] 提交按钮有 `:loading` 或 `:disable` 防重复
59	- [ ] 超过 20 字段的表单已拆分为子组件
60	
61	## E. 弹窗检查
62	
63	- [ ] QDialog 使用 `v-model` 双向绑定（非单向 `:model-value`）
64	- [ ] 弹窗内有 `@hide` 清理定时器/订阅/表单
65	- [ ] 不需保持状态的弹窗用 `v-if`（非 `v-show`）
66	
67	## F. 性能检查
68	
69	- [ ] 高频数据路径（串口/网络/SCOE）有缓冲层，非逐条触发 Vue 响应式
70	- [ ] 大数组（>50元素）用 `shallowRef`，更新走整组替换
71	- [ ] 无 `watch(x, fn, { deep: true })` 监听大对象
72	- [ ] computed 返回对象/数组时引用稳定（无每次返回新引用的 .filter/.map）
73	- [ ] UI 定时器用 `requestAnimationFrame`（非 `setInterval`），且 onUnmounted 清理
74	- [ ] v-for 的 `:key` 是稳定唯一 ID（非 index）
75	- [ ] 模板中无内联 `.filter()` / `.map()` / `.reduce()`
76	- [ ] 仪表盘/总览页多区域布局用了 `v-memo` 限制重渲染
77	
78	## G. 数据展示检查
79	
80	- [ ] 数字有格式化（千分位、有效精度），非原始值直出
81	- [ ] 时间格式统一（`YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss`），非 ISO 字符串或时间戳
82	- [ ] 状态用 QBadge + 状态色映射（positive/warning/negative/info），非裸文字+颜色
83	- [ ] 空值显示 `--`，非 `null` / `undefined` / `NaN`
84	- [ ] 物理量有单位标注（`12.5 V`、`25.3 ℃`）
85	- [ ] 长文本有截断 + QTooltip
86	
87	## H. Electron 检查
88	
89	- [ ] 高频 IPC 已批量化（main 端缓冲，16ms 批量发送）
90	- [ ] 单窗口 DOM 节点数 ≤ 3000
91	- [ ] 折叠面板/隐藏区域用 `v-if`（非 `v-show`）
92	
93	## I. Composable 检查
94	
95	- [ ] 异步操作用 `useAsyncAction()`，无手写 `operatingIds` Set + try-finally 清理
96	- [ ] 页面轮询用 `usePolling()`，无手写 rAF + disposed + 节流逻辑
97	- [ ] 通知用 `useNotify()`，无直接 `$q.notify({ type: 'positive/negative', ... })` 散落
98	- [ ] StatusBadge 使用泛型 `statusMap` 配置，无硬编码业务状态枚举
99	- [ ] 页面状态按 O1 分组声明（Service → 业务数据 → 查询/筛选 → UI → 派生 → 操作）
100	- [ ] runtime 定期操作的 `.catch()` 非空（至少 `console.error`）
101	- [ ] 2+ 文件中的同一代码模式（标签映射、stable key、操作样板、默认值工厂）已提取（O3）
102	- [ ] 选项数组和标签映射在模块级定义，非 setup 内（O4）
103	
```

1	# 任务域 + 发送 + 指令接入页面 Design
2	
3	## 元数据
4	
5	- **类型**: design（结论和变化）
6	- **直接合同**: pages-ta[REDACTED_SK].md, rewrite-ui-architecture-design.md, rewrite-frontend-conventions.md
7	- **边界护栏**: CLAUDE.md, rewrite-target-structure.md, command-ingress-brainstorm.md, pages-frame-connection-brainstorm.md, 2026-05-07-runtime-next-phase-global-planning.md
8	- **日期**: 2026-05-12
9	- **brainstorm**: pages-ta[REDACTED_SK].md
10	- **状态**: REVISE v3（设计-代码对齐审计修正 + Service Readiness Audit）
11	
12	---
13	
14	## 页面 1: 帧发送页 (/frames/send)
15	
16	### 现状
17	
18	- Send feature 有 SendService.execute + SendReader + selector，无帧实例管理能力
19	- buildFrame / resolveFieldValues / evaluateFieldExpressions 等纯函数存在于 send/core 但未 re-export
20	- 无 UI 侧帧实例类型，无预览 composable
21	- 旧系统发送页包含定时/触发/顺序发送和任务监控，全部与发送页耦合
22	
23	### 变化
24	
25	**D-S1 布局**: 模式B 三栏（左 240px + 中 flex:1 + 右 300px）
26	
27	**D-S2 左栏帧格式列表**:
28	- QList + QItem，分组标题（收藏/全部）
29	- QInput dense 搜索，实时过滤（纯内存 < 1ms，无 debounce）
30	- 数据源: `listFrameAssetSummaries(source, { direction: 'send', query, favoriteOnly? })`
31	- 单击 → 创建实例 + 中栏选中 + 右栏预览
32	- 双击 → 创建实例 + 打开编辑对话框
33	- 收藏按钮: toggle isFavorite → replaceFrames
34	
35	**D-S3 中栏帧实例表**:
36	- DataTable Widget + virtual-scroll + selection="single"
37	- 列定义抽到 `send/components/instance-columns.ts`:
38	
39	| 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
40	|----|------|------|------|
41	| # | index | fixed 60px | 自动序号 |
42	| 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
43	| 参数数 | fieldCount | fixed 80px | 数字 |
44	| 发送次数 | sendCount | fixed 80px | 数字(V1) |
45	| 上次发送 | lastSendAt | fixed 140px | 时间格式化(V2) |
46	| 备注 | description | auto | 文本，长文本截断+QTooltip(V4) |
47	| 操作 | — | fixed 120px | icon 按钮(编辑/复制/删除) |
48	
49	- 空状态: `#no-data` → "暂无帧实例，从左侧选择帧格式创建"
50	- loading: `:loading="isLoading"`
51	- 排序: 上下移动按钮（本期不做拖拽）
52	- 批量模式: selection="multiple" + 批量删除($q.dialog 确认)
53	- 数据源: useSendInstances composable
54	
55	**D-S4 右栏预览 + 发送**:
56	- 上部: 实例基本信息（名称/时间/备注）
57	- 中部: 十六进制帧（pre code font-mono，按字节空格分隔）
58	- 下部: 可配置字段表（字段名 | 十六进制值）
59	- 底部: SendTargetSelector + [发送] 按钮 `:loading="isSending" :disable="!selectedInstance || !selectedTarget"`(F4)
60	- 数据源: useFramePreview composable
61	
62	**D-S5 SendFrameInstance 类型**（归口 send/core/types.ts）:
63	
64	```typescript
65	interface SendFrameInstance {
66	  readonly instanceId: string;        // nanoid
67	  readonly frameId: string;
68	  readonly name: string;
69	  readonly description?: string;
70	  readonly userFieldValues: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
71	  readonly sendCount: number;
72	  readonly lastSendAt?: string;       // ISO timestamp
73	  readonly createdAt: string;
74	  readonly updatedAt: string;
75	}
76	```
77	
78	不进 core/service 层——帧实例是页面级 UI 状态，不是全局领域状态。
79	
80	**D-S6 单帧发送**: 选中实例 + 选择目标 → `sendService.execute({ frameId, targetId, userFieldValues, context: { source: 'user' } })`。成功 $q.notify('positive')，失败 $q.notify('negative') + buildIssues。
81	
82	**C9 说明**: `SendRequest.variables` 字段在实际代码中不存在。外部变量通过 service 创建时注入的 `variableProvider` 获取，`resolveFieldValues` 内部合并 provider 变量和调用方传入的 variables 参数。UI 发送时不需传递 variables——service 内部自动从 variableProvider 获取。`SendRequest` 同时接受 `fieldValues` 和 `userFieldValues`，service 内部 `userFieldValues ?? fieldValues`。UI 应统一使用 `userFieldValues`。
83	
84	**D-S7 实例编辑对话框**:
85	- QDialog v-model + @hide 清理(D1)
86	- 宽度: `rw-dialog-lg`(D2)
87	- 使用 FieldEditWidget 组件（见跨页面共享）
88	- [取消] [确认] 按钮，确认按钮 `:loading="isSaving" :disable="!isValid"`(F4)
89	
90	**D-S8 发送页定位**: 只做单帧快速发送 + 帧实例管理。定时/触发/顺序/监控全部在任务页。
91	
92	**D-S9 已移除**: 定时/触发/顺序发送对话框、任务监控对话框、拖拽排序（简化为上下移动按钮）、批量发送 API。
93	
94	---
95	
96	## 页面 2: 任务管理页 (/tasks)
97	
98	### 现状
99	
100	- Task feature 有完整的 TaskService + Reader + selector
101	- 无任务编辑/创建 UI 能力
102	- 无任务历史查看能力
103	- 无 validateTaskDefinition / serialization / builders
104	- 旧系统任务作为发送页对话框，与全局规划中 task = 通用执行引擎的定位冲突
105	
106	### 变化
107	
108	**D-T1 定位**: 任务是通用执行引擎的完整管理界面，独立于发送页。
109	
110	**D-T2 布局**: 主从双栏 — 左侧任务列表(flex:1) + 右侧面板(360px)
111	
112	右侧面板只承载**执行监控和任务摘要**（360px 足够）。
113	任务编辑器因表单复杂度（4 种步骤 + 3 种调度 + 高级配置）使用**全宽对话框**（rw-dialog-xl）。
114	
115	```
116	┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
117	│ 任务管理  [新建任务] [导入] [导出]                              │
118	├────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┤
119	│ TableToolbar(搜索+筛选) │ 未选中: 空状态                       │
120	│                        │                                     │
121	│ Tab: 活动任务|历史记录   │ 定义态: 任务摘要 + [编辑][启动]       │
122	│ DataTable              │ 运行态: 执行监控(TaskExecutionDetail)│
123	│                        │                                     │
124	│ QChip 状态筛选          │                                     │
125	└────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────┘
126	
127	[新建任务]/[编辑] → QDialog rw-dialog-xl → 任务编辑器对话框
128	```
129	
130	**D-T3 任务列表区**:
131	- Tab 切换: 活动任务 | 历史记录（keep-alive）
132	- DataTable + virtual-scroll（防御性开启）
133	- 活动任务列定义抽到 `task/components/task-columns.ts`:
134	
135	| 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
136	|----|------|------|------|
137	| 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
138	| 调度类型 | schedule.kind | fixed 100px | QChip(immediate/timer/event) |
139	| 状态 | status | fixed 100px | StatusBadge |
140	| 进度 | progress | fixed 120px | QLinearProgress + 百分比 |
141	| 操作 | — | fixed 140px | icon 按钮(暂停/恢复/停止) |
142	
143	- 历史记录列定义:
144	
145	| 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
146	|----|------|------|------|
147	| 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
148	| 调度类型 | schedule.kind | fixed 100px | QChip |
149	| 结果 | result | fixed 80px | StatusBadge(positive 已完成/negative 失败) |
150	| 耗时 | elapsedMs | fixed 100px | 时间格式化(V2) |
151	| 完成时间 | completedAt | fixed 140px | 时间格式化(V2) |
152	| 操作 | — | fixed 120px | icon 按钮(查看/重新执行/删除) |
153	
154	- 空状态: `#no-data` → 活动 Tab "暂无活动任务"，历史 Tab "暂无历史记录"
155	- loading: `:loading="isLoading"`
156	- 状态筛选 QChip 组: 全部/待启动/运行中/已暂停/已完成/失败
157	- 数据源: selectActiveInstances + selectTaskHistory
158	- **历史记录数据流**: task state 已有 `instances`（活跃）+ `history`（removeTask 归档）两层数据。task feature 新增 `selectActiveInstances` selector，只返回非终止态实例（created/running/paused）。`selectTaskHistory` 扩展为返回 history + 仍在 instances 中的终止态实例（completed/failed/stopped）。UI composable 直接调两个 selector，不做任何过滤。
159	- 操作: 活动 Tab 中终止态任务显示 [移至历史] 按钮 → 调 removeTask → 从 active 消失、入 history
160	- 合并 useTaskList + useTaskHistory → 单一 useTaskList composable，内部按 mode('active' | 'history') 切换 selector
161	
162	**D-T4 任务摘要（右侧面板，定义态）**:
163	- 任务名称 + 调度类型摘要 + 步骤数量
164	- 可变参数摘要（有/无）
165	- [编辑] 按钮 → 打开任务编辑器对话框(rw-dialog-xl)
166	- [启动] 按钮 `:loading="isStarting" :disable="lifecycle !== 'created'"`(F4)
167	
168	**D-T5 任务编辑器对话框**:
169	- QDialog v-model + @hide 清理(D1)
170	- 宽度: `rw-dialog-xl`(D2)
171	- 基本信息: 任务名称(QInput `:rules="[val => !!val || '请输入任务名称']"`(F1/F2)) + 调度类型(QSelect: immediate/timer/event)
172	- 调度配置: 按 schedule.kind 动态渲染
173	  - immediate: 无额外配置项
174	  - timer: 间隔(QInput type=number `:rules="[val => val > 0 || '间隔必须大于0']"`) + 次数归口 TaskStopCondition.maxIterations + 无限循环(QToggle)
175	  - event: 条件编辑器（ConditionTerm 列表 + 添加按钮，至少一条校验）
176	- 步骤编辑器: 步骤列表 + [添加步骤] 下拉(send/wait-condition/delay)
177	  - 每步: QExpansionItem 展开/折叠 + 类型图标 + 摘要 + 删除按钮
178	  - SendStep: FrameSelector(required) + SendTargetSelector(required) + FieldEditWidget(独立 userFieldValues) + 发送后延时(QInput type=number) + 可选 repeat 配置
179	  - WaitConditionStep: 条件列表(至少一条) + 超时(QInput type=number `:rules="[val => val > 0 || '请输入超时时间']"`) + 超时策略(QSelect: continue/skip/fail)
180	  - DelayStep: 持续时间(QInput type=number `:rules="[val => val > 0 || '请输入延时时间']"`)
181	  - 字段数 > 20 时 SendStep 的 FieldEditWidget 必须拆子组件(F3)
182	- 高级配置（QExpansionItem 折叠）:
183	  - 停止条件: maxIterations / maxDurationMs
184	  - 错误策略: onFailure(QSelect) / retryCount / retryDelayMs
185	  - ~~可变参数: FieldVariation 编辑器（字段选择 + 值列表）~~ → Phase 2（类型已就绪，UI 编辑器待实现）
186	  - ~~exitCondition~~ → Phase 2（类型已就绪，UI 编辑器待实现）
187	  - ~~step repeat 配置~~ → Phase 2（类型已就绪，UI 编辑器待实现）
188	- 操作: [取消] [保存] [保存并启动]
189	  - [保存] `:loading="isSaving" :disable="!isValid"`(F4)
190	  - [保存并启动] `:loading="isSaving"`
191	- 保存前 validateTaskDefinition 校验
192	
193	**D-T6 执行监控（右侧面板，运行态）**:
194	- 复用 TaskExecutionDetail 基础组件（widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue）
195	- 任务状态: StatusBadge + 名称
196	- 进度: QLinearProgress + 文字(stepsCompleted/stepsTotal)
197	- 耗时: elapsedMs 格式化(V2)
198	- 步骤状态列表: 每步 StatusBadge + 结果摘要
199	- 操作: 暂停/恢复/停止（停止需 $q.dialog() 确认）
200	- 错误: TaskInstanceState.error 内联展示
201	
202	**D-T7 任务状态映射**:
203	
204	| TaskLifecycleStatus | UI 显示 | StatusBadge color(C2) |
205	|---------------------|---------|----------------------|
206	| created | 待启动 | grey |
207	| running | 运行中 | positive |
208	| running + schedule.kind='event' | 等待触发 | positive (副标签) |
209	| running + schedule.kind='timer' | 等待调度 | positive (副标签) |
210	| paused | 已暂停 | warning |
211	| completed | 已完成 | positive |
212	| failed | 失败 | negative |
213	
214	waiting-trigger/waiting-schedule 由 UI 层从 `instance.definitionRef.schedule.kind` + lifecycle 推导，不改 core。
215	
216	**D-T8 SendStep 字段值**: 方案 A（copy-on-create）。每步独立持有 userFieldValues，不引用发送页实例。提供"复制上一步"按钮降低重复配置成本。
217	
218	**D-T9 导入导出**: serializeTaskDefinition → JSON 文件写入(platform facade)；读取 → deserializeTaskDefinition → 校验 → 创建。
219	
220	**D-T10 TaskService 方法签名**:
221	
222	```typescript
223	// 已实现
224	createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState
225	startTask(instanceId: string): void
226	pauseTask(instanceId: string): void
227	resumeTask(instanceId: string): void
228	stopTask(instanceId: string): void
229	removeTask(instanceId: string): void
230	retryTask(sourceInstanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined
231	stopAll(): number
232	getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined
233	getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined
234	// 已实现（工具函数）
235	validateTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): TaskValidationIssue[]
236	serializeTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): string
237	deserializeTaskDefinition(json: string): TaskDefinition
238	```
239	
240	**D-T11 Service Readiness Gaps**（UI 实施前需补齐）:
241	
242	| Gap | 影响 | 修复方式 |
243	|-----|------|---------|
244	| 无 `updateTask` 方法 | 编辑任务无法保存 | 新增 `updateTask(instanceId, definition)`：仅 `created` 态可改定义 |
245	| 历史记录缺 `elapsedMs` / `completedAt` | D-T3 历史列无法渲染 | selector 层从 `startedAt`/`stoppedAt`/`completedAt`/`failedAt` 派生 |
246	| `stopAll()` 无返回值 | UI 无法显示停止了多少 | 改为返回 `stoppedCount: number` |
247	
248	D-T5 编辑器保存逻辑：`created` 态 → `updateTask` → 刷新面板；`running/paused` 态 → 禁用编辑。

410	## 跨页面共享
411	
412	### JSON 持久化模式（shared/ 纯 TS + feature composable Vue 封装）
413	
414	shared/ 要求零 Vue 依赖(CLAUDE.md)，所以拆为两层:
415	
416	**shared/persistence/json-persistence.ts**（纯 TS，无 Vue 依赖）:
417	
418	```typescript
419	interface JsonPersistenceOptions {
420	  readonly filename: string;
421	  readonly debounceMs?: number;      // 默认 500ms
422	}
423	
424	function createJsonPersistence<T>(
425	  readFile: (path: string) => Promise<string>,
426	  writeFile: (path: string, content: string) => Promise<void>,
427	  options: JsonPersistenceOptions
428	): {
429	  load(): Promise<T | undefined>;
430	  save(data: T): Promise<void>;
431	}
432	```
433	
434	**各 feature composable 内部**各自封装 Vue 响应式:
435	
436	```typescript
437	// 例: send/composables/use-send-instances.ts
438	const persistence = createJsonPersistence(
439	  platform.readFile, platform.writeFile,
440	  { filename: 'send-instances.json' }
441	);
442	const data = shallowRef<SendFrameInstance[]>([]);
443	const isLoaded = ref(false);
444	async function load() { data.value = await persistence.load() ?? []; }
445	async function save() { await persistence.save(toRaw(data.value)); }
446	```
447	
448	消费方:
449	- useSendInstances → 帧实例 JSON 持久化
450	- useScoeConfig → SCOE 配置 JSON 持久化（satelliteConfigs 管理在此 composable 内，不在 CommandIngressState）
451	
452	不建 config-storage-service wrapper，composable 直接调 platform facade + shared 纯 TS 工具。
453	
454	### TaskExecutionDetail 基础组件
455	
456	归口: `widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue`
457	
458	Props:
459	```typescript
460	{
461	  readonly instance: TaskInstanceState;
462	  readonly progress: TaskProgress;
463	}
464	```
465	
466	展示: StatusBadge + QLinearProgress + 步骤状态列表 + elapsedMs
467	
468	Slots:
469	- `#actions`: 操作按钮（暂停/恢复/停止由消费方传入）
470	- `#extension`: 甲方扩展区域（上报状态、JSON 报告交付进度）
471	
472	消费方:
473	- 任务页右侧面板（运行态）—— 无 extension slot
474	- 中心对接 Tab 任务详情弹窗—— + 甲方 extension slot
475	
476	不 props 膨胀基础组件——甲方专用信息通过 extension slot 注入。
477	
478	### FieldEditWidget 字段编辑组件
479	
480	归口: `widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue`
481	
482	Props:
483	```typescript
484	{
485	  readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
486	  readonly values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
487	  readonly direction: 'send' | 'receive';
488	}
489	```
490	
491	Events:
492	- `update:values` — 字段值变化时 emit
493	
494	字段分组渲染（使用 Quasar brand 色，不硬编码(C1)）:
495	- 可调字段: `color="info"` 标识，用户可编辑
496	- 计算参数: `color="warning"` 标识，表达式字段
497	- 不可配置: `color="grey"` 文字，只读展示
498	
499	表达式实时联动: 字段值变化 → `evaluateFieldPreviewForUI(frame, fieldId, userFieldValues, variableProvider, variables?)` 重算依赖字段 → emit 更新后的 values。这是 send feature 面向 UI 的公共函数，内部封装 frameToBuildInput → 查找字段 → evaluateFieldPreview 的转换链路。UI composable 只传 frame + fieldId + userFieldValues + variableProvider，不接触 send 内部编码类型。按字段粒度调用，不全量 resolveFieldValues。computed 缓存 + 字段级依赖追踪。
500	
501	**variableProvider 获取**：send service 创建时注入 variableProvider（`CreateSendServiceOptions.variableProvider`），但 `SendReader` 接口不暴露它。UI composable 需要 runtime 提供独立的 variableProvider 实例（或 noop provider 用于纯本地预览场景）。详见发送页 Service Readiness Audit GS2。
502	
503	消费方:
504	- 发送页实例编辑对话框
505	- 任务页编辑器对话框 SendStep 字段值编辑
506	
507	### Tab keep-alive
508	
509	指令接入页 3 个 Tab 内容用 `<keep-alive>` 包裹，防止切 Tab 丢失测试工具录制状态、编辑表单、监控数据。
510	任务页活动任务/历史记录 Tab 同理 keep-alive。
511	
512	---
513	
514	## Composable 合并审视
515	
516	brainstorm 提出 11 个 composable，审视后合并为 10 个:
517	
518	| 原始 | 合并结果 | 理由 |
519	|------|---------|------|
520	| useTaskList + useTaskHistory | → useTaskList（mode: 'active' \| 'history'） | 直接消费 selectActiveInstances / selectTaskHistory 两个 selector，不做 UI 层过滤 |
521	| useSendInstances | 保留 | 独立的帧实例 CRUD + 持久化 |
522	| useFramePreview | 保留 | 预览计算逻辑独立，依赖 evaluateFieldPreviewForUI |
523	| useTaskEditor | 保留 | 编辑表单状态复杂，职责独立 |
524	| useTaskMonitor | 保留 | 运行态轮询 + 状态映射，与编辑器互斥 |
525	| useScoeConfig | 保留 | 配置 CRUD + satelliteConfigs 管理 + 持久化 |
526	| useScoeMonitor | 保留 | 统计卡片 + 命令日志，rAF 1000ms |
527	| useTestTool | 保留 | HEX 收发 + DataRecorder + 高亮，与监控数据独立 |
528	| useCentralDocking | 保留 | 甲方连接状态 + 任务列表 |
529	| useTaskReport | 保留 | 任务选择 + 上报流程 |
530	
531	useScoeMonitor + useTestTool 不合并: 数据完全独立（统计 vs HEX 收发），共享 rAF 刷新循环的收益不足以抵消职责混合的复杂度。
532

556	- main: 实现文件读写 + 系统对话框

763	## 发送页 Service Readiness Audit
764	
765	**日期**: 2026-05-13
766	**基线**: `rewrite/src/features/send/` 当前代码 + D-S1~D-S9 设计条目
767	**结论**: 核心发送 API 就绪，2 个 gap 需在 UI 实施前补齐
768	
769	### 就绪项（直接可用）
770	
771	| 操作 | 方法/函数 | 状态 |
772	|------|---------|------|
773	| 发送帧 | `sendService.execute(SendRequest)` → `Promise<SendResult>` | OK |
774	| 帧构建 | `frameToBuildInput(frame)` → `{ fields, totalByteLength }` | OK |
775	| 字段值解析 | `resolveFieldValues(fields, userFieldValues, variableProvider, variables?)` | OK |
776	| 因子逆换算 | `applyFactor(fields, values)` | OK |
777	| 字节编码 | `buildFrame(SendBuildInput)` → `FrameBuildOutput` | OK |

## Verification Report: G1-G3 Fix Status for Task Feature

### **G1: `updateTask` method in task-service.ts**
✅ **FOUND** - Line 262-266
- **Interface signature** (Line 40): `updateTask(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState | undefined`
- **Implementation signature** (Line 262): `updateTask(instanceId, definition)`
- **Return type**: `TaskInstanceState | undefined`
- **Implementation**: Returns `undefined` if instance doesn't exist or lifecycle is not `'created'`, otherwise calls `state.updateInstance(instanceId, { definitionRef: definition })`

### **G2: `elapsedMs`/`completedAt` in task selectors**
✅ **DERIVED** - Not directly in history, but calculated via `calculateProgress()`
- **Location**: `rewrite/src/features/task/selectors/task-selectors.ts`
- **History selector** (Lines 33-40): `selectTaskHistory()` returns `ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[]`
- **Progress calculation** (Line 28): Calls `calculateProgress(instance)` from core
- **Implementation**: `calculateProgress()` in `rewrite/src/features/task/core/progress.ts` (Lines 80-86):
  ```typescript
  function calculateElapsedMs(instance: TaskInstanceState): number {
    if (!instance.startedAt) return 0;
    const start = new Date(instance.startedAt).getTime();
    const endRef = instance.completedAt ?? instance.stoppedAt ?? instance.failedAt;
    const end = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
    return Math.max(0, end - start);
  }
  ```
- **Status fields** (`TaskInstanceState` in `types.ts` Lines 163-176): Include `startedAt`, `completedAt`, `stoppedAt`, `failedAt` as optional ISO string fields

### **G3: `stopAll()` return type**
✅ **RETURNS NUMBER** - Line 226-239
- **Interface signature** (Line 37): `stopAll(): number`
- **Implementation**: Returns `stoppedCount` (number of tasks stopped)
- **Logic**: Iterates through all instances, stops those with `'running'` or `'paused'` lifecycle, returns count

## Comprehensive Summary of Task Feature Module

### 1. **rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts** (Complete Core Types)

**Exported Constants:**
- `TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay']`
- `COMPARISON_OPERATORS = ['eq', 'neq', 'gt', 'lt', 'gte', 'lte', 'contains', 'change', 'any']`
- `TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause']`
- `TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = ['created', 'running', 'paused', 'stopped', 'completed', 'failed']`

**Key Discriminated Unions:**

**ScheduleDriver** (replaces TaskSchedulingMode):
```typescript
type ScheduleDriver =
  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number }
```

**TaskStepDefinition** (unified config field):
```typescript
type TaskStepDefinition =
  | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
  | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig }
```

**TaskStepResultPayload**:
```typescript
type TaskStepResultPayload = SendStepResult | WaitConditionStepResult | DelayStepResult
```

**Key Interfaces:**

**ConditionTerm** (replaces WaitCondition):
```typescript
interface ConditionTerm {
  readonly frameId: string
  readonly fieldId: string
  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator
  readonly threshold: string | number
  readonly sourceId?: string
  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or' // default 'and'
}
```

**FieldVariation**:
```typescript
interface FieldVariation {
  readonly fieldId: string
  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[]
}
```

**StepRepeat**:
```typescript
interface StepRepeat {
  readonly intervalMs: number
  readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[]
  readonly maxCount?: number
}
```

**SendStepConfig**:
```typescript
interface SendStepConfig {
  readonly frameId: string
  readonly targetId: string // now required
  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>
  readonly variables?: VariableMap
  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number
  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat
}
```

**WaitConditionConfig**:
```typescript
interface WaitConditionConfig {
  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]
  readonly timeoutMs?: number
  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail'
}
```

**DelayStepConfig**:
```typescript
interface DelayStepConfig {
  readonly durationMs: number
}
```

**TaskStopCondition**:
```typescript
interface TaskStopCondition {
  readonly maxDurationMs?: number
  readonly maxIterations?: number
  readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[] // new early exit
}
```

**TaskDefinition**:
```typescript
interface TaskDefinition {
  readonly id: string
  readonly name: string
  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[]
  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver
  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition
  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[]
  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy
}
```

**TaskErrorPolicy**:
```typescript
interface TaskErrorPolicy {
  readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction
  readonly retryCount?: number
  readonly retryDelayMs?: number
}
```

**TaskInstanceState**:
```typescript
interface TaskInstanceState {
  readonly instanceId: string
  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition
  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus
  readonly startedAt?: string
  readonly pausedAt?: string
  readonly stoppedAt?: string
  readonly completedAt?: string
  readonly failedAt?: string
  readonly currentStepIndex: number
  readonly currentIteration: number
  readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[]
  readonly error?: string
}
```

**TaskProgress**:
```typescript
interface TaskProgress {
  readonly stepsTotal: number
  readonly stepsCompleted: number
  readonly stepsFailed: number
  readonly stepsSkipped: number
  readonly iterationsCompleted: number
  readonly iterationsTotal: number | null
  readonly elapsedMs: number
  readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null
  readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null
}
```

**Key Function:**
```typescript
function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition
// Returns maxIterations based on fieldVariations or defaults to 1 for immediate
```

---

### 2. **rewrite/src/features/task/services/task-service.ts** (Full TaskService Interface)

**Exported Interfaces:**

**TaskReader**:
```typescript
interface TaskReader {
  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot
  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined
  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined
  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot
}
```

**TaskService** (extends TaskReader):
```typescript
interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState
  startTask(instanceId: string): void
  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void
  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void
  stopTask(instanceId: string): void
  stopAll(): number
  removeTask(instanceId: string): void
  retryTask(sourceInstanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined
  updateTask(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState | undefined
  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>
}
```

**CreateTaskServiceOptions**:
```typescript
interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider
  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource
  readonly timerService?: unknown // placeholder
  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>
  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer
  readonly now?: () => string
}
```

**Factory Function:**
```typescript
function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService
```

**Key Implementation Details:**
- Manages subscription lifecycle with `ensureSubscription()` and `releaseSubscription()`
- Uses `ConditionRegistry` for condition matching
- Coordinates sub-modules: `lifecycle`, `stepExecutors`, `errorPolicy`, `loops`
- Execution loop via `runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal)`
- Fire-and-forget execution pattern for `startTask()`

### 4. **rewrite/src/features/task/core/task-validation.ts** (Validation)

**Exported Interface:**
```typescript
interface TaskValidationIssue {
  readonly severity: 'error' | 'warning'
  readonly code: string
  readonly message: string
  readonly stepId?: string
}
```

**Main Function:**
```typescript
function validateTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): TaskValidationIssue[]
```

**Validates:**
- Task has id and name
- At least one step exists
- Step IDs are unique
- Send steps have frameId and targetId
- Wait-condition steps have at least one condition
- Delay steps have positive durationMs
- Stop conditions have positive maxIterations/maxDurationMs

**Validation Codes:**
- `task.definition.missingId`, `task.definition.missingName`, `task.definition.emptySteps`
- `task.step.missingId`, `task.step.duplicateId`
- `task.step.send.missingFrameId`, `task.step.send.missingTargetId`
- `task.step.wait.emptyConditions`, `task.step.wait.invalidTimeout`
- `task.step.invalidDelay`
- `task.stop.invalidMaxIterations`, `task.stop.invalidMaxDuration`

### Key Type Guards and Discriminated Union Patterns

1. **ScheduleDriver**: Discriminated by `kind` field ('immediate' | 'timer' | 'event')
2. **TaskStepDefinition**: Discriminated by `kind` field ('send' | 'wait-condition' | 'delay')
3. **TaskStepResultPayload**: Discriminated by `kind` field in each result type
4. **isTerminal()**: Used to check if lifecycle status is terminal (not shown but referenced)
5. **TERMINATED_LIFECYCLES**: Set for filtering history selectors

This architecture provides a comprehensive task execution framework with strong typing, validation, serialization, and state management capabilities.

## Complete File Summaries

Here are the exact signatures and implementation details for all requested files:

### 1. **use-async-action.ts** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/shared/composables/use-async-action.ts`

**API Surface:**
```typescript
function useAsyncAction(): {
  operatingIds: ShallowRef<Set<string>>;
  execute<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined>;
  isOperating(id: string): boolean;
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Uses `shallowRef<Set<string>>` to track currently operating IDs
- `execute` prevents concurrent operations with same ID, returns `undefined` if already operating
- Automatically shows Quasar negative notifications on errors
- Uses immutable Set pattern for reactivity

---

### 2. **use-polling.ts** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/shared/composables/use-polling.ts`

**API Surface:**
```typescript
function usePolling(fn: () => void, intervalMs: number): {
  start(): void;
  stop(): void;
  restart(): void;
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Uses `requestAnimationFrame` instead of `setInterval` for better performance
- Tracks disposed state to prevent memory leaks
- Auto-cleanup on unmount
- Time-based throttling to respect `intervalMs`

---

### 3. **use-notify.ts** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/shared/composables/use-notify.ts`

**API Surface:**
```typescript
function useNotify(): {
  success(message: string): void;
  error(message: string, detail?: string): void;
  info(message: string): void;
  warning(message: string): void;
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Thin wrapper around Quasar's `$q.notify`
- Maps to Quasar notification types: `positive`, `negative`, `info`, `warning`
- `error` combines message and detail with colon separator

---

### 4. **use-stable-keys.ts** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/shared/composables/use-stable-keys.ts`

**API Surface:**
```typescript
function useStableKeys(prefix = 'key'): {
  keys: Ref<string[]>;
  syncKeys(items: readonly unknown[]): void;
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Generates stable keys using incrementing UID with prefix pattern: `${prefix}_${uid}`
- Grows keys array when needed, shrinks when items decrease
- Preserves existing keys to avoid Vue re-rendering

---

### 5. **DataTable.vue** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/widgets/DataTable.vue`

**Props:**
```typescript
interface DataTableProps<T extends Record<string, unknown>> {
  columns: QTableColumn[];
  rows: readonly T[];
  rowKey: string;
  loading?: boolean;
  selection?: 'single' | 'multiple' | 'none';
  selected?: readonly T[];
  virtualScrollItemSize?: number;
  containerHeight?: string;
}
```

**Defaults:** `loading: false`, `selection: 'none'`, `selected: () => []`, `virtualScrollItemSize: 48`, `containerHeight: 'calc(100vh - 200px)'`

**Events:**
```typescript
emit: {
  'row-click': [row: T, index: number, event: MouseEvent];
  'update:selected': [selected: T[]];
}
```

**Slots:** All QTable slots forwarded via `v-for`

---

### 6. **TableToolbar.vue** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/widgets/TableToolbar.vue`

**Props:**
```typescript
interface TableToolbarProps {
  searchPlaceholder?: string;
  searchModelValue?: string;
}
```

**Defaults:** `searchPlaceholder: '搜索...'`, `searchModelValue: ''`

**Events:**
```typescript
emit: {
  'update:searchModelValue': [value: string];
}
```

**Slots:**
- `filters` - For filter controls between search and actions
- `actions` - For action buttons on the right side

---

### 7. **StatusBadge.vue** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/widgets/StatusBadge.vue`

**Props:**
```typescript
interface Props {
  readonly status: string;
  readonly statusMap: Record<string, { label: string; color: string }>;
}
```

**Implementation:**
- Falls back to status as label with 'grey' color if not found in map
- Uses Quasar `q-badge` with outline style

---

### 8. **FieldEditWidget.vue** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue`

**Props:**
```typescript
interface Props {
  readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
  readonly values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
  readonly direction: 'send' | 'receive';
}
```

**Events:**
```typescript
emit: {
  'update:values': [values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>];
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Classifies fields into: `configurable`, `expression`, `fixed`
- Supports input types: `input`, `select`, `radio`
- Handles numeric fields with factor display
- Read-only display for expression/fixed fields

---

**Semantic Classes:**

**Configuration:**

export default defineConfig({
  presets: [presetUno()],
  theme: {

  }

```

**Spacing Scale:** 0, 0.5, 1-12, 14, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 36, 40, 44, 48, 52, 56, 60, 64, 72, 80, 96 (mapped to pixel values)

---

### 11. **SendPage.vue** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/pages/SendPage.vue`

**Structure:**
- **3-column layout:**
  - Left (240px): Frame format list with search, favorites filter
  - Middle (flex:1): Instance table with DataTable
  - Right (300px): Preview panel + send controls

**Key Patterns:**
- Uses `useSendInstances` for instance management
- Uses `useFramePreview` for hex preview
- Uses `useAsyncAction` for send operation
- Dialog for editing instances with FieldEditWidget
- Manual refresh tracking via `refreshKey` ref

---

### 12. **Send Composables** - `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/composables/`

#### **use-send-instances.ts**
```typescript
function useSendInstances(): {
  instances: ShallowRef<SendFrameInstance[]>;
  selectedInstanceId: Ref<string | null>;
  isLoaded: Ref<boolean>;
  load(): void;
  createInstance(frameId, frameName, userFieldValues?, description?): SendFrameInstance;
  updateInstance(instanceId, updates): SendFrameInstance | undefined;
  removeInstance(instanceId): void;
  cloneInstance(instanceId): SendFrameInstance | undefined;
  incrementSendCount(instanceId): void;
  moveInstanceUp(instanceId): void;
  moveInstanceDown(instanceId): void;
  selectInstance(instanceId): void;
}
```

#### **use-frame-preview.ts**
```typescript
interface FramePreviewResult {
  readonly hexPreview: string;
  readonly fieldPreviews: ReadonlyArray<{
    readonly fieldId: string;
    readonly fieldName: string;
    readonly value: SendFieldValue;
  }>;
  readonly issues: ReadonlyArray<{ readonly severity: string; readonly message: string }>;
}

function useFramePreview(
  frame: Ref<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>,
  userFieldValues: Ref<Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>>
): {
  fullPreview: ComputedRef<FramePreviewResult>;
}
```

**Implementation Details:**
- Builds frame using `frameToBuildInput`, `resolveFieldValues`, `applyFactor`, `buildFrame`, `applyBuildPostPatch`
- Returns hex preview with space-separated bytes
- Collects issues from all build stages
- Uses noop variable provider for UI preview
```

35	function readCssPixelToken(name: string): number | undefined {
36	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
37	    return undefined;
38	  }
39	
40	  const rawValue = window.getComputedStyle(window.document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name);
41	  const parsedValue = Number.parseFloat(rawValue);
42	
43	  return Number.isFinite(parsedValue) ? parsedValue : undefined;
44	}

55	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
56	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
57	    if (!field.configurable) continue;
58	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
59	      const num = Number(field.defaultValue);
60	      values[field.id] = !Number.isNaN(num) && field.defaultValue.trim() !== '' ? num : field.defaultValue;
61	    } else if (field.options.length > 0) {
62	      const defaultOpt = field.options.find(o => o.isDefault);
63	      values[field.id] = defaultOpt ? Number(defaultOpt.value) || defaultOpt.value : Number(field.options[0].value) || field.options[0].value;
64	    }
65	  }
66	  return values;
67	}

2	  createFrameAssetService,

4	  type FrameAssetService,
5	} from '@/features/frame';
6	import {
7	  createSettingsService,
8	  type SettingsService,
9	} from '@/features/settings';
10	import {
11	  createStorageLocalReader,
12	  type StorageLocalReader,
13	} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
14	import {
15	  createConnectionService,
16	  type ConnectionService,
17	  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
18	} from '@/features/connection';
19	import {
20	  createReceiveService,
21	  type ReceiveService,
22	} from '@/features/receive';
23	import {
24	  createSendService,
25	  type SendService,
26	} from '@/features/send';
27	import {
28	  createTaskService,
29	  type TaskService,
30	} from '@/features/task';
31	import { ConnectionBackedSendWriter } from './bridges/connection-backed-writer';
32	import { ConnectionBackedTargetResolver } from './bridges/connection-backed-target-resolver';
33	import { ReceiveEventSourceBridge } from './bridges/receive-event-source-bridge';
34	
35	export interface RewriteWiredFeatures {
36	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
37	  readonly frameService: FrameAssetService;
38	  readonly settingsService: SettingsService;
39	  readonly storageReader: StorageLocalReader;
40	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
41	  readonly receiveService: ReceiveService;
42	  readonly sendService: SendService;
43	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
44	  readonly receiveEventSourceBridge: ReceiveEventSourceBridge;
45	}
46	
47	export interface WireFeaturesOptions {
48	  readonly connectionAdapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
49	}
50	
51	function createDefaultFrameService(): FrameAssetService {
52	  return createFrameAssetService();
53	}
54	
55	function createDefaultStorageReader(): StorageLocalReader {
56	  return createStorageLocalReader(() => ({
57	    records: [],
58	    historyMaterials: [],
59	    csvMaterials: [],
60	    legacyMaterials: [],
61	  }));
62	}
63	
64	export function wireFeatures(
65	  options: WireFeaturesOptions,
66	): RewriteWiredFeatures {
67	  // L0: no cross-dependencies
68	  const frameService = createDefaultFrameService();
69	  const frameReader = frameService;
70	  const settingsService = createSettingsService();
71	  const storageReader = createDefaultStorageReader();
72	
73	  // L1: needs adapter
74	  const connectionService = createConnectionService({
75	    adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
76	  });
77	
78	  // L2: needs L0 + L1
79	  const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });
80	
81	  const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
82	  const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
83	  const sendService = createSendService({
84	    frameReader,
85	    targetResolver,
86	    transportWriter: sendWriter,
87	  });
88	
89	  // L3: needs L2
90	  const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
91	  const taskService = createTaskService({
92	    sendService,
93	    receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
94	  });
95	
96	  return {
97	    frameReader,
98	    frameService,
99	    settingsService,
100	    storageReader,
101	    connectionService,
102	    receiveService,
103	    sendService,
104	    taskService,
105	    receiveEventSourceBridge,
106	  };
107	}
108

1	import { inject, provide, type InjectionKey } from 'vue';
2	import { createRewriteRuntime, type RewriteRuntime } from '@/runtime';
3	import { getTransportFacade } from '@/platform';
4	import { createRealSerialAdapter } from '@/features/connection';
5	
6	const rewriteRuntimeKey: InjectionKey<RewriteRuntime> = Symbol('rewrite-runtime');
7	
8	export interface BootstrapResult {
9	  readonly runtime: RewriteRuntime;
10	  readonly mode: 'real' | 'noOp';
11	}
12	
13	export function bootstrapRewriteRuntime(): BootstrapResult {
14	  const transportFacade = getTransportFacade();
15	  if (transportFacade) {
16	    const adapter = createRealSerialAdapter({ transport: transportFacade });
17	    const runtime = createRewriteRuntime({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
18	    return { runtime, mode: 'real' };
19	  }
20	
21	  const runtime = createRewriteRuntime();
22	  return { runtime, mode: 'noOp' };
23	}
24	
25	export function provideRewriteRuntime(runtime: RewriteRuntime = createRewriteRuntime()): RewriteRuntime {
26	  provide(rewriteRuntimeKey, runtime);
27	  return runtime;
28	}
29	
30	export function useRewriteRuntime(): RewriteRuntime {
31	  const runtime = inject(rewriteRuntimeKey);
32	  if (!runtime) {
33	    throw new Error('Rewrite runtime has not been provided.');
34	  }
35	
36	  return runtime;
37	}
38	
```

1	import {
2	  type FrameAssetReader,
3	} from '@/features/frame';
4	import {
5	  type SettingsOperationResult,
6	  type SettingsResetScope,
7	  type SettingsService,
8	} from '@/features/settings';
9	import {
10	  type StorageLocalReader,
11	} from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
12	import {
13	  type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
14	} from '@/features/connection';
15	import { wireFeatures, type RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
16	import { routingTick, type RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
17	
18	export type { RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
19	export type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
20	
21	export interface RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot {
22	  readonly frame: {
23	    readonly totalFrames: number;
24	    readonly totalFields: number;
25	    readonly selectedFrameName: string | null;
26	  };
27	  readonly settings: {
28	    readonly autoStartRecording: boolean;
29	    readonly csvDefaultOutputPath: string;
30	    readonly csvSaveIntervalMinutes: number;
31	  };
32	  readonly storage: {
33	    readonly localRecordCount: number;
34	    readonly historyHourCount: number;
35	    readonly csvMaterialCount: number;
36	    readonly legacyMaterialCount: number;
37	    readonly lastIssue: {
38	      readonly code: string;
39	      readonly message: string;
40	    } | null;
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	export interface RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
45	  readonly ok: boolean;
46	  readonly issues: readonly {
47	    readonly severity: string;
48	    readonly code: string;
49	    readonly message: string;
50	  }[];
51	}
52	
53	type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;
54	type SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;
55	type StorageOverviewPort = Pick<
56	  StorageLocalReader,
57	  | 'getLastIssue'
58	  | 'listCsvMaterials'
59	  | 'listHistoryHours'
60	  | 'listLegacyMaterials'
61	  | 'listLocalRecords'
62	>;
63	
64	export const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
65	
66	export interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {
67	  readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
68	  readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;
69	  readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;
70	  readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;
71	}
72	
73	export interface RewriteRuntime {
74	  getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
75	  resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
76	  readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
77	  routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;
78	  startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;
79	  stopTickDriver(): void;
80	  readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;
81	  destroy(): void;
82	}
83	
84	function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
85	  const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
86	  return {
87	    connect: async () => accepted,
88	    disconnect: async () => accepted,
89	    write: async () => accepted,
90	    cleanup: async () => accepted,
91	    drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
92	  };
93	}
94	
95	function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
96	  return {
97	    ok: result.ok,
98	    issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
99	      severity: issue.severity,
100	      code: issue.code,
101	      message: issue.message,
102	    })),
103	  };
104	}
105	
106	export function createRewriteRuntime(
107	  dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
108	): RewriteRuntime {
109	  const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
110	  const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
111	
112	  const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
113	  const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
114	  const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;
115	
116	  let destroyed = false;
117	  let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
118	
119	  function stopTick(): void {
120	    if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
121	      clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
122	      tickIntervalId = null;
123	    }
124	  }
125	
126	  return {
127	    features: wiredFeatures,
128	
129	    getOverviewSnapshot() {
130	      const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
131	      const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
132	      const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
133	      const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
134	
135	      return {
136	        frame: {
137	          totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
138	          totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
139	          selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
140	        },
141	        settings: {
142	          autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
143	          csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
144	          csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
145	        },
146	        storage: {
147	          localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
148	          historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
149	          csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
150	          legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
151	          lastIssue: storageIssue
152	            ? {
153	                code: storageIssue.code,
154	                message: storageIssue.message,
155	              }
156	            : null,
157	        },
158	      };
159	    },
160	
161	    resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
162	      return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
163	    },
164	
165	    routingTick() {
166	      return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
167	    },
168	
169	    startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
170	      if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
171	      tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
172	        routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
173	      }, intervalMs);
174	    },
175	
176	    stopTickDriver() {
177	      stopTick();
178	    },
179	
180	    get isTickDriverRunning() {
181	      return tickIntervalId !== null;
182	    },
183	
184	    destroy() {
185	      if (destroyed) return;
186	      destroyed = true;
187	      stopTick();
188	      wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
189	      wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
190	    },
191	  };
192	}
193

1	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';

4	
5	// --- Step kinds ---
6	
7	export const TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay'] as const;
8	export type TaskStepKind = (typeof TASK_STEP_KINDS)[number];
9	
10	// --- Comparison ---
11	
12	export const COMPARISON_OPERATORS = [
13	  'eq',
14	  'neq',
15	  'gt',
16	  'lt',
17	  'gte',
18	  'lte',
19	  'contains',
20	  'change',
21	  'any',
22	] as const;
23	export type ComparisonOperator = (typeof COMPARISON_OPERATORS)[number];
24	
25	// --- ConditionTerm (replaces WaitCondition, adds logicOperator) ---
26	
27	export interface ConditionTerm {
28	  readonly frameId: string;
29	  readonly fieldId: string;
30	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
31	  readonly threshold: string | number;
32	  readonly sourceId?: string;
33	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or'; // default 'and'; first term's logicOperator is ignored
34	}
35	
36	// --- ScheduleDriver (replaces TaskSchedulingMode + flat scheduling fields) ---
37	
38	export type ScheduleDriver =
39	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
40	  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
41	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
42	
43	// --- FieldVariation ---
44	
45	export interface FieldVariation {
46	  readonly fieldId: string;
47	  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
48	}
49	
50	// --- StepRepeat ---
51	
52	export interface StepRepeat {
53	  readonly intervalMs: number;
54	  readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
55	  readonly maxCount?: number;
56	}
57	
58	// --- Step definitions ---
59	
60	export interface SendStepConfig {
61	  readonly frameId: string;
62	  readonly targetId: string; // required (was optional + fallback to definition.targetId)
63	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
64	  readonly variables?: VariableMap;
65	  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;
66	  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;
67	}
68	
69	export interface WaitConditionConfig {
70	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // single condition -> condition array
71	  readonly timeoutMs?: number; // optional (undefined = wait indefinitely until signal interrupt)
72	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
73	}
74	
75	export interface DelayStepConfig {
76	  readonly durationMs: number;
77	}
78	
79	// --- Unified TaskStepDefinition (config field name unified) ---
80	
81	export type TaskStepDefinition =
82	  | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
83	  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
84	  | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
85	
86	// --- Error policy ---
87	
88	export const TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause'] as const;
89	export type TaskErrorAction = (typeof TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS)[number];
90	
91	export interface TaskErrorPolicy {
92	  readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction;
93	  readonly retryCount?: number;
94	  readonly retryDelayMs?: number;
95	}
96	
97	// --- Stop condition ---
98	
99	export interface TaskStopCondition {
100	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
101	  readonly maxIterations?: number;
102	  readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // new: early exit when condition met
103	}
104	
105	// --- TaskDefinition (restructured) ---
106	
107	export interface TaskDefinition {
108	  readonly id: string;
109	  readonly name: string;
110	  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
111	  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver; // replaces schedulingMode + flat scheduling fields
112	  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
113	  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
114	  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
115	}
116	
117	// --- Lifecycle ---
118	
119	export const TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = [
120	  'created',
121	  'running',
122	  'paused',
123	  'stopped',
124	  'completed',
125	  'failed',
126	] as const;
127	export type TaskLifecycleStatus = (typeof TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES)[number];
128	
129	export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail';
130	
131	// --- Step results ---
132	
133	export interface SendStepResult {
134	  readonly kind: 'send';
135	  readonly sendResult: SendResult;
136	}
137	
138	export interface WaitConditionStepResult {
139	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
140	  readonly matched: boolean;
141	  readonly matchedValue?: number | string;
142	  readonly timedOut: boolean;
143	}
144	
145	export interface DelayStepResult {
146	  readonly kind: 'delay';
147	  readonly completed: boolean;
148	}
149	
150	export type TaskStepResultPayload =
151	  | SendStepResult
152	  | WaitConditionStepResult
153	  | DelayStepResult;
154	
155	export interface TaskStepResult extends TaskStepResultPayload {
156	  readonly stepIndex: number;
157	  readonly iteration: number;
158	  readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction;
159	}
160	
161	// --- Instance state ---
162	
163	export interface TaskInstanceState {
164	  readonly instanceId: string;
165	  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
166	  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus;
167	  readonly startedAt?: string;
168	  readonly pausedAt?: string;
169	  readonly stoppedAt?: string;
170	  readonly completedAt?: string;
171	  readonly failedAt?: string;
172	  readonly currentStepIndex: number;
173	  readonly currentIteration: number;
174	  readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[];
175	  readonly error?: string;
176	}
177	
178	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
179	
180	// --- Progress ---
181	
182	export interface TaskProgress {
183	  readonly stepsTotal: number;
184	  readonly stepsCompleted: number;
185	  readonly stepsFailed: number;
186	  readonly stepsSkipped: number;
187	  readonly iterationsCompleted: number;
188	  readonly iterationsTotal: number | null;
189	  readonly elapsedMs: number;
190	  readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null;
191	  readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null;
192	}
193	
194	// --- Execution summary ---
195	
196	export interface TaskExecutionSummary {
197	  readonly kind: 'completed' | 'stopped' | 'failed';
198	  readonly summary: {
199	    readonly stepsTotal: number;
200	    readonly stepsSucceeded: number;
201	    readonly stepsFailed: number;
202	    readonly stepsSkipped: number;
203	  };
204	  readonly durationMs: number;
205	  readonly startedAt: string;
206	  readonly finishedAt: string;
207	}
208	
209	// --- Condition match input ---
210	// Decoupled from receive internals. Service layer maps from receive public API outputs.
211	
212	export interface ConditionMatchInput {
213	  readonly frameId: string;
214	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
215	  readonly sourceId?: string;
216	}
217	
218	// --- ResolvedStopCondition & resolveStopCondition ---
219	
220	export type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
221	
222	export function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
223	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
224	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
225	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
226	  }
227	  // Immediate schedule defaults to 1 iteration
228	  if (def.schedule.kind === 'immediate' && !def.stopCondition?.maxIterations) {
229	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: 1 };
230	  }
231	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
232	}
233	
```

  2. codestable/quality/rewrite-frontend-checklist.md

  8. rewrite/src/features/task/core/serialization.ts — serialize/deserialize

  10. rewrite/src/shared/composables/（useAsyncAction、usePolling、useNotify、useStableKeys）
  11. rewrite/src/css/layers/_utilities.scss
  12. rewrite/uno.config.ts

  ### 已有基础设施（直接消费）
  - widgets/DataTable.vue + TableToolbar.vue + StatusBadge.vue
  - widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue（发送页已创建）
  - shared/composables/{use-async-action,use-polling,use-notify,use-stable-keys}.ts
  - css/layers/_utilities.scss 全套

  ### 前置条件
  - Prompt 4 发送页已完成（FieldEditWidget、SendTargetSelector 可复用）
  - G1（updateTask）、G2（历史记录 elapsedMs）、G3（stopAll 返回 number）均已修复

  ### 实现范围
  1. widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue — 跨页面执行监控（#actions + #extension slot）
  2. features/task/composables/use-task-list.ts — mode: 'active' | 'history'
  3. features/task/composables/use-task-editor.ts — 编辑表单 + validate + save
  4. features/task/composables/use-task-monitor.ts — 运行态轮询 + 状态映射
  5. features/task/components/task-columns.ts
  6. features/task/components/history-columns.ts
  7. features/task/components/taskStatusMap.ts — 任务状态→{label, color}映射
  8. features/task/components/scheduleKindMap.ts — immediate/timer/event→中文标签+QChip颜色
  9. features/task/components/task-labels.ts — 步骤类型标签等不变映射（O4）
  10. pages/TaskManagePage.vue — 主从双栏 + Tab keep-alive
  11. 路由注册 /tasks

  ### 子 Agent 策略
  - 阅读：派 explore agent 并行读 task feature 全部代码 + conventions + G1~G3 状态
  - 执行（3 个 executor 并行）：
    - Executor A：TaskExecutionDetail.vue
    - Executor B：composables + statusMap + columns + labels
    - Executor C：TaskManagePage.vue + 路由
  - 验证：派 code-reviewer 对照 conventions + 任务域 SRA

  ### 关键约束

  **布局（D-T2）：**
  - 主从双栏：左任务列表 flex:1 + 右面板 360px
  - 编辑器全宽对话框 rw-dialog-xl + rw-dialog-scroll-body
  - Tab: 活动 | 历史，keep-alive

  **调度类型（D-T5 — 关键类型变化）：**
  - QSelect 选项：immediate / timer / event
  - schedule.kind 动态渲染：
    - immediate: 无额外配置
    - timer: 间隔 + 次数归口 TaskStopCondition.maxIterations + 无限循环 toggle
    - event: 条件编辑器（ConditionTerm 列表，至少一条）
  - 列定义字段：schedule.kind（不是 schedulingMode）
  - QChip 映射用 scheduleKindMap

  **状态推导（D-T7）：**
  - running + schedule.kind='event' → "等待触发"
  - running + schedule.kind='timer' → "等待调度"
  - taskStatusMap 中定义完整映射含推导状态
  - StatusBadge :status-map="taskStatusMap"

  **Phase 2 功能隐藏：**
  - fieldVariations、exitCondition、step repeat 类型已就绪
  - UI 不渲染编辑器，高级配置区标注"待后续版本"

  **Service API（已修正）：**
  - stopAll(): number
  - retryTask(id): TaskInstanceState | undefined（同步）
  - updateTask(id, definition): TaskInstanceState | undefined（仅 created 态）

  **步骤编辑器：**
  - SendStep: FrameSelector + SendTargetSelector + FieldEditWidget(:values="step.config.userFieldValues") + intervalAfterMs + 可选 repeat
  - WaitConditionStep: ConditionTerm 列表 + timeoutMs + onTimeout(continue/skip/fail)
  - DelayStep: durationMs
  - 步骤用 QExpansionItem 展开/折叠

  **数据流（P4/O3/O4）：**
  - 任务列表 shallowRef + watch(selector 结果) 手动更新
  - 轮询用 usePolling(fn, 1000)
  - 不变映射（statusMap、kindMap、labels）放模块级（O4）
  - 步骤类型标签如出现 2+ 处相同模式提取（O3）

  **异步操作：**
  - start/pause/resume/stop/remove/retry 用 useAsyncAction().execute
  - 通知用 useNotify()

  ### 验证
  - pnpm -C rewrite build
  - pnpm -C rewrite lint 通用纪律（每个对话适用）

  1. 先读完 conventions + checklist 再写代码
  2. 间距用 UnoCSS（gap-3 p-4），颜色用 rw-text-* / Quasar brand，不硬编码
  3. 弹窗宽度用 rw-dialog-{sm|md|lg|xl} + rw-dialog-scroll-body
  4. 派生数组用 shallowRef + watch（P4），不用 computed + .map/.filter
  5. useAsyncAction / useNotify / usePolling / useStableKeys 四件套
  6. StatusBadge 必须传 statusMap（各 feature 定义自己的映射）
  7. 不变映射放模块级常量（O4），2+ 处相同模式提取（O3）
  8. 每批组件完成就跑 build + lint，不攒到最后
