```
1	import type {
2	  TaskDefinition,
3	  TaskInstanceState,
4	  TaskProgress,
5	} from '../core';
6	import { isTerminal } from '../core';
7	import type { SendServiceProvider, ReceiveEventSource } from '../adapters';
8	import type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';
9	import { createTaskState, type TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
10	import {
11	  selectTaskInstance,
12	  selectTaskProgress,
13	  selectTaskSnapshot,
14	  selectTaskStatistics,
15	} from '../selectors';
16	import { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
17	import { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
18	import { createLifecycleManager } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
19	import { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';
20	import { createIterationLoops } from './task-iteration-loops';
21	
22	// --- Public interfaces ---
23	
24	export interface TaskReader {
25	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
26	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
27	  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined;
28	  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
29	}
30	
31	export interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
32	  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
33	  startTask(instanceId: string): void;
34	  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;
35	  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;
36	  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;
37	  stopAll(): number;
38	  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;
39	  retryTask(sourceInstanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
40	  updateTask(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
41	  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;
42	}
43	
44	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
45	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
46	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
47	  readonly timerService?: unknown; // placeholder for future TimerService injection
48	  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
49	  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;
50	  readonly now?: () => string;
51	}
52	
53	// --- Factory ---
54	
55	let nextInstanceId = 1;
56	
57	export function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService {
58	  const sendService = options.sendService;
59	  const receiveSource = options.receiveEventSource;
60	  const state = options.state ?? createTaskState();
61	  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
62	  const fieldValueProvider = options.fieldValueProvider;
63	
64	  // Execution control
65	  const abortResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
66	  const settleResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
67	
68	  // Condition matching
69	  const conditionRegistry = new ConditionRegistry();
70	  let receiveSubscription: (() => void) | null = null;
71	  let subscriptionRefCount = 0;
72	
73	  // Wire sub-modules
74	  const lifecycle = createLifecycleManager({
75	    state,
76	    conditionRegistry,
77	    abortResolvers,
78	    settleResolvers,
79	    now,
80	  });
81	
82	  const stepExecutors = createStepExecutors({
83	    state,
84	    sendService,
85	    conditionRegistry,
86	    fieldValueProvider,
87	    now,
88	  });
89	
90	  const errorPolicy = createErrorPolicyHandler({
91	    state,
92	    conditionRegistry,
93	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
94	    stepExecutors,
95	    now,
96	  });
97	
98	  const loops = createIterationLoops({
99	    state,
100	    conditionRegistry,
101	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
102	    stepExecutors,
103	    errorPolicy,
104	    fieldValueProvider,
105	    now,
106	  });
107	
108	  // --- Subscription management ---
109	
110	  function ensureSubscription(): void {
111	    if (receiveSubscription) {
112	      subscriptionRefCount++;
113	      return;
114	    }
115	    receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {
116	      conditionRegistry.processInput(input);
117	    });
118	    subscriptionRefCount = 1;
119	  }
120	
121	  function releaseSubscription(): void {
122	    subscriptionRefCount--;
123	    if (subscriptionRefCount <= 0 && receiveSubscription) {
124	      receiveSubscription();
125	      receiveSubscription = null;
126	      subscriptionRefCount = 0;
127	    }
128	  }
129	
130	  // --- Execution loop orchestration ---
131	
132	  async function runExecutionLoop(
133	    instanceId: string,
134	    signal: Promise<void>,
135	  ): Promise<void> {
136	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
137	    if (!inst) return;
138	
139	    try {
140	      ensureSubscription();
141	
142	      // Use the unified runTask via the loops context
143	      await loops.runTimedLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
144	
145	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
146	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
147	        lifecycle.completeTask(instanceId);
148	      }
149	    } catch (err) {
150	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
151	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
152	        lifecycle.failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
153	      }
154	    } finally {
155	      releaseSubscription();
156	      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
157	    }
158	  }
159	
160	  // --- Public API ---
161	
162	  return {
163	    getSnapshot() {
164	      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());
165	    },
166	
167	    getInstance(instanceId) {
168	      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
169	    },
170	
171	    getProgress(instanceId) {
172	      return selectTaskProgress(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
173	    },
174	
175	    getStatistics() {
176	      return selectTaskStatistics(state.getSnapshot());
177	    },
178	
179	    createTask(definition) {
180	      const instanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
181	      return state.createInstance(instanceId, definition);
182	    },
183	
184	    startTask(instanceId) {
185	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
186	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return;
187	
188	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'start');
189	
190	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
191	
192	      // Fire-and-forget execution loop
193	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
194	    },
195	
196	    pauseTask(instanceId) {
197	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
198	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
199	
200	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
201	      conditionRegistry.clear();
202	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
203	    },
204	
205	    resumeTask(instanceId) {
206	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
207	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
208	
209	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'resume');
210	
211	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
212	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
213	    },
214	
215	    stopTask(instanceId) {
216	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
217	      if (!inst) return;
218	      if (inst.lifecycle !== 'running' && inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
219	
220	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
221	      conditionRegistry.clear();
222	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
223	      lifecycle.resolveSettle(instanceId);
224	    },
225	
226	    stopAll(): number {
227	      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
228	      let stoppedCount = 0;
229	      for (const inst of snapshot.instances) {
230	        if (inst.lifecycle === 'running' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
231	          lifecycle.updateLifecycle(inst.instanceId, 'stop');
232	          conditionRegistry.clear();
233	          lifecycle.abortInstance(inst.instanceId);
234	          lifecycle.resolveSettle(inst.instanceId);
235	          stoppedCount++;
236	        }
237	      }
238	      return stoppedCount;
239	    },
240	
241	    removeTask(instanceId) {
242	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
243	      if (!inst) return;
244	      if (!isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
245	
246	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
247	      state.removeInstance(instanceId);
248	    },
249	
250	    retryTask(sourceInstanceId) {
251	      const source = state.getInstance(sourceInstanceId);
252	      if (!source || !isTerminal(source.lifecycle)) return undefined;
253	
254	      const newInstanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
255	      state.createInstance(newInstanceId, source.definitionRef);
256	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(newInstanceId, 'start');
257	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(newInstanceId);
258	      runExecutionLoop(newInstanceId, signal);
259	      return state.getInstance(newInstanceId);
260	    },
261	
262	    updateTask(instanceId, definition) {
263	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
264	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return undefined;
265	      return state.updateInstance(instanceId, { definitionRef: definition });
266	    },
267	
268	    async onSettled(instanceId) {
269	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
270	      if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
271	
272	      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
273	        settleResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
274	      });
275	    },
276	  };
277	}
278	
```

```
1	import type {
2	  TaskInstanceState,
3	  TaskProgress,
4	} from '../core';
5	import { calculateProgress } from '../core';
6	import type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';
7	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
8	
9	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
10	
11	export function selectTaskInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[] {
12	  return [...snapshot.instances] as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
13	}
14	
15	export function selectTaskInstance(
16	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
17	  instanceId: string,
18	): ReadonlyTaskInstanceState | undefined {
19	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
20	  return instance ? structuredClone(instance) as ReadonlyTaskInstanceState : undefined;
21	}
22	
23	export function selectTaskProgress(
24	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
25	  instanceId: string,
26	): TaskProgress | undefined {
27	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
28	  return instance ? calculateProgress(instance) : undefined;
29	}
30	
31	const TERMINATED_LIFECYCLES = new Set(['completed', 'failed', 'stopped']);
32	
33	export function selectTaskHistory(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[] {
34	  const historyInstances = snapshot.instances.filter(
35	    (i) => TERMINATED_LIFECYCLES.has(i.lifecycle),
36	  );
37	  const historyIds = new Set(snapshot.history.map((i) => i.instanceId));
38	  const merged = [...snapshot.history, ...historyInstances.filter((i) => !historyIds.has(i.instanceId))];
39	  return merged as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
40	}
41	
42	export function selectTaskStatistics(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): Readonly<TaskStatisticsSnapshot> {
43	  return { ...snapshot.statistics };
44	}
45	
46	export function selectActiveInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[] {
47	  return snapshot.instances.filter(
48	    (i) => i.lifecycle === 'created' || i.lifecycle === 'running' || i.lifecycle === 'paused',
49	  ) as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
50	}
51	
52	export interface TaskUiSnapshot {
53	  readonly instances: readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
54	  readonly history: readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[];
55	  readonly statistics: Readonly<TaskStatisticsSnapshot>;
56	}
57	
58	export function selectTaskSnapshot(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskUiSnapshot {
59	  return {
60	    instances: [...snapshot.instances] as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[],
61	    history: [...snapshot.history] as readonly ReadonlyTaskInstanceState[],
62	    statistics: { ...snapshot.statistics },
63	  };
64	}
65
```

```
4	
5	interface DataTableProps {
6	  columns: QTableColumn[];
7	  rows: readonly T[];
8	  rowKey: string;
9	  loading?: boolean;
10	  selection?: 'single' | 'multiple' | 'none';
11	  selected?: readonly T[];
12	  virtualScrollItemSize?: number;
13	  containerHeight?: string;

15	
16	const props = withDefaults(defineProps<DataTableProps>(), {
17	  loading: false,
18	  selection: 'none',

20	  virtualScrollItemSize: 48,

22	});
23	
24	const emit = defineEmits<{

27	}>();
28	
29	const tableRef = ref<QTableProps | null>(null);

50	    :virtual-scroll-item-size="virtualScrollItemSize"
51	    :rows-per-page-options="[0]"

62	    <template v-for="(_, slot) in $slots" #[slot]="scope">
```

```
1	import type { TaskDefinition } from './types';
2	
3	const SERIALIZATION_VERSION = 1;
4	
5	export interface SerializedTaskDefinition {
6	  readonly _version: number;
7	  readonly definition: TaskDefinition;
8	}
9	
10	export function serializeTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): string {
11	  const wrapped: SerializedTaskDefinition = { _version: SERIALIZATION_VERSION, definition: def };
12	  return JSON.stringify(wrapped);
13	}
14	
15	export function deserializeTaskDefinition(json: string): TaskDefinition {
16	  const parsed = JSON.parse(json) as SerializedTaskDefinition;
17	
18	  if (!parsed._version || !parsed.definition) {
19	    throw new Error('Invalid task definition format: missing _version or definition.');
20	  }
21	
22	  if (parsed._version > SERIALIZATION_VERSION) {
23	    throw new Error(`Unsupported task definition version: ${parsed._version}. Maximum supported: ${SERIALIZATION_VERSION}.`);
24	  }
25	
26	  return parsed.definition;
27	}
28
```

```
1	import type { TaskDefinition, SendStepConfig, WaitConditionConfig, TaskStopCondition } from './types';
2	
3	export interface TaskValidationIssue {
4	  readonly severity: 'error' | 'warning';
5	  readonly code: string;
6	  readonly message: string;
7	  readonly stepId?: string;
8	}
9	
10	export function validateTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): TaskValidationIssue[] {
11	  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];
12	
13	  if (!def.id) {
14	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.definition.missingId', message: 'Task definition id is required.' });
15	  }
16	
17	  if (!def.name) {
18	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.definition.missingName', message: 'Task definition name is required.' });
19	  }
20	
21	  if (!def.steps || def.steps.length === 0) {
22	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.definition.emptySteps', message: 'Task must have at least one step.' });
23	    return issues;
24	  }
25	
26	  const stepIds = new Set<string>();
27	  for (const step of def.steps) {
28	    if (!step.id) {
29	      issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.missingId', message: `Step at index ${def.steps.indexOf(step)} is missing an id.` });
30	    } else if (stepIds.has(step.id)) {
31	      issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.duplicateId', message: `Duplicate step id: "${step.id}".`, stepId: step.id });
32	    } else {
33	      stepIds.add(step.id);
34	    }
35	
36	    switch (step.kind) {
37	      case 'send':
38	        issues.push(...validateSendStepConfig(step.id, step.config));
39	        break;
40	      case 'wait-condition':
41	        issues.push(...validateWaitConditionConfig(step.id, step.config));
42	        break;
43	      case 'delay':
44	        if (!step.config.durationMs || step.config.durationMs <= 0) {
45	          issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.invalidDelay', message: `Delay step "${step.id}" must have durationMs > 0.`, stepId: step.id });
46	        }
47	        break;
48	    }
49	  }
50	
51	  if (def.stopCondition) {
52	    issues.push(...validateStopCondition(def.stopCondition));
53	  }
54	
55	  return issues;
56	}
57	
58	function validateSendStepConfig(stepId: string, config: SendStepConfig): TaskValidationIssue[] {
59	  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];
60	  if (!config.frameId) {
61	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.send.missingFrameId', message: `Send step "${stepId}" must have a frameId.`, stepId });
62	  }
63	  if (!config.targetId) {
64	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.send.missingTargetId', message: `Send step "${stepId}" must have a targetId.`, stepId });
65	  }
66	  return issues;
67	}
68	
69	function validateWaitConditionConfig(stepId: string, config: WaitConditionConfig): TaskValidationIssue[] {
70	  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];
71	  if (!config.conditions || config.conditions.length === 0) {
72	    issues.push({ severity: 'error', code: 'task.step.wait.emptyConditions', message: `Wait-condition step "${stepId}" must have at least one condition.`, stepId });
73	  }
74	  if (config.timeoutMs !== undefined && config.timeoutMs <= 0) {
75	    issues.push({ severity: 'warning', code: 'task.step.wait.invalidTimeout', message: `Wait-condition step "${stepId}" has timeoutMs <= 0.`, stepId });
76	  }
77	  return issues;
78	}
79	
80	function validateStopCondition(cond: TaskStopCondition): TaskValidationIssue[] {
81	  const issues: TaskValidationIssue[] = [];
82	  if (cond.maxIterations !== undefined && cond.maxIterations <= 0) {
83	    issues.push({ severity: 'warning', code: 'task.stop.invalidMaxIterations', message: 'maxIterations should be > 0.' });
84	  }
85	  if (cond.maxDurationMs !== undefined && cond.maxDurationMs <= 0) {
86	    issues.push({ severity: 'warning', code: 'task.stop.invalidMaxDuration', message: 'maxDurationMs should be > 0.' });
87	  }
88	  return issues;
89	}
90
```

```
1	import type {
2	  SendStepConfig,
3	  WaitConditionConfig,
4	  TaskStepDefinition,
5	  TaskDefinition,
6	  ScheduleDriver,
7	  TaskErrorPolicy,
8	  TaskStopCondition,
9	  FieldVariation,
10	} from './types';
11	
12	let nextStepId = 1;
13	
14	export function createSendStep(
15	  config: SendStepConfig,
16	  options?: { readonly id?: string; readonly name?: string },
17	): TaskStepDefinition {
18	  return {
19	    id: options?.id ?? `step-${nextStepId++}`,
20	    name: options?.name,
21	    kind: 'send',
22	    config,
23	  };
24	}
25	
26	export function createDelayStep(
27	  durationMs: number,
28	  options?: { readonly id?: string; readonly name?: string },
29	): TaskStepDefinition {
30	  return {
31	    id: options?.id ?? `step-${nextStepId++}`,
32	    name: options?.name,
33	    kind: 'delay',
34	    config: { durationMs },
35	  };
36	}
37	
38	export function createWaitConditionStep(
39	  config: WaitConditionConfig,
40	  options?: { readonly id?: string; readonly name?: string },
41	): TaskStepDefinition {
42	  return {
43	    id: options?.id ?? `step-${nextStepId++}`,
44	    name: options?.name,
45	    kind: 'wait-condition',
46	    config,
47	  };
48	}
49	
50	export function createTaskDefinition(
51	  options: {
52	    readonly id: string;
53	    readonly name: string;
54	    readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver;
55	    readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
56	    readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
57	    readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
58	    readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
59	  },
60	): TaskDefinition {
61	  return options as TaskDefinition;
62	}
63	
64	export function cloneStepDefinition(step: Readonly<TaskStepDefinition>): TaskStepDefinition {
65	  return structuredClone(step);
66	}
67
```

```
1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { computed } from 'vue';
3	import type { FrameFieldDefinition } from '@/features/frame';
4	import type { SendFieldValue } from '@/features/send';
5	
6	// --- Types ---
7	
8	interface FieldSection {
9	  readonly key: string;
10	  readonly label: string;
11	  readonly color: string;
12	  readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
13	}
14	
15	// --- Props & Emits ---
16	
17	const props = defineProps<{
18	  readonly fields: readonly FrameFieldDefinition[];
19	  readonly values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
20	  readonly direction: 'send' | 'receive';
21	}>();
22	
23	const emit = defineEmits<{
24	  'update:values': [values: Record<string, SendFieldValue>];
25	}>();
26	
27	// --- Section definitions (O4: module-level constants) ---
28	
29	const SECTION_DEFS = [
30	  { key: 'configurable', label: '可调字段', color: 'info' },
31	  { key: 'expression', label: '计算参数', color: 'warning' },
32	  { key: 'fixed', label: '不可配置', color: 'grey' },
33	] as const;
34	
35	// --- Classification ---
36	
37	function classifyField(field: FrameFieldDefinition): 'configurable' | 'expression' | 'fixed' {
38	  if (field.configurable) return 'configurable';
39	  if (field.expressionConfig) return 'expression';
40	  return 'fixed';
41	}
42	
43	const sections = computed<readonly FieldSection[]>(() => {
44	  const buckets = new Map<string, FrameFieldDefinition[]>();
45	  for (const def of SECTION_DEFS) {
46	    buckets.set(def.key, []);
47	  }
48	  for (const field of props.fields) {
49	    const bucket = classifyField(field);
50	    buckets.get(bucket)!.push(field);
51	  }
52	  return SECTION_DEFS
53	    .map((def) => ({
54	      key: def.key,
55	      label: def.label,
56	      color: def.color,
57	      fields: buckets.get(def.key)!,
58	    }))
59	    .filter((section) => section.fields.length > 0);
60	});
61	
62	// --- Helpers ---
63	
64	const NUMERIC_DATA_TYPES = new Set([
65	  'uint8', 'int8', 'uint16', 'int16', 'uint32', 'int32',
66	  'uint64', 'int64', 'float', 'double',
67	]);
68	
69	function isNumericField(field: FrameFieldDefinition): boolean {
70	  return NUMERIC_DATA_TYPES.has(field.dataType);
71	}
72	
73	function hasNonTrivialFactor(field: FrameFieldDefinition): boolean {
74	  return field.factor !== undefined && field.factor !== 1;
75	}
76	
77	function getDisplayValue(field: FrameFieldDefinition): string {
78	  const val = props.values[field.id];
79	  if (val !== undefined && val !== null) return String(val);
80	  return '--';
81	}
82	
83	function resolveFieldInputValue(field: FrameFieldDefinition): SendFieldValue {
84	  return props.values[field.id] ?? field.defaultValue ?? '';
85	}
86	
87	// --- Mutation ---
88	
89	function onFieldChange(fieldId: string, value: SendFieldValue): void {
90	  emit('update:values', { ...props.values, [fieldId]: value });
91	}
92	
93	function onNumericInput(field: FrameFieldDefinition, raw: string | number | null): void {
94	  if (raw === null || raw === '') {
95	    onFieldChange(field.id, '');
96	    return;
97	  }
98	  const num = Number(raw);
99	  onFieldChange(field.id, Number.isNaN(num) ? String(raw) : num);
100	}
101	</script>
102	
103	<template>
104	  <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
105	    <template v-for="(section, si) in sections" :key="section.key">
106	      <q-separator v-if="si > 0" />
107	
108	      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
109	        <q-badge :color="section.color" :label="section.label" />
110	
111	        <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 pl-2">
112	          <template v-for="field in section.fields" :key="field.id">
113	
114	            <!-- ===== Configurable — input ===== -->
115	            <template v-if="section.key === 'configurable' && field.inputType === 'input'">
116	              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
117	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs min-w-[80px]">
118	                  {{ field.name }}
119	                  <template v-if="hasNonTrivialFactor(field)">
120	                    &nbsp;(x{{ field.factor }})
121	                  </template>
122	                </span>
123	                <q-input
124	                  outlined
125	                  dense
126	                  :type="isNumericField(field) ? 'number' : 'text'"
127	                  :model-value="resolveFieldInputValue(field)"
128	                  class="flex-1"
129	                  @update:model-value="onNumericInput(field, $event)"
130	                />
131	                <span v-if="field.description" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
132	                  {{ field.description }}
133	                </span>
134	              </div>
135	            </template>
136	
137	            <!-- ===== Configurable — select ===== -->
138	            <template v-else-if="section.key === 'configurable' && field.inputType === 'select'">
139	              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
140	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs min-w-[80px]">
141	                  {{ field.name }}
142	                </span>
143	                <q-select
144	                  outlined
145	                  dense
146	                  emit-value
147	                  map-options
148	                  :options="field.options"
149	                  :model-value="resolveFieldInputValue(field)"
150	                  class="flex-1"
151	                  @update:model-value="onFieldChange(field.id, $event)"
152	                />
153	                <span v-if="field.description" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
154	                  {{ field.description }}
155	                </span>
156	              </div>
157	            </template>
158	
159	            <!-- ===== Configurable — radio ===== -->
160	            <template v-else-if="section.key === 'configurable' && field.inputType === 'radio'">
161	              <div class="flex flex-col gap-1">
162	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">
163	                  {{ field.name }}
164	                </span>
165	                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
166	                  <q-radio
167	                    v-for="opt in field.options"
168	                    :key="opt.value"
169	                    dense
170	                    :val="opt.value"
171	                    :label="opt.label"
172	                    :model-value="resolveFieldInputValue(field)"
173	                    color="primary"
174	                    @update:model-value="onFieldChange(field.id, $event)"
175	                  />
176	                </div>
177	              </div>
178	            </template>
179	
180	            <!-- ===== Read-only: expression or fixed ===== -->
181	            <template v-else>
182	              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
183	                <span class="rw-text-label text-xs min-w-[80px]">
184	                  {{ field.name }}
185	                  <template v-if="hasNonTrivialFactor(field)">
186	                    &nbsp;(x{{ field.factor }})
187	                  </template>
188	                </span>
189	                <span class="rw-text-value text-sm">
190	                  {{ getDisplayValue(field) }}
191	                </span>
192	                <span v-if="field.description" class="rw-text-desc text-xs">
193	                  {{ field.description }}
194	                </span>
195	              </div>
196	            </template>
197	
198	          </template>
199	        </div>
200	      </div>
201	    </template>
202	  </div>
203	</template>
204
```

```
1	import { shallowRef } from 'vue';
2	import { useQuasar } from 'quasar';
3	
4	export function useAsyncAction() {
5	  const $q = useQuasar();
6	  const operatingIds = shallowRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
7	
8	  async function execute<T>(id: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T | undefined> {
9	    if (operatingIds.value.has(id)) return undefined;
10	
11	    const next = new Set(operatingIds.value);
12	    next.add(id);
13	    operatingIds.value = next;
14	
15	    try {
16	      return await fn();
17	    } catch (err) {
18	      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
19	      $q.notify({ type: 'negative', message });
20	      return undefined;
21	    } finally {
22	      const after = new Set(operatingIds.value);
23	      after.delete(id);
24	      operatingIds.value = after;
25	    }
26	  }
27	
28	  function isOperating(id: string): boolean {
29	    return operatingIds.value.has(id);
30	  }
31	
32	  return { operatingIds, execute, isOperating };
33	}
34	
```

```
1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { computed, ref, shallowRef, watch } from 'vue';
3	import { useQuasar } from 'quasar';
4	import { useRewriteRuntime } from '@/app/rewriteRuntime';
5	import DataTable from '@/widgets/DataTable.vue';
6	import FieldEditWidget from '@/widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue';
7	import SendTargetSelector from '@/features/send/components/SendTargetSelector.vue';
8	import { instanceColumns, type InstanceTableRow } from '@/features/send/components/instance-columns';
9	import { useSendInstances } from '@/features/send/composables/use-send-instances';
10	import { useFramePreview } from '@/features/send/composables/use-frame-preview';
11	import { useAsyncAction, useNotify } from '@/shared/composables';
12	import { listFrameAssetSummaries, cloneFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
13	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset, FrameAssetSummary } from '@/features/frame';
14	import type { SendFieldValue, SendFrameInstance } from '@/features/send';
15	
16	const $q = useQuasar();
17	const notify = useNotify();
18	const { execute: executeAction, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();
19	const runtime = useRewriteRuntime();
20	const frameService = runtime.features.frameService;
21	const sendService = runtime.features.sendService;
22	const connectionService = runtime.features.connectionService;
23	
24	const sendInstances = useSendInstances();
25	
26	// ===== Left column: frame list =====
27	
28	const searchText = ref('');
29	const favoriteOnly = ref(false);
30	
31	// Reactive refresh — service state is non-reactive, track mutations manually
32	const refreshKey = ref(0);
33	function refreshFrameList(): void {
34	  refreshKey.value++;
35	}
36	
37	const frameSummaries = computed<FrameAssetSummary[]>(() => {
38	  void refreshKey.value;
39	  return listFrameAssetSummaries(frameService.getSnapshot(), {
40	    direction: 'send',
41	    query: searchText.value || undefined,
42	    favoriteOnly: favoriteOnly.value || undefined,
43	  });
44	});
45	
46	const favoriteFrames = computed(() =>
47	  frameSummaries.value.filter((f) => f.isFavorite),
48	);
49	
50	const otherFrames = computed(() =>
51	  frameSummaries.value.filter((f) => !f.isFavorite),
52	);
53	
54	function getDefaultFieldValues(frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset): Record<string, SendFieldValue> {
55	  const values: Record<string, SendFieldValue> = {};
56	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
57	    if (!field.configurable) continue;
58	    if (field.defaultValue !== undefined) {
59	      const num = Number(field.defaultValue);
60	      values[field.id] = !Number.isNaN(num) && field.defaultValue.trim() !== '' ? num : field.defaultValue;
61	    } else if (field.options.length > 0) {
62	      const defaultOpt = field.options.find(o => o.isDefault);
63	      values[field.id] = defaultOpt ? Number(defaultOpt.value) || defaultOpt.value : Number(field.options[0].value) || field.options[0].value;
64	    }
65	  }
66	  return values;
67	}
68	
69	function onFrameClick(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
70	  const fullFrame = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
71	  if (!fullFrame) return;
72	  const defaultValues = getDefaultFieldValues(fullFrame);
73	  const instance = sendInstances.createInstance(frame.id, frame.name, defaultValues);
74	  sendInstances.selectInstance(instance.instanceId);
75	}
76	
77	function toggleFavorite(frame: FrameAssetSummary): void {
78	  const full = frameService.getFrame(frame.id);
79	  if (!full) return;
80	  const cloned = cloneFrameAsset(full);
81	  cloned.isFavorite = !cloned.isFavorite;
82	  const result = frameService.upsertFrame(cloned);
83	  if (result.ok) {
84	    refreshFrameList();
85	  } else {
86	    notify.error('操作失败');
87	  }
88	}
89	
90	// ===== Middle column: instance table =====
91	
92	const tableRows = shallowRef<InstanceTableRow[]>([]);
93	const selectedRows = shallowRef<InstanceTableRow[]>([]);
94	
95	watch(sendInstances.instances, (instances) => {
96	  tableRows.value = instances.map((inst, index) => ({
97	    instanceId: inst.instanceId,
98	    name: inst.name,
99	    fieldCount: Object.keys(inst.userFieldValues).length,
100	    sendCount: inst.sendCount,
101	    lastSendAt: inst.lastSendAt,
102	    description: inst.description,
103	    _index: index + 1,
104	  }));
105	}, { immediate: true });
106	
107	watch([tableRows, sendInstances.selectedInstanceId], ([rows, id]) => {
108	  selectedRows.value = id ? rows.filter((r) => r.instanceId === id) : [];
109	}, { immediate: true });
110	
111	function onRowClick(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
112	  sendInstances.selectInstance(row.instanceId);
113	}
114	
115	function onSelectionChange(selected: InstanceTableRow[]): void {
116	  if (selected.length > 0) {
117	    sendInstances.selectInstance(selected[0].instanceId);
118	  } else {
119	    sendInstances.selectInstance(null);
120	  }
121	}
122	
123	function onEditInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
124	  const inst = sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === row.instanceId);
125	  if (!inst) return;
126	  editingInstanceId.value = inst.instanceId;
127	  editValues.value = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inst.userFieldValues));
128	  editDescription.value = inst.description ?? '';
129	  editName.value = inst.name;

132	
133	function onCloneInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
134	  const cloned = sendInstances.cloneInstance(row.instanceId);
135	  if (cloned) {
136	    notify.success('已复制');
137	  }
138	}
139	
140	function onRemoveInstance(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
141	  $q.dialog({
142	    title: '确认删除',
143	    message: `确定要删除实例「${row.name}」吗？此操作不可撤销。`,
144	    cancel: true,
145	    persistent: false,
146	  }).onOk(() => {
147	    sendInstances.removeInstance(row.instanceId);
148	  });
149	}
150	
151	function onMoveUp(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
152	  sendInstances.moveInstanceUp(row.instanceId);
153	}
154	
155	function onMoveDown(row: InstanceTableRow): void {
156	  sendInstances.moveInstanceDown(row.instanceId);
157	}
158	
159	// ===== Right column: preview + send =====
160	
161	const selectedInstance = computed<SendFrameInstance | null>(() => {
162	  const id = sendInstances.selectedInstanceId.value;
163	  if (!id) return null;
164	  return sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === id) ?? null;
165	});
166	
167	const selectedFrame = computed<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>(() => {
168	  if (!selectedInstance.value) return null;
169	  return frameService.getFrame(selectedInstance.value.frameId) ?? null;
170	});
171	
172	const selectedValues = computed<Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>>(() => {
173	  return selectedInstance.value?.userFieldValues ?? {};
174	});
175	
176	const { fullPreview } = useFramePreview(selectedFrame, selectedValues);
177	
178	const selectedTargetId = ref<string | null>(null);
179	
180	async function onSend(): Promise<void> {
181	  const inst = selectedInstance.value;
182	  if (!inst || !selectedTargetId.value) return;
183	
184	  await executeAction('send', async () => {
185	    const result = await sendService.execute({
186	      frameId: inst.frameId,
187	      targetId: selectedTargetId.value!,
188	      userFieldValues: inst.userFieldValues,
189	      context: { source: 'user' },
190	    });
191	
192	    if (result.kind === 'sent') {
193	      notify.success('发送成功');
194	      sendInstances.incrementSendCount(inst.instanceId);
195	    } else {
196	      notify.error('发送失败', result.error?.message);
197	      if (result.buildIssues.length > 0) {
198	        const msgs = result.buildIssues
199	          .filter(i => i.severity === 'error')
200	          .map(i => i.message)
201	          .join('; ');
202	        if (msgs) {
203	          notify.error('构建问题', msgs);
204	        }
205	      }
206	    }
207	  });
208	}
209	
210	// ===== Instance edit dialog =====
211	
212	const showEditDialog = ref(false);
213	const editingInstanceId = ref<string | null>(null);
214	const editValues = ref<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>({});
215	const editDescription = ref('');
216	const editName = ref('');
217	const isSaving = ref(false);
218	
219	const isValid = computed(() => {
220	  return editName.value.trim().length > 0;
221	});
222	
223	function onEditDialogHide(): void {
224	  editingInstanceId.value = null;
225	  editValues.value = {};
226	  editDescription.value = '';
227	  editName.value = '';
228	}
229	
230	async function onEditConfirm(): Promise<void> {
231	  if (!editingInstanceId.value || !isValid.value) return;
232	
233	  isSaving.value = true;
234	  try {
235	    sendInstances.updateInstance(editingInstanceId.value, {
236	      name: editName.value,
237	      description: editDescription.value,
238	      userFieldValues: { ...editValues.value },
239	    });
240	    showEditDialog.value = false;
241	  } finally {
242	    isSaving.value = false;
243	  }
244	}
245	
246	// Configurable fields of selected frame for right panel display
247	const configurableFields = computed(() => {
248	  const frame = selectedFrame.value;
249	  if (!frame) return [];
250	  return frame.fields.filter(f => f.configurable);
251	});
252	
253	// Edit dialog: frame fields for the instance being edited
254	const editFrame = computed<ReadonlyFrameAsset | null>(() => {
255	  if (!editingInstanceId.value) return null;
256	  const inst = sendInstances.instances.value.find(i => i.instanceId === editingInstanceId.value);
257	  if (!inst) return null;
258	  return frameService.getFrame(inst.frameId) ?? null;
259	});
260	
261	const editFrameFields = computed(() => editFrame.value?.fields ?? []);
262	</script>
263	
264	<template>
265	  <q-page class="send-page">
266	    <div class="flex h-full">
267	      <!-- Left column: frame format list (240px) -->
268	      <div class="w-[240px] flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
269	        <div class="p-3">
270	          <q-input
271	            v-model="searchText"
272	            dense
273	            outlined
274	            placeholder="搜索..."
275	            clearable
276	          >
277	            <template #prepend>
278	              <q-icon name="o_search" size="xs" />
279	            </template>
280	          </q-input>
281	        </div>
282	
283	        <div class="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 pb-2">
284	          <q-btn
285	            flat
286	            dense
287	            no-caps
288	            size="sm"
289	            :icon="favoriteOnly ? 'o_star' : 'o_star_border'"
290	            :color="favoriteOnly ? 'warning' : 'grey'"
291	            @click="favoriteOnly = !favoriteOnly"
292	          />
293	          <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">仅收藏</span>
294	        </div>
295	
296	        <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
297	          <!-- Favorite section -->
298	          <template v-if="favoriteFrames.length > 0">
299	            <div class="px-3 pt-2 pb-1">
300	              <span class="rw-text-label text-xs">收藏</span>
301	            </div>
302	            <q-list dense>
303	              <q-item
304	                v-for="frame in favoriteFrames"
305	                :key="frame.id"
306	                clickable
307	                dense
308	                class="q-py-xs q-px-sm"
309	                @click="onFrameClick(frame)"
```

```
1	import { useQuasar } from 'quasar';
2	
3	export function useNotify() {
4	  const $q = useQuasar();
5	
6	  function success(message: string): void {
7	    $q.notify({ type: 'positive', message });
8	  }
9	
10	  function error(message: string, detail?: string): void {
11	    $q.notify({
12	      type: 'negative',
13	      message: detail ? `${message}: ${detail}` : message,
14	    });
15	  }
16	
17	  function info(message: string): void {
18	    $q.notify({ type: 'info', message });
19	  }
20	
21	  function warning(message: string): void {
22	    $q.notify({ type: 'warning', message });
23	  }
24	
25	  return { success, error, info, warning };
26	}
27
```

```
1	import { onUnmounted, ref } from 'vue';
2	
3	export function usePolling(fn: () => void, intervalMs: number) {
4	  let rafId = 0;
5	  let lastTime = 0;
6	  const disposed = ref(false);
7	
8	  function tick(now: number): void {
9	    if (disposed.value) return;
10	    if (now - lastTime >= intervalMs) {
11	      fn();
12	      lastTime = now;
13	    }
14	    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
15	  }
16	
17	  function start(): void {
18	    if (disposed.value) return;
19	    stop();
20	    lastTime = performance.now();
21	    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
22	  }
23	
24	  function stop(): void {
25	    if (rafId) {
26	      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
27	      rafId = 0;
28	    }
29	  }
30	
31	  function restart(): void {
32	    start();
33	  }
34	
35	  onUnmounted(() => {
36	    disposed.value = true;
37	    stop();
38	  });
39	
40	  return { start, stop, restart };
41	}
42	
```

```
1	import { ref } from 'vue';
2	
3	export function useStableKeys(prefix = 'key') {
4	  let uid = 0;
5	  const keys = ref<string[]>([]);
6	
7	  function syncKeys(items: readonly unknown[]): void {
8	    while (keys.value.length < items.length) {
9	      keys.value = [...keys.value, `${prefix}_${uid++}`];
10	    }
11	    if (keys.value.length > items.length) {
12	      keys.value = keys.value.slice(0, items.length);
13	    }
14	  }
15	
16	  return { keys, syncKeys };
17	}
18
```

```
1	import type { TaskLifecycleStatus, LifecycleAction } from './types';
2	
3	const VALID_TRANSITIONS: Record<TaskLifecycleStatus, readonly TaskLifecycleStatus[]> = {
4	  created: ['running'],
5	  running: ['paused', 'stopped', 'completed', 'failed'],
6	  paused: ['running', 'stopped'],
7	  stopped: [],
8	  completed: [],
9	  failed: [],
10	};
11	
12	const ACTION_TARGETS: Record<LifecycleAction, readonly { from: TaskLifecycleStatus; to: TaskLifecycleStatus }[]> = {
13	  start: [{ from: 'created', to: 'running' }],
14	  pause: [{ from: 'running', to: 'paused' }],
15	  resume: [{ from: 'paused', to: 'running' }],
16	  stop: [{ from: 'running', to: 'stopped' }, { from: 'paused', to: 'stopped' }],
17	  complete: [{ from: 'running', to: 'completed' }],
18	  fail: [{ from: 'running', to: 'failed' }],
19	};
20	
21	export function canTransition(from: TaskLifecycleStatus, to: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean {
22	  return VALID_TRANSITIONS[from].includes(to);
23	}
24	
25	export function transition(state: TaskLifecycleStatus, action: LifecycleAction): TaskLifecycleStatus {
26	  const targets = ACTION_TARGETS[action];
27	  const match = targets.find((t) => t.from === state);
28	  if (!match) {
29	    throw new Error(`Invalid lifecycle transition: cannot '${action}' from '${state}'`);
30	  }
31	  return match.to;
32	}
33	
34	export function isTerminal(status: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean {
35	  return status === 'stopped' || status === 'completed' || status === 'failed';
36	}
37
```

```
1	import type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepResult } from './types';
2	import { resolveStopCondition } from './types';
3	
4	export function calculateProgress(instance: TaskInstanceState): TaskProgress {
5	  const stepsPerIteration = instance.definitionRef.steps.length;
6	  const { currentIteration, stepResults } = instance;
7	
8	  const currentIterResults = stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIteration);
9	
10	  let stepsCompleted = 0;
11	  let stepsFailed = 0;
12	  let stepsSkipped = 0;
13	
14	  for (const result of currentIterResults) {
15	    if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {
16	      stepsSkipped++;
17	    } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {
18	      stepsFailed++;
19	    } else {
20	      stepsCompleted++;
21	    }
22	  }
23	
24	  const iterationsCompleted = countCompletedIterations(instance, stepsPerIteration);
25	  const iterationsTotal = resolveIterationsTotal(instance);
26	  const elapsedMs = calculateElapsedMs(instance);
27	
28	  const estimatedRemainingMs = estimateRemainingMs(instance, iterationsCompleted, iterationsTotal, elapsedMs);
29	  const lastStepResult = stepResults.length > 0 ? stepResults[stepResults.length - 1] : null;
30	
31	  return {
32	    stepsTotal: stepsPerIteration,
33	    stepsCompleted,
34	    stepsFailed,
35	    stepsSkipped,
36	    iterationsCompleted,
37	    iterationsTotal,
38	    elapsedMs,
39	    estimatedRemainingMs,
40	    lastStepResult,
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	export function isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {
45	  switch (result.kind) {
46	    case 'send':
47	      return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
48	    case 'wait-condition':
49	      return !result.matched && result.timedOut;
50	    case 'delay':
51	      return !result.completed;
52	  }
53	}
54	
55	function countCompletedIterations(instance: TaskInstanceState, stepsPerIteration: number): number {
56	  const iterationIndices = new Set(instance.stepResults.map((r) => r.iteration));
57	  let count = 0;
58	  for (const iter of iterationIndices) {
59	    const iterResults = instance.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === iter);
60	    if (iterResults.length >= stepsPerIteration) {
61	      count++;
62	    }
63	  }
64	  return count;
65	}
66	
67	function resolveIterationsTotal(instance: TaskInstanceState): number | null {
68	  const resolved = resolveStopCondition(instance.definitionRef);
69	  const { schedule } = instance.definitionRef;
70	
71	  if (resolved.maxIterations !== undefined) {
72	    return resolved.maxIterations;
73	  }
74	  if (schedule.kind === 'immediate') {
75	    return 1;
76	  }
77	  return null;
78	}
79	
80	function calculateElapsedMs(instance: TaskInstanceState): number {
81	  if (!instance.startedAt) return 0;
82	  const start = new Date(instance.startedAt).getTime();
83	  const endRef = instance.completedAt ?? instance.stoppedAt ?? instance.failedAt;
84	  const end = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();
85	  return Math.max(0, end - start);
86	}
87	
88	function estimateRemainingMs(
89	  instance: TaskInstanceState,
90	  iterationsCompleted: number,
91	  iterationsTotal: number | null,
92	  elapsedMs: number,
93	): number | null {
94	  if (instance.definitionRef.schedule.kind !== 'timer' || iterationsTotal === null) {
95	    return null;
96	  }
97	  if (iterationsCompleted <= 0 || iterationsTotal <= iterationsCompleted) {
98	    return null;
99	  }
100	  const avgIterMs = elapsedMs / iterationsCompleted;
101	  return Math.round(avgIterMs * (iterationsTotal - iterationsCompleted));
102	}
103	
```

```
 10 │ import {
 11 │   createStorageLocalReader,

 13 │ } from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
 14 │ import {
 15 │   createConnectionService,
 16 │   type ConnectionService,
 17 │   type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
 18 │ } from '@/features/connection';
 19 │ import {
 20 │   createReceiveService,
 21 │   type ReceiveService,
 22 │ } from '@/features/receive';
 23 │ import {
 24 │   createSendService,
 25 │   type SendService,
 26 │ } from '@/features/send';
 27 │ import {
 28 │   createTaskService,
 29 │   type TaskService,
 30 │ } from '@/features/task';
 31 │ import { ConnectionBackedSendWriter } from './bridges/connection-backed-writer';
 32 │ import { ConnectionBackedTargetResolver } from './bridges/connection-backed-target-resolver';
 33 │ import { ReceiveEventSourceBridge } from './bridges/receive-event-source-bridge';
 34 │ 
 35 │ export interface RewriteWiredFeatures {
 36 │   readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
 37 │   readonly frameService: FrameAssetService;
 38 │   readonly settingsService: SettingsService;
 39 │   readonly storageReader: StorageLocalReader;
 40 │   readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
 41 │   readonly receiveService: ReceiveService;
 42 │   readonly sendService: SendService;
 43 │   readonly taskService: TaskService;
 44 │   readonly receiveEventSourceBridge: ReceiveEventSourceBridge;
 45 │ }
 46 │ 
 47 │ export interface WireFeaturesOptions {
 48 │   readonly connectionAdapter: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
 49 │ }
 50 │ 
 51 │ function createDefaultFrameService(): FrameAssetService {
 52 │   return createFrameAssetService();
 53 │ }
 54 │ 
 55 │ function createDefaultStorageReader(): StorageLocalReader {
 56 │   return createStorageLocalReader(() => ({
 57 │     records: [],
 58 │     historyMaterials: [],
 59 │     csvMaterials: [],
 60 │     legacyMaterials: [],
 61 │   }));
 62 │ }
 63 │ 
 64 │ export function wireFeatures(
 65 │   options: WireFeaturesOptions,
 66 │ ): RewriteWiredFeatures {
 67 │   // L0: no cross-dependencies
 68 │   const frameService = createDefaultFrameService();
 69 │   const frameReader = frameService;
 70 │   const settingsService = createSettingsService();
 71 │   const storageReader = createDefaultStorageReader();
 72 │ 
 73 │   // L1: needs adapter
 74 │   const connectionService = createConnectionService({
 75 │     adapter: options.connectionAdapter,
 76 │   });
 77 │ 
 78 │   // L2: needs L0 + L1
 79 │   const receiveService = createReceiveService({ frameReader });
 80 │ 
 81 │   const sendWriter = new ConnectionBackedSendWriter(connectionService);
 82 │   const targetResolver = new ConnectionBackedTargetResolver(connectionService);
 83 │   const sendService = createSendService({
 84 │     frameReader,
 85 │     targetResolver,
 86 │     transportWriter: sendWriter,
 87 │   });
 88 │ 
 89 │   // L3: needs L2
 90 │   const receiveEventSourceBridge = new ReceiveEventSourceBridge();
 91 │   const taskService = createTaskService({
 92 │     sendService,
 93 │     receiveEventSource: receiveEventSourceBridge,
 94 │   });
 95 │ 
 96 │   return {
 97 │     frameReader,
 98 │     frameService,
 99 │     settingsService,
100 │     storageReader,
101 │     connectionService,
102 │     receiveService,
103 │     sendService,
104 │     taskService,
105 │     receiveEventSourceBridge,
106 │   };
107 │ }
```

```
35 │ function readCssPixelToken(name: string): number | undefined {
36 │   if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
37 │     return undefined;
38 │   }
39 │ 
40 │   const rawValue = window.getComputedStyle(window.document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name);
41 │   const parsedValue = Number.parseFloat(rawValue);
42 │ 
43 │   return Number.isFinite(parsedValue) ? parsedValue : undefined;
44 │ }
45 │
```

```
  1 │ import {
  2 │   type FrameAssetReader,
  3 │ } from '@/features/frame';
  4 │ import {
  5 │   type SettingsOperationResult,
  6 │   type SettingsResetScope,
  7 │   type SettingsService,
  8 │ } from '@/features/settings';
  9 │ import {
 10 │   type StorageLocalReader,
 11 │ } from '@/features/storage-local-baseline';
 12 │ import {
 13 │   type ConnectionTransportAdapter,
 14 │ } from '@/features/connection';
 15 │ import { wireFeatures, type RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
 16 │ import { routingTick, type RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
 17 │ 
 18 │ export type { RoutingTickResult } from './routing-tick';
 19 │ export type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
 20 │ 
 21 │ export interface RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot {
 22 │   readonly frame: {
 23 │     readonly totalFrames: number;
 24 │     readonly totalFields: number;
 25 │     readonly selectedFrameName: string | null;
 26 │   };
 27 │   readonly settings: {
 28 │     readonly autoStartRecording: boolean;
 29 │     readonly csvDefaultOutputPath: string;
 30 │     readonly csvSaveIntervalMinutes: number;
 31 │   };
 32 │   readonly storage: {
 33 │     readonly localRecordCount: number;
 34 │     readonly historyHourCount: number;
 35 │     readonly csvMaterialCount: number;
 36 │     readonly legacyMaterialCount: number;
 37 │     readonly lastIssue: {
 38 │       readonly code: string;
 39 │       readonly message: string;
 40 │     } | null;
 41 │   };
 42 │ }
 43 │ 
 44 │ export interface RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
 45 │   readonly ok: boolean;
 46 │   readonly issues: readonly {
 47 │     readonly severity: string;
 48 │     readonly code: string;
 49 │     readonly message: string;
 50 │   }[];
 51 │ }
 52 │ 
 53 │ type FrameOverviewPort = Pick<FrameAssetReader, 'getSelectedFrame' | 'listFrames'>;
 54 │ type SettingsOverviewPort = Pick<SettingsService, 'getRecordingSettings' | 'reset'>;
 55 │ type StorageOverviewPort = Pick<
 56 │   StorageLocalReader,
 57 │   | 'getLastIssue'
 58 │   | 'listCsvMaterials'
 59 │   | 'listHistoryHours'
 60 │   | 'listLegacyMaterials'
 61 │   | 'listLocalRecords'
 62 │ >;
 63 │ 
 64 │ export const ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
 65 │ 
 66 │ export interface RewriteRuntimeDependencies {
 67 │   readonly connectionAdapter?: ConnectionTransportAdapter;
 68 │   readonly frameReader?: FrameOverviewPort;
 69 │   readonly settingsService?: SettingsOverviewPort;
 70 │   readonly storageReader?: StorageOverviewPort;
 71 │ }
 72 │ 
 73 │ export interface RewriteRuntime {
 74 │   getOverviewSnapshot(): RewriteRuntimeOverviewSnapshot;
 75 │   resetSettings(scope?: SettingsResetScope): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult;
 76 │   readonly features: RewriteWiredFeatures;
 77 │   routingTick(): Promise<RoutingTickResult>;
 78 │   startTickDriver(intervalMs?: number): void;
 79 │   stopTickDriver(): void;
 80 │   readonly isTickDriverRunning: boolean;
 81 │   destroy(): void;
 82 │ }
 83 │ 
 84 │ function createNoOpConnectionAdapter(): ConnectionTransportAdapter {
 85 │   const accepted = { ok: true as const, events: [] as const };
 86 │   return {
 87 │     connect: async () => accepted,
 88 │     disconnect: async () => accepted,
 89 │     write: async () => accepted,
 90 │     cleanup: async () => accepted,
 91 │     drainEvents: async () => [] as const,
 92 │   };
 93 │ }
 94 │ 
 95 │ function toRuntimeCommandResult(result: SettingsOperationResult): RewriteRuntimeCommandResult {
 96 │   return {
 97 │     ok: result.ok,
 98 │     issues: result.validation.issues.map((issue) => ({
 99 │       severity: issue.severity,
100 │       code: issue.code,
101 │       message: issue.message,
102 │     })),
103 │   };
104 │ }
105 │ 
106 │ export function createRewriteRuntime(
107 │   dependencies: RewriteRuntimeDependencies = {},
108 │ ): RewriteRuntime {
109 │   const adapter = dependencies.connectionAdapter ?? createNoOpConnectionAdapter();
110 │   const wiredFeatures = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
111 │ 
112 │   const frameReader = dependencies.frameReader ?? wiredFeatures.frameReader;
113 │   const settingsService = dependencies.settingsService ?? wiredFeatures.settingsService;
114 │   const storageReader = dependencies.storageReader ?? wiredFeatures.storageReader;
115 │ 
116 │   let destroyed = false;
117 │   let tickIntervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
118 │ 
119 │   function stopTick(): void {
120 │     if (tickIntervalId !== null) {
121 │       clearInterval(tickIntervalId);
122 │       tickIntervalId = null;
123 │     }
124 │   }
125 │ 
126 │   return {
127 │     features: wiredFeatures,
128 │ 
129 │     getOverviewSnapshot() {
130 │       const frameSummaries = frameReader.listFrames();
131 │       const selectedFrame = frameReader.getSelectedFrame();
132 │       const recordingSettings = settingsService.getRecordingSettings();
133 │       const storageIssue = storageReader.getLastIssue();
134 │ 
135 │       return {
136 │         frame: {
137 │           totalFrames: frameSummaries.length,
138 │           totalFields: frameSummaries.reduce((sum, frame) => sum + frame.fieldCount, 0),
139 │           selectedFrameName: selectedFrame?.name ?? null,
140 │         },
141 │         settings: {
142 │           autoStartRecording: recordingSettings.autoStartRecording,
143 │           csvDefaultOutputPath: recordingSettings.csvDefaultOutputPath,
144 │           csvSaveIntervalMinutes: recordingSettings.csvSaveIntervalMinutes,
145 │         },
146 │         storage: {
147 │           localRecordCount: storageReader.listLocalRecords().length,
148 │           historyHourCount: storageReader.listHistoryHours().length,
149 │           csvMaterialCount: storageReader.listCsvMaterials().length,
150 │           legacyMaterialCount: storageReader.listLegacyMaterials().length,
151 │           lastIssue: storageIssue
152 │             ? {
153 │                 code: storageIssue.code,
154 │                 message: storageIssue.message,
155 │               }
156 │             : null,
157 │         },
158 │       };
159 │     },
160 │ 
161 │     resetSettings(scope = 'all') {
162 │       return toRuntimeCommandResult(settingsService.reset(scope));
163 │     },
164 │ 
165 │     routingTick() {
166 │       return routingTick(wiredFeatures);
167 │     },
168 │ 
169 │     startTickDriver(intervalMs = ROUTING_TICK_DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS) {
170 │       if (tickIntervalId !== null || destroyed) return;
171 │       tickIntervalId = setInterval(() => {
172 │         routingTick(wiredFeatures).catch((err) => console.error('[routingTick]', err));
173 │       }, intervalMs);
174 │     },
175 │ 
176 │     stopTickDriver() {
177 │       stopTick();
178 │     },
179 │ 
180 │     get isTickDriverRunning() {
181 │       return tickIntervalId !== null;
182 │     },
183 │ 
184 │     destroy() {
185 │       if (destroyed) return;
186 │       destroyed = true;
187 │       stopTick();
188 │       wiredFeatures.connectionService.cleanup();
189 │       wiredFeatures.receiveEventSourceBridge.clear();
190 │     },
191 │   };
192 │ }
```

```
 14 │ ## 页面 1: 帧发送页 (/frames/send)
 15 │ 
 16 │ ### 现状
 17 │ 
 18 │ - Send feature 有 SendService.execute + SendReader + selector，无帧实例管理能力
 19 │ - buildFrame / resolveFieldValues / evaluateFieldExpressions 等纯函数存在于 send/core 但未 re-export

 21 │ - 旧系统发送页包含定时/触发/顺序发送和任务监控，全部与发送页耦合

 25 │ **D-S1 布局**: 模式B 三栏（左 240px + 中 flex:1 + 右 300px）
 26 │ 
 27 │ **D-S2 左栏帧格式列表**:
 28 │ - QList + QItem，分组标题（收藏/全部）
 29 │ - QInput dense 搜索，实时过滤（纯内存 < 1ms，无 debounce）
 30 │ - 数据源: `listFrameAssetSummaries(source, { direction: 'send', query, favoriteOnly? })`
 31 │ - 单击 → 创建实例 + 中栏选中 + 右栏预览
 32 │ - 双击 → 创建实例 + 打开编辑对话框
 33 │ - 收藏按钮: toggle isFavorite → replaceFrames
 34 │ 
 35 │ **D-S3 中栏帧实例表**:
 36 │ - DataTable Widget + virtual-scroll + selection="single"
 37 │ - 列定义抽到 `send/components/instance-columns.ts`:
 38 │ 
 39 │ | 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
 40 │ |----|------|------|------|
 41 │ | # | index | fixed 60px | 自动序号 |
 42 │ | 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
 43 │ | 参数数 | fieldCount | fixed 80px | 数字 |
 44 │ | 发送次数 | sendCount | fixed 80px | 数字(V1) |
 45 │ | 上次发送 | lastSendAt | fixed 140px | 时间格式化(V2) |
```

 14 │  14 │ ## 页面 1: 帧发送页 (/frames/send)
 15 │  15 │ 
 16 │  16 │ ### 现状
 17 │  17 │ 
 18 │  18 │ - Send feature 有 SendService.execute + SendReader + selector，无帧实例管理能力
 19 │  19 │ - buildFrame / resolveFieldValues / evaluateFieldExpressions 等纯函数存在于 send/core 但未 re-export
 20 │  20 │ - 无 UI 侧帧实例类型，无预览 composable
 21 │  21 │ - 旧系统发送页包含定时/触发/顺序发送和任务监控，全部与发送页耦合
 22 │  22 │ 
 23 │  23 │ ### 变化
 24 │  24 │ 
 25 │  25 │ **D-S1 布局**: 模式B 三栏（左 240px + 中 flex:1 + 右 300px）
 26 │  26 │ 
 27 │  27 │ **D-S2 左栏帧格式列表**:
 28 │  28 │ - QList + QItem，分组标题（收藏/全部）
 29 │  29 │ - QInput dense 搜索，实时过滤（纯内存 < 1ms，无 debounce）
 30 │  30 │ - 数据源: `listFrameAssetSummaries(source, { direction: 'send', query, favoriteOnly? })`
 31 │  31 │ - 单击 → 创建实例 + 中栏选中 + 右栏预览
 32 │  32 │ - 双击 → 创建实例 + 打开编辑对话框
 33 │  33 │ - 收藏按钮: toggle isFavorite → replaceFrames
 34 │  34 │ 
 35 │  35 │ **D-S3 中栏帧实例表**:
 36 │  36 │ - DataTable Widget + virtual-scroll + selection="single"
 37 │  37 │ - 列定义抽到 `send/components/instance-columns.ts`:
 38 │  38 │ 
 39 │  39 │ | 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
 40 │  40 │ |----|------|------|------|
 41 │  41 │ | # | index | fixed 60px | 自动序号 |
 42 │  42 │ | 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
 43 │  43 │ | 参数数 | fieldCount | fixed 80px | 数字 |
 44 │  44 │ | 发送次数 | sendCount | fixed 80px | 数字(V1) |
 45 │  45 │ | 上次发送 | lastSendAt | fixed 140px | 时间格式化(V2) |
 46 │  46 │ | 备注 | description | auto | 文本，长文本截断+QTooltip(V4) |
 47 │  47 │ | 操作 | — | fixed 120px | icon 按钮(编辑/复制/删除) |
 48 │  48 │ 
 49 │  49 │ - 空状态: `#no-data` → "暂无帧实例，从左侧选择帧格式创建"
 50 │  50 │ - loading: `:loading="isLoading"`
 51 │  51 │ - 排序: 上下移动按钮（本期不做拖拽）
 52 │  52 │ - 批量模式: selection="multiple" + 批量删除($q.dialog 确认)
 53 │  53 │ - 数据源: useSendInstances composable
 54 │  54 │ 
 55 │  55 │ **D-S4 右栏预览 + 发送**:
 56 │  56 │ - 上部: 实例基本信息（名称/时间/备注）
 57 │  57 │ - 中部: 十六进制帧（pre code font-mono，按字节空格分隔）
 58 │  58 │ - 下部: 可配置字段表（字段名 | 十六进制值）
 59 │  59 │ - 底部: SendTargetSelector + [发送] 按钮 `:loading="isSending" :disable="!selectedInstance || !selectedTarget"`(F4)
 60 │  60 │ - 数据源: useFramePreview composable
 61 │  61 │ 
 62 │  62 │ **D-S5 SendFrameInstance 类型**（归口 send/core/types.ts）:
 63 │  63 │ 
 64 │  64 │ ```typescript
 65 │  65 │ interface SendFrameInstance {
 66 │  66 │   readonly instanceId: string;        // nanoid
 67 │  67 │   readonly frameId: string;
 68 │  68 │   readonly name: string;
 69 │  69 │   readonly description?: string;
 70 │  70 │   readonly userFieldValues: Record<string, SendFieldValue>;
 71 │  71 │   readonly sendCount: number;
 72 │  72 │   readonly lastSendAt?: string;       // ISO timestamp
 73 │  73 │   readonly createdAt: string;
 74 │  74 │   readonly updatedAt: string;
 75 │  75 │ }
 76 │  76 │ ```
 77 │  77 │ 
 78 │  78 │ 不进 core/service 层——帧实例是页面级 UI 状态，不是全局领域状态。
 79 │  79 │ 
 80 │  80 │ **D-S6 单帧发送**: 选中实例 + 选择目标 → `sendService.execute({ frameId, targetId, userFieldValues, context: { source: 'user' } })`。成功 $q.notify('positive')，失败 $q.notify('negative') + buildIssues。
 81 │  81 │ 
 82 │  82 │ **C9 说明**: `SendRequest.variables` 字段在实际代码中不存在。外部变量通过 service 创建时注入的 `variableProvider` 获取，`resolveFieldValues` 内部合并 provider 变量和调用方传入的 variables 参数。UI 发送时不需传递 variables——service 内部自动从 variableProvider 获取。`SendRequest` 同时接受 `fieldValues` 和 `userFieldValues`，service 内部 `userFieldValues ?? fieldValues`。UI 应统一使用 `userFieldValues`。
 83 │  83 │ 
 84 │  84 │ **D-S7 实例编辑对话框**:
 85 │  85 │ - QDialog v-model + @hide 清理(D1)
 86 │  86 │ - 宽度: `rw-dialog-lg`(D2)
 87 │  87 │ - 使用 FieldEditWidget 组件（见跨页面共享）
 88 │  88 │ - [取消] [确认] 按钮，确认按钮 `:loading="isSaving" :disable="!isValid"`(F4)
 89 │  89 │ 
 90 │  90 │ **D-S8 发送页定位**: 只做单帧快速发送 + 帧实例管理。定时/触发/顺序/监控全部在任务页。
 91 │  91 │ 
 92 │  92 │ **D-S9 已移除**: 定时/触发/顺序发送对话框、任务监控对话框、拖拽排序（简化为上下移动按钮）、批量发送 API。

 96 │  96 │ ## 页面 2: 任务管理页 (/tasks)
 97 │  97 │ 
 98 │  98 │ ### 现状
 99 │  99 │ 
100 │ 100 │ - Task feature 有完整的 TaskService + Reader + selector
101 │ 101 │ - 无任务编辑/创建 UI 能力
102 │ 102 │ - 无任务历史查看能力
103 │ 103 │ - 无 validateTaskDefinition / serialization / builders
104 │ 104 │ - 旧系统任务作为发送页对话框，与全局规划中 task = 通用执行引擎的定位冲突
105 │ 105 │ 
106 │ 106 │ ### 变化
107 │ 107 │ 
108 │ 108 │ **D-T1 定位**: 任务是通用执行引擎的完整管理界面，独立于发送页。
109 │ 109 │ 
110 │ 110 │ **D-T2 布局**: 主从双栏 — 左侧任务列表(flex:1) + 右侧面板(360px)
111 │ 111 │ 
112 │ 112 │ 右侧面板只承载**执行监控和任务摘要**（360px 足够）。
113 │ 113 │ 任务编辑器因表单复杂度（4 种步骤 + 3 种调度 + 高级配置）使用**全宽对话框**（rw-dialog-xl）。
114 │ 114 │ 
115 │ 115 │ ```
116 │ 116 │ ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
117 │ 117 │ │ 任务管理  [新建任务] [导入] [导出]                              │
118 │ 118 │ ├────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┤
119 │ 119 │ │ TableToolbar(搜索+筛选) │ 未选中: 空状态                       │
120 │ 120 │ │                        │                                     │
121 │ 121 │ │ Tab: 活动任务|历史记录   │ 定义态: 任务摘要 + [编辑][启动]       │
122 │ 122 │ │ DataTable              │ 运行态: 执行监控(TaskExecutionDetail)│
123 │ 123 │ │                        │                                     │
124 │ 124 │ │ QChip 状态筛选          │                                     │
125 │ 125 │ └────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────┘
126 │ 126 │ 
127 │ 127 │ [新建任务]/[编辑] → QDialog rw-dialog-xl → 任务编辑器对话框
128 │ 128 │ ```
129 │ 129 │ 
130 │ 130 │ **D-T3 任务列表区**:
131 │ 131 │ - Tab 切换: 活动任务 | 历史记录（keep-alive）
132 │ 132 │ - DataTable + virtual-scroll（防御性开启）
133 │ 133 │ - 活动任务列定义抽到 `task/components/task-columns.ts`:
134 │ 134 │ 
135 │ 135 │ | 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
136 │ 136 │ |----|------|------|------|
137 │ 137 │ | 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
138 │ 138 │ | 调度类型 | schedule.kind | fixed 100px | QChip(immediate/timer/event) |
139 │ 139 │ | 状态 | status | fixed 100px | StatusBadge |
140 │ 140 │ | 进度 | progress | fixed 120px | QLinearProgress + 百分比 |
141 │ 141 │ | 操作 | — | fixed 140px | icon 按钮(暂停/恢复/停止) |
142 │ 142 │ 
143 │ 143 │ - 历史记录列定义:
144 │ 144 │ 
145 │ 145 │ | 列 | 字段 | 宽度 | 渲染 |
146 │ 146 │ |----|------|------|------|
147 │ 147 │ | 名称 | name | auto | 文本 |
148 │ 148 │ | 调度类型 | schedule.kind | fixed 100px | QChip |
149 │ 149 │ | 结果 | result | fixed 80px | StatusBadge(positive 已完成/negative 失败) |
150 │ 150 │ | 耗时 | elapsedMs | fixed 100px | 时间格式化(V2) |
151 │ 151 │ | 完成时间 | completedAt | fixed 140px | 时间格式化(V2) |
152 │ 152 │ | 操作 | — | fixed 120px | icon 按钮(查看/重新执行/删除) |
153 │ 153 │ 
154 │ 154 │ - 空状态: `#no-data` → 活动 Tab "暂无活动任务"，历史 Tab "暂无历史记录"
155 │ 155 │ - loading: `:loading="isLoading"`
156 │ 156 │ - 状态筛选 QChip 组: 全部/待启动/运行中/已暂停/已完成/失败
157 │ 157 │ - 数据源: selectActiveInstances + selectTaskHistory
158 │ 158 │ - **历史记录数据流**: task state 已有 `instances`（活跃）+ `history`（removeTask 归档）两层数据。task feature 新增 `selectActiveInstances` selector，只返回非终止态实例（created/running/paused）。`selectTaskHistory` 扩展为返回 history + 仍在 instances 中的终止态实例（completed/failed/stopped）。UI composable 直接调两个 selector，不做任何过滤。
159 │ 159 │ - 操作: 活动 Tab 中终止态任务显示 [移至历史] 按钮 → 调 removeTask → 从 active 消失、入 history
160 │ 160 │ - 合并 useTaskList + useTaskHistory → 单一 useTaskList composable，内部按 mode('active' | 'history') 切换 selector
161 │ 161 │ 
162 │ 162 │ **D-T4 任务摘要（右侧面板，定义态）**:
163 │ 163 │ - 任务名称 + 调度类型摘要 + 步骤数量
164 │ 164 │ - 可变参数摘要（有/无）
165 │ 165 │ - [编辑] 按钮 → 打开任务编辑器对话框(rw-dialog-xl)
166 │ 166 │ - [启动] 按钮 `:loading="isStarting" :disable="lifecycle !== 'created'"`(F4)
167 │ 167 │ 
168 │ 168 │ **D-T5 任务编辑器对话框**:
169 │ 169 │ - QDialog v-model + @hide 清理(D1)
170 │ 170 │ - 宽度: `rw-dialog-xl`(D2)
171 │ 171 │ - 基本信息: 任务名称(QInput `:rules="[val => !!val || '请输入任务名称']"`(F1/F2)) + 调度类型(QSelect: immediate/timer/event)
172 │ 172 │ - 调度配置: 按 schedule.kind 动态渲染
173 │ 173 │   - immediate: 无额外配置项
174 │ 174 │   - timer: 间隔(QInput type=number `:rules="[val => val > 0 || '间隔必须大于0']"`) + 次数归口 TaskStopCondition.maxIterations + 无限循环(QToggle)
175 │ 175 │   - event: 条件编辑器（ConditionTerm 列表 + 添加按钮，至少一条校验）
176 │ 176 │ - 步骤编辑器: 步骤列表 + [添加步骤] 下拉(send/wait-condition/delay)
177 │ 177 │   - 每步: QExpansionItem 展开/折叠 + 类型图标 + 摘要 + 删除按钮
178 │ 178 │   - SendStep: FrameSelector(required) + SendTargetSelector(required) + FieldEditWidget(独立 userFieldValues) + 发送后延时(QInput type=number) + 可选 repeat 配置
179 │ 179 │   - WaitConditionStep: 条件列表(至少一条) + 超时(QInput type=number `:rules="[val => val > 0 || '请输入超时时间']"`) + 超时策略(QSelect: continue/skip/fail)
180 │ 180 │   - DelayStep: 持续时间(QInput type=number `:rules="[val => val > 0 || '请输入延时时间']"`)
181 │ 181 │   - 字段数 > 20 时 SendStep 的 FieldEditWidget 必须拆子组件(F3)
182 │ 182 │ - 高级配置（QExpansionItem 折叠）:
183 │ 183 │   - 停止条件: maxIterations / maxDurationMs
184 │ 184 │   - 错误策略: onFailure(QSelect) / retryCount / retryDelayMs
185 │ 185 │   - ~~可变参数: FieldVariation 编辑器（字段选择 + 值列表）~~ → Phase 2（类型已就绪，UI 编辑器待实现）
186 │ 186 │   - ~~exitCondition~~ → Phase 2（类型已就绪，UI 编辑器待实现）
187 │ 187 │   - ~~step repeat 配置~~ → Phase 2（类型已就绪，UI 编辑器待实现）
188 │ 188 │ - 操作: [取消] [保存] [保存并启动]
189 │ 189 │   - [保存] `:loading="isSaving" :disable="!isValid"`(F4)
190 │ 190 │   - [保存并启动] `:loading="isSaving"`
191 │ 191 │ - 保存前 validateTaskDefinition 校验
192 │ 192 │ 
193 │ 193 │ **D-T6 执行监控（右侧面板，运行态）**:
194 │ 194 │ - 复用 TaskExecutionDetail 基础组件（widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue）
195 │ 195 │ - 任务状态: StatusBadge + 名称
196 │ 196 │ - 进度: QLinearProgress + 文字(stepsCompleted/stepsTotal)
197 │ 197 │ - 耗时: elapsedMs 格式化(V2)
198 │ 198 │ - 步骤状态列表: 每步 StatusBadge + 结果摘要
199 │ 199 │ - 操作: 暂停/恢复/停止（停止需 $q.dialog() 确认）
200 │ 200 │ - 错误: TaskInstanceState.error 内联展示
201 │ 201 │ 
202 │ 202 │ **D-T7 任务状态映射**:
203 │ 203 │ 
204 │ 204 │ | TaskLifecycleStatus | UI 显示 | StatusBadge color(C2) |
205 │ 205 │ |---------------------|---------|----------------------|
206 │ 206 │ | created | 待启动 | grey |
207 │ 207 │ | running | 运行中 | positive |
208 │ 208 │ | running + schedule.kind='event' | 等待触发 | positive (副标签) |
209 │ 209 │ | running + schedule.kind='timer' | 等待调度 | positive (副标签) |
210 │ 210 │ | paused | 已暂停 | warning |
211 │ 211 │ | completed | 已完成 | positive |
212 │ 212 │ | failed | 失败 | negative |
213 │ 213 │ 
214 │ 214 │ waiting-trigger/waiting-schedule 由 UI 层从 `instance.definitionRef.schedule.kind` + lifecycle 推导，不改 core。
215 │ 215 │ 
216 │ 216 │ **D-T8 SendStep 字段值**: 方案 A（copy-on-create）。每步独立持有 userFieldValues，不引用发送页实例。提供"复制上一步"按钮降低重复配置成本。
217 │ 217 │ 
218 │ 218 │ **D-T9 导入导出**: serializeTaskDefinition → JSON 文件写入(platform facade)；读取 → deserializeTaskDefinition → 校验 → 创建。
219 │ 219 │ 
220 │ 220 │ **D-T10 TaskService 方法签名**:
221 │ 221 │ 
222 │ 222 │ ```typescript
223 │ 223 │ // 已实现
224 │ 224 │ createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState
225 │ 225 │ startTask(instanceId: string): void
226 │ 226 │ pauseTask(instanceId: string): void
227 │ 227 │ resumeTask(instanceId: string): void
228 │ 228 │ stopTask(instanceId: string): void
229 │ 229 │ removeTask(instanceId: string): void

231 │ 231 │ stopAll(): number

235 │ 235 │ validateTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): TaskValidationIssue[]
236 │ 236 │ serializeTaskDefinition(def: TaskDefinition): string
237 │ 237 │ deserializeTaskDefinition(json: string): TaskDefinition
238 │ 238 │ ```
239 │ 239 │ 
240 │ 240 │ **D-T11 Service Readiness Gaps**（UI 实施前需补齐）:
241 │ 241 │ 
242 │ 242 │ | Gap | 影响 | 修复方式 |
243 │ 243 │ |-----|------|---------|
244 │ 244 │ | 无 `updateTask` 方法 | 编辑任务无法保存 | 新增 `updateTask(instanceId, definition)`：仅 `created` 态可改定义 |
245 │ 245 │ | 历史记录缺 `elapsedMs` / `completedAt` | D-T3 历史列无法渲染 | selector 层从 `startedAt`/`stoppedAt`/`completedAt`/`failedAt` 派生 |
246 │ 246 │ | `stopAll()` 无返回值 | UI 无法显示停止了多少 | 改为返回 `stoppedCount: number` |
247 │ 247 │ 
248 │ 248 │ D-T5 编辑器保存逻辑：`created` 态 → `updateTask` → 刷新面板；`running/paused` 态 → 禁用编辑。

```
18 │   set: (val) => emit('update:modelValue', val),
19 │ });
20 │ 
21 │ function refreshTargets(): void {
22 │   targets.value = props.connectionService.listTransportTargets();
23 │ }
24 │ 
25 │ // Refresh targets on mount and when connection state might change
26 │ watch(() => props.connectionService, () => {
27 │   refreshTargets();
28 │ }, { immediate: true });
29 │ 
30 │ const targetOptions = computed(() =>
31 │   targets.value.map((t) => ({
32 │     value: t.targetId,
33 │     label: `${t.label} (${t.kind})`,
34 │     disable: !t.available,
35 │   })),
36 │ );
37 │ </script>
38 │ 
39 │ <template>
40 │   <q-select
41 │     v-model="selectedTargetId"
42 │     :options="targetOptions"
43 │     :disable="disable"
44 │     outlined
45 │     dense
46 │     emit-value
47 │     map-options
48 │     clearable
49 │     placeholder="选择发送目标"
50 │     class="w-full"
51 │   >
52 │     <template #prepend>
53 │       <q-icon name="o_send" size="xs" />
54 │     </template>
55 │     <template #no-option>
56 │       <div class="q-pa-sm rw-text-desc">
57 │         暂无可用目标，请先连接设备
58 │       </div>
59 │     </template>
60 │   </q-select>
61 │ </template>
```

```
  1 │ import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
  2 │ import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
  3 │ import type { VariableMap } from '@/shared/expression/types';
  4 │ 
  5 │ // --- Step kinds ---
  6 │ 
  7 │ export const TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay'] as const;
  8 │ export type TaskStepKind = (typeof TASK_STEP_KINDS)[number];
  9 │ 
 10 │ // --- Comparison ---
 11 │ 
 12 │ export const COMPARISON_OPERATORS = [
 13 │   'eq',
 14 │   'neq',
 15 │   'gt',
 16 │   'lt',
 17 │   'gte',
 18 │   'lte',
 19 │   'contains',
 20 │   'change',
 21 │   'any',
 22 │ ] as const;
 23 │ export type ComparisonOperator = (typeof COMPARISON_OPERATORS)[number];
 24 │ 
 25 │ // --- ConditionTerm (replaces WaitCondition, adds logicOperator) ---
 26 │ 
 27 │ export interface ConditionTerm {
 28 │   readonly frameId: string;
 29 │   readonly fieldId: string;
 30 │   readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
 31 │   readonly threshold: string | number;
 32 │   readonly sourceId?: string;
 33 │   readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or'; // default 'and'; first term's logicOperator is ignored
 34 │ }
 35 │ 
 36 │ // --- ScheduleDriver (replaces TaskSchedulingMode + flat scheduling fields) ---
 37 │ 
 38 │ export type ScheduleDriver =
 39 │   | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
 40 │   | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
 41 │   | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
 42 │ 
 43 │ // --- FieldVariation ---
 44 │ 
 45 │ export interface FieldVariation {
 46 │   readonly fieldId: string;
 47 │   readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
 48 │ }
 49 │ 
 50 │ // --- StepRepeat ---
 51 │ 
 52 │ export interface StepRepeat {
 53 │   readonly intervalMs: number;
 54 │   readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
 55 │   readonly maxCount?: number;
 56 │ }
 57 │ 
 58 │ // --- Step definitions ---
 59 │ 
 60 │ export interface SendStepConfig {
 61 │   readonly frameId: string;
 62 │   readonly targetId: string; // required (was optional + fallback to definition.targetId)
 63 │   readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
 64 │   readonly variables?: VariableMap;
 65 │   readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;
 66 │   readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;
 67 │ }
 68 │ 
 69 │ export interface WaitConditionConfig {
 70 │   readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // single condition -> condition array
 71 │   readonly timeoutMs?: number; // optional (undefined = wait indefinitely until signal interrupt)
 72 │   readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
 73 │ }
 74 │ 
 75 │ export interface DelayStepConfig {
 76 │   readonly durationMs: number;
 77 │ }
 78 │ 
 79 │ // --- Unified TaskStepDefinition (config field name unified) ---
 80 │ 
 81 │ export type TaskStepDefinition =
 82 │   | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
 83 │   | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
 84 │   | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
 85 │ 
 86 │ // --- Error policy ---
 87 │ 
 88 │ export const TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause'] as const;
 89 │ export type TaskErrorAction = (typeof TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS)[number];
 90 │ 
 91 │ export interface TaskErrorPolicy {
 92 │   readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction;
 93 │   readonly retryCount?: number;
 94 │   readonly retryDelayMs?: number;
 95 │ }
 96 │ 
 97 │ // --- Stop condition ---
 98 │ 
 99 │ export interface TaskStopCondition {
100 │   readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
101 │   readonly maxIterations?: number;
102 │   readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // new: early exit when condition met
103 │ }
104 │ 
105 │ // --- TaskDefinition (restructured) ---
106 │ 
107 │ export interface TaskDefinition {
108 │   readonly id: string;
109 │   readonly name: string;
110 │   readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
111 │   readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver; // replaces schedulingMode + flat scheduling fields
112 │   readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
113 │   readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
114 │   readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
115 │ }
116 │ 
117 │ // --- Lifecycle ---
118 │ 
119 │ export const TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = [
120 │   'created',
121 │   'running',
122 │   'paused',
123 │   'stopped',
124 │   'completed',
125 │   'failed',
126 │ ] as const;
127 │ export type TaskLifecycleStatus = (typeof TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES)[number];
128 │ 
129 │ export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail';
130 │ 
131 │ // --- Step results ---
132 │ 
133 │ export interface SendStepResult {
134 │   readonly kind: 'send';
135 │   readonly sendResult: SendResult;
136 │ }
137 │ 
138 │ export interface WaitConditionStepResult {
139 │   readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
140 │   readonly matched: boolean;
141 │   readonly matchedValue?: number | string;
142 │   readonly timedOut: boolean;
143 │ }
144 │ 
145 │ export interface DelayStepResult {
146 │   readonly kind: 'delay';
147 │   readonly completed: boolean;
148 │ }
149 │ 
150 │ export type TaskStepResultPayload =
151 │   | SendStepResult
152 │   | WaitConditionStepResult
153 │   | DelayStepResult;
154 │ 
155 │ export interface TaskStepResult extends TaskStepResultPayload {
156 │   readonly stepIndex: number;
157 │   readonly iteration: number;
158 │   readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction;
159 │ }
160 │ 
161 │ // --- Instance state ---
162 │ 
163 │ export interface TaskInstanceState {
164 │   readonly instanceId: string;
165 │   readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
166 │   readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus;
167 │   readonly startedAt?: string;
168 │   readonly pausedAt?: string;
169 │   readonly stoppedAt?: string;
170 │   readonly completedAt?: string;
171 │   readonly failedAt?: string;
172 │   readonly currentStepIndex: number;
173 │   readonly currentIteration: number;
174 │   readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[];
175 │   readonly error?: string;
176 │ }
177 │ 
178 │ export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
179 │ 
180 │ // --- Progress ---
181 │ 
182 │ export interface TaskProgress {
183 │   readonly stepsTotal: number;
184 │   readonly stepsCompleted: number;
185 │   readonly stepsFailed: number;
186 │   readonly stepsSkipped: number;
187 │   readonly iterationsCompleted: number;
188 │   readonly iterationsTotal: number | null;
189 │   readonly elapsedMs: number;
190 │   readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null;
191 │   readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null;
192 │ }
193 │ 
194 │ // --- Execution summary ---
195 │ 
196 │ export interface TaskExecutionSummary {
197 │   readonly kind: 'completed' | 'stopped' | 'failed';
198 │   readonly summary: {
199 │     readonly stepsTotal: number;
200 │     readonly stepsSucceeded: number;
201 │     readonly stepsFailed: number;
202 │     readonly stepsSkipped: number;
203 │   };
204 │   readonly durationMs: number;
205 │   readonly startedAt: string;
206 │   readonly finishedAt: string;
207 │ }
208 │ 
209 │ // --- Condition match input ---
210 │ // Decoupled from receive internals. Service layer maps from receive public API outputs.
211 │ 
212 │ export interface ConditionMatchInput {
213 │   readonly frameId: string;
214 │   readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
215 │   readonly sourceId?: string;
216 │ }
217 │ 
218 │ // --- ResolvedStopCondition & resolveStopCondition ---
219 │ 
220 │ export type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
221 │ 
222 │ export function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
223 │   if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
224 │     const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
225 │     return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
226 │   }
227 │   // Immediate schedule defaults to 1 iteration
228 │   if (def.schedule.kind === 'immediate' && !def.stopCondition?.maxIterations) {
229 │     return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: 1 };
230 │   }
231 │   return def.stopCondition ?? {};
232 │ }
```
