  "content": "import { shallowRef, watch } from 'vue';\nimport type { TaskService } from '../services';\nimport type { ReadonlyTaskInstanceState } from '../core';\nimport { selectActiveInstances, selectTaskHistory } from '../selectors';\nimport { resolveDisplayStatus } from '../components/taskStatusMap';\nimport { calculateProgress } from '../core';\nimport type { TaskTableRow } from '../components/task-columns';\nimport type { HistoryTableRow } from '../components/history-columns';\n\nfunction toTaskRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): TaskTableRow {\n  const displayStatus = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);\n  const progress = calculateProgress(inst);\n  const total = progress.stepsTotal || 1;\n  const pct = Math.round((progress.stepsCompleted / total) * 100);\n  return {\n    instanceId: inst.instanceId,\n    name: inst.definitionRef.name,\n    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,\n    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,\n    displayStatus,\n    progressPercent: pct,\n    progressLabel: `${progress.stepsCompleted}/${progress.stepsTotal}`,\n    _original: inst,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toHistoryRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): HistoryTableRow {\n  const startedAt = inst.startedAt ? new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime() : 0;\n  const endRef = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt;\n  const endTime = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();\n  const elapsedMs = startedAt > 0 ? Math.max(0, endTime - startedAt) : 0;\n  const finishedAt = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt ?? '';\n  return {\n    instanceId: inst.instanceId,\n    name: inst.definitionRef.name,\n    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,\n    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,\n    elapsedMs,\n    finishedAt,\n    _original: inst,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function useTaskList(taskService: TaskService, mode: 'active' | 'history') {\n  const rows = shallowRef<TaskTableRow[]>([]);\n  const historyRows = shallowRef<HistoryTableRow[]>([]);\n\n  function refresh(): void {\n    const snapshot = taskService.getSnapshot();\n    if (mode === 'active') {\n      const active = selectActiveInstances(snapshot);\n      rows.value = active.map(toTaskRow);\n    } else {\n      const history = selectTaskHistory(snapshot);\n      historyRows.value = history.map(toHistoryRow);\n    }\n  }\n\n  function selectAllRows(): readonly (TaskTableRow | HistoryTableRow)[] {\n    return mode === 'active' ? rows.value : historyRows.value;\n  }\n\n  return { rows, historyRows, refresh, selectAllRows };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { ref, computed, shallowRef } from 'vue';\nimport type { TaskService } from '../services';\nimport type {\n  TaskDefinition,\n  TaskStepDefinition,\n  ScheduleDriver,\n  TaskErrorPolicy,\n  TaskStopCondition,\n  SendStepConfig,\n  WaitConditionConfig,\n  ConditionTerm,\n} from '../core';\nimport { validateTaskDefinition, createTaskDefinition, cloneStepDefinition } from '../core';\nimport type { TaskValidationIssue } from '../core/task-validation';\n\nexport function useTaskEditor(taskService: TaskService) {\n  const isEditing = ref(false);\n  const editingInstanceId = ref<string | null>(null);\n  const isSaving = ref(false);\n\n  // Form state\n  const taskName = ref('');\n  const scheduleKind = ref<ScheduleDriver['kind']>('immediate');\n  const timerIntervalMs = ref(1000);\n  const timerInfinite = ref(false);\n  const timerIterations = ref(1);\n  const eventConditions = ref<ConditionTerm[]>([]);\n  const eventCooldownMs = ref(0);\n  const steps = ref<TaskStepDefinition[]>([]);\n  const stopCondition = ref<TaskStopCondition>({});\n  const errorPolicy = ref<TaskErrorPolicy>({\n    onFailure: 'stop',\n  });\n\n  const validationIssues = shallowRef<TaskValidationIssue[]>([]);\n\n  const hasErrors = computed(() =>\n    validationIssues.value.some((i) => i.severity === 'error'),\n  );\n\n  function buildScheduleDriver(): ScheduleDriver {\n    switch (scheduleKind.value) {\n      case 'immediate':\n        return { kind: 'immediate' };\n      case 'timer':\n        return { kind: 'timer', intervalMs: timerIntervalMs.value };\n      case 'event':\n        return {\n          kind: 'event',\n          conditions: eventConditions.value,\n          cooldownMs: eventCooldownMs.value || undefined,\n        };\n    }\n  }\n\n  function buildStopCondition(): TaskStopCondition | undefined {\n    const sc: TaskStopCondition = { ...stopCondition.value };\n    if (scheduleKind.value === 'timer' && !timerInfinite.value) {\n      sc.maxIterations = timerIterations.value;\n    }\n    return Object.keys(sc).length > 0 ? sc : undefined;\n  }\n\n  function buildDefinition(): TaskDefinition {\n    return createTaskDefinition({\n      id: editingInstanceId.value ?? `task-${Date.now()}`,\n      name: taskName.value,\n      schedule: buildScheduleDriver(),\n      steps: steps.value,\n      errorPolicy: errorPolicy.value,\n      stopCondition: buildStopCondition(),\n    });\n  }\n\n  function validate(): boolean {\n    const def = buildDefinition();\n    const issues = validateTaskDefinition(def);\n    validationIssues.value = issues;\n    return !issues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');\n  }\n\n  function openNew(): void {\n    editingInstanceId.value = null;\n    taskName.value = '';\n    scheduleKind.value = 'immediate';\n    timerIntervalMs.value = 1000;\n    timerInfinite.value = false;\n    timerIterations.value = 1;\n    eventConditions.value = [];\n    eventCooldownMs.value = 0;\n    steps.value = [];\n    stopCondition.value = {};\n    errorPolicy.value = { onFailure: 'stop' };\n    validationIssues.value = [];\n    isEditing.value = true;\n  }\n\n  function openEdit(instanceId: string): void {\n    const instance = taskService.getInstance(instanceId);\n    if (!instance) return;\n    if (instance.lifecycle !== 'created') return;\n\n    editingInstanceId.value = instanceId;\n    const def = instance.definitionRef;\n    taskName.value = def.name;\n    scheduleKind.value = def.schedule.kind;\n\n    if (def.schedule.kind === 'timer') {\n      timerIntervalMs.value = def.schedule.intervalMs;\n    }\n    if (def.schedule.kind === 'event') {\n      eventConditions.value = def.schedule.conditions.map((c) => ({ ...c }));\n      eventCooldownMs.value = def.schedule.cooldownMs ?? 0;\n    }\n\n    steps.value = def.steps.map(cloneStepDefinition);\n    stopCondition.value = def.stopCondition ? { ...def.stopCondition } : {};\n    errorPolicy.value = { ...def.errorPolicy };\n\n    if (stopCondition.value.maxIterations !== undefined) {\n      timerIterations.value = stopCondition.value.maxIterations;\n      timerInfinite.value = false;\n    } else if (def.schedule.kind === 'timer') {\n      timerInfinite.value = true;\n    }\n\n    validationIssues.value = [];\n    isEditing.value = true;\n  }\n\n  function save(): string | null {\n    if (!validate()) return null;\n    isSaving.value = true;\n    try {\n      const def = buildDefinition();\n      if (editingInstanceId.value) {\n        taskService.updateTask(editingInstanceId.value, def);\n      } else {\n        const inst = taskService.createTask(def);\n        editingInstanceId.value = inst.instanceId;\n      }\n      isEditing.value = false;\n      return editingInstanceId.value;\n    } finally {\n      isSaving.value = false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  function saveAndStart(): string | null {\n    const id = save();\n    if (id) {\n      taskService.startTask(id);\n    }\n    return id;\n  }\n\n  function close(): void {\n    isEditing.value = false;\n    editingInstanceId.value = null;\n  }\n\n  function addStep(step: TaskStepDefinition): void {\n    steps.value = [...steps.value, step];\n  }\n\n  function removeStep(index: number): void {\n    steps.value = steps.value.filter((_, i) => i !== index);\n  }\n\n  function updateStep(index: number, step: TaskStepDefinition): void {\n    steps.value = steps.value.map((s, i) => (i === index ? step : s));\n  }\n\n  function addEventCondition(): void {\n    eventConditions.value = [\n      ...eventConditions.value,\n      { frameId: '', fieldId: '', operator: 'eq', threshold: '' },\n    ];\n  }\n\n  function removeEventCondition(index: number): void {\n    eventConditions.value = eventConditions.value.filter((_, i) => i !== index);\n  }\n\n  function updateEventCondition(index: number, cond: ConditionTerm): void {\n    eventConditions.value = eventConditions.value.map((c, i) =>\n      i === index ? cond : c,\n    );\n  }\n\n  function duplicateStepValuesFromPrevious(stepIndex: number): void {\n    if (stepIndex <= 0) return;\n    const prev = steps.value[stepIndex - 1];\n    const curr = steps.value[stepIndex];\n    if (prev.kind === 'send' && curr.kind === 'send') {\n      const prevConfig = prev.config as SendStepConfig;\n      const currConfig = { ...curr.config } as SendStepConfig;\n      if (prevConfig.userFieldValues) {\n        currConfig.userFieldValues = { ...prevConfig.userFieldValues };\n      }\n      updateStep(stepIndex, { ...curr, config: currConfig });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    isEditing,\n    editingInstanceId,\n    isSaving,\n    taskName,\n    scheduleKind,\n    timerIntervalMs,\n    timerInfinite,\n    timerIterations,\n    eventConditions,\n    eventCooldownMs,\n    steps,\n    stopCondition,\n    errorPolicy,\n    validationIssues,\n    hasErrors,\n    openNew,\n    openEdit,\n    save,\n    saveAndStart,\n    close,\n    addStep,\n    removeStep,\n    updateStep,\n    addEventCondition,\n    removeEventCondition,\n    updateEventCondition,\n    duplicateStepValuesFromPrevious,\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/composables/use-task-monitor.ts",
  "content": "import { ref, shallowRef, watch } from 'vue';\nimport type { TaskService } from '../services';\nimport type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress } from '../core';\nimport { isTerminal, calculateProgress } from '../core';\nimport { resolveDisplayStatus } from '../components/taskStatusMap';\nimport { TASK_STATUS_MAP } from '../components/taskStatusMap';\nimport { usePolling } from '@/shared/composables';\n\nexport function useTaskMonitor(taskService: TaskService) {\n  const selectedId = ref<string | null>(null);\n  const instance = shallowRef<TaskInstanceState | undefined>(undefined);\n  const progress = shallowRef<TaskProgress | null>(null);\n  const displayStatus = ref('');\n  const statusInfo = shallowRef<{ label: string; color: string }>({\n    label: '',\n    color: 'grey',\n  });\n\n  const polling = usePolling(refresh, 1000);\n\n  function refresh(): void {\n    if (!selectedId.value) return;\n    const inst = taskService.getInstance(selectedId.value);\n    if (!inst) {\n      instance.value = undefined;\n      progress.value = null;\n      displayStatus.value = '';\n      statusInfo.value = { label: '', color: 'grey' };\n      return;\n    }\n    instance.value = inst;\n    progress.value = calculateProgress(inst);\n    const status = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);\n    displayStatus.value = status;\n    statusInfo.value = TASK_STATUS_MAP[status] ?? { label: status, color: 'grey' };\n\n    if (isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) {\n      polling.stop();\n    }\n  }\n\n  function select(instanceId: string | null): void {\n    selectedId.value = instanceId;\n    if (instanceId) {\n      refresh();\n      const inst = taskService.getInstance(instanceId);\n      if (inst && !isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) {\n        polling.start();\n      }\n    } else {\n      instance.value = undefined;\n      progress.value = null;\n      displayStatus.value = '';\n      statusInfo.value = { label: '', color: 'grey' };\n      polling.stop();\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    selectedId,\n    instance,\n    progress,\n    displayStatus,\n    statusInfo,\n    polling,\n    select,\n    refresh,\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "file_path": "/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { computed } from 'vue';\nimport type { TaskInstanceState, TaskProgress, TaskStepDefinition, TaskStepResult } from '@/features/task';\nimport { STEP_KIND_LABELS } from '@/features/task/components/task-labels';\nimport StatusBadge from './StatusBadge.vue';\nimport { TASK_STATUS_MAP } from '@/features/task/components/taskStatusMap';\nimport { isStepResultFailed } from '@/features/task';\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  readonly instance: TaskInstanceState;\n  readonly progress: TaskProgress | null;\n  readonly displayStatus: string;\n  readonly statusInfo: { label: string; color: string };\n}>();\n\nconst emit = defineEmits<{\n  pause: [];\n  resume: [];\n  stop: [];\n}>();\n\nconst stepStatuses = computed(() => {\n  const steps = props.instance.definitionRef.steps;\n  const results = props.instance.stepResults;\n  const currentIter = props.instance.currentIteration;\n  const currentStepIdx = props.instance.currentStepIndex;\n\n  const currentIterResults = results.filter((r) => r.iteration === currentIter);\n  const resultMap = new Map<number, TaskStepResult>();\n  for (const r of currentIterResults) {\n    resultMap.set(r.stepIndex, r);\n  }\n\n  return steps.map((step, idx) => {\n    const result = resultMap.get(idx);\n    let status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'skipped' = 'pending';\n    if (result) {\n      if (result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step') {\n        status = 'skipped';\n      } else if (isStepResultFailed(result)) {\n        status = 'failed';\n      } else {\n        status = 'done';\n      }\n    } else if (idx === currentStepIdx) {\n      status = 'running';\n    }\n    return { step, index: idx, status, result };\n  });\n});\n\nfunction formatElapsed(ms: number): string {\n  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;\n  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);\n  if (s < 60) return `${s}s`;\n  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);\n  const rs = s % 60;\n  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ${rs}s`;\n  const h = Math.floor(m / 60);\n  const rm = m % 60;\n  return `${h}h ${rm}m`;\n}\n\nfunction formatStepResult(step: TaskStepDefinition, result: TaskStepResult | undefined): string {\n  if (!result) return '';\n  switch (step.kind) {\n    case 'send':\n      return result.kind === 'send'\n        ? result.sendResult.kind === 'sent' ? '发送成功' : '发送失败'\n        : '';\n    case 'wait-condition':\n      return result.kind === 'wait-condition'\n        ? result.matched ? '条件匹配' : result.timedOut ? '超时' : ''\n        : '';\n    case 'delay':\n      return result.kind === 'delay'\n        ? result.completed ? '完成' : '中断'\n        : '';\n  }\n}\n\nconst isRunning = computed(() => props.instance.lifecycle === 'running');\nconst isPaused = computed(() => props.instance.lifecycle === 'paused');\n\nconst progressPct = computed(() => {\n  if (!props.progress) return 0;\n  const total = props.progress.stepsTotal || 1;\n  return Math.round((props.progress.stepsCompleted / total) * 100);\n});\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-3\">\n    <!-- Header -->\n    <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n      <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n        <StatusBadge :status=\"displayStatus\" :status-map=\"TASK_STATUS_MAP\" />\n        <span class=\"rw-text-value text-sm\">{{ instance.definitionRef.name }}</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Progress -->\n    <div v-if=\"progress\" class=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n      <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">进度</span>\n        <span class=\"rw-text-value text-xs\">{{ progress.stepsCompleted }}/{{ progress.stepsTotal }} ({{ progressPct }}%)</span>\n      </div>\n      <q-linear-progress :value=\"progressPct / 100\" color=\"primary\" size=\"6px\" rounded />\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Elapsed -->\n    <div v-if=\"progress\" class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n      <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">耗时</span>\n      <span class=\"rw-text-value text-xs\">{{ formatElapsed(progress.elapsedMs) }}</span>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Iteration info -->\n    <div v-if=\"progress && progress.iterationsTotal !== null\" class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n      <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">迭代</span>\n      <span class=\"rw-text-value text-xs\">{{ progress.iterationsCompleted }}/{{ progress.iterationsTotal }}</span>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Step status list -->\n    <q-separator />\n    <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n      <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">步骤状态</span>\n      <div\n        v-for=\"item in stepStatuses\"\n        :key=\"item.index\"\n        class=\"flex items-center gap-2\"\n      >\n        <q-icon\n          :name=\"item.status === 'running' ? 'o_pending' : item.status === 'done' ? 'o_check_circle' : item.status === 'failed' ? 'o_error' : item.status === 'skipped' ? 'o_skip_next' : 'o_radio_button_unchecked'\"\n          :color=\"item.status === 'done' ? 'positive' : item.status === 'failed' ? 'negative' : item.status === 'running' ? 'primary' : 'grey'\"\n          size=\"xs\"\n        />\n        <span class=\"rw-text-value text-xs flex-1\">\n          {{ item.step.name ?? STEP_KIND_LABELS[item.step.kind].label }} #{{ item.index + 1 }}\n        </span>\n        <span v-if=\"item.result\" class=\"rw-text-desc text-xs\">\n          {{ formatStepResult(item.step, item.result) }}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Error -->\n    <div v-if=\"instance.error\" class=\"rw-text-error text-xs p-2 rounded\" style=\"background: var(--rw-color-surface-elevated)\">\n      {{ instance.error }}\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Actions -->\n    <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n      <q-btn\n        v-if=\"isRunning\"\n        flat\n        no-caps\n        dense\n        icon=\"o_pause\"\n        label=\"暂停\"\n        color=\"warning\"\n        size=\"sm\"\n        @click=\"emit('pause')\"\n      />\n      <q-btn\n        v-if=\"isPaused\"\n        flat\n        no-caps\n        dense\n        icon=\"o_play_arrow\"\n        label=\"恢复\"\n        color=\"primary\"\n        size=\"sm\"\n        @click=\"emit('resume')\"\n      />\n      <q-btn\n        v-if=\"isRunning || isPaused\"\n        flat\n        no-caps\n        dense\n        icon=\"o_stop\"\n        label=\"停止\"\n        color=\"negative\"\n        size=\"sm\"\n        @click=\"emit('stop')\"\n      />\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ref, shallowRef, watch, computed, onUnmounted } from 'vue';\nimport { useQuasar } from 'quasar';\nimport { useRewriteRuntime } from '@/app/rewriteRuntime';\nimport DataTable from '@/widgets/DataTable.vue';\nimport TableToolbar from '@/widgets/TableToolbar.vue';\nimport StatusBadge from '@/widgets/StatusBadge.vue';\nimport TaskExecutionDetail from '@/widgets/TaskExecutionDetail.vue';\nimport FieldEditWidget from '@/widgets/FieldEditWidget.vue';\nimport SendTargetSelector from '@/features/send/components/SendTargetSelector.vue';\nimport { useAsyncAction, useNotify, usePolling } from '@/shared/composables';\nimport { isTerminal } from '@/features/task/core';\nimport { createSendStep, createDelayStep, createWaitConditionStep } from '@/features/task/core';\nimport type { TaskStepDefinition, SendStepConfig, WaitConditionConfig, DelayStepConfig, ConditionTerm } from '@/features/task/core';\nimport type { ReadonlyTaskInstanceState } from '@/features/task';\nimport { taskColumns, type TaskTableRow } from '@/features/task/components/task-columns';\nimport { historyColumns, type HistoryTableRow } from '@/features/task/components/history-columns';\nimport { TASK_STATUS_MAP, resolveDisplayStatus } from '@/features/task/components/taskStatusMap';\nimport { SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP, SCHEDULE_KIND_OPTIONS } from '@/features/task/components/scheduleKindMap';\nimport { STEP_KIND_LABELS, ERROR_ACTION_LABELS, ON_TIMEOUT_OPTIONS, ADD_STEP_OPTIONS } from '@/features/task/components/task-labels';\nimport { useTaskEditor } from '@/features/task/composables/use-task-editor';\n\nconst $q = useQuasar();\nconst notify = useNotify();\nconst { execute: executeAction, isOperating } = useAsyncAction();\nconst runtime = useRewriteRuntime();\nconst taskService = runtime.features.taskService;\nconst frameService = runtime.features.frameService;\nconst connectionService = runtime.features.connectionService;\n\nconst editor = useTaskEditor(taskService);\n\n// ===== Tab & filter state =====\nconst activeTab = ref<'active' | 'history'>('active');\nconst searchText = ref('');\nconst statusFilter = ref<string>('all');\n\n// ===== Task list data =====\nconst activeRows = shallowRef<TaskTableRow[]>([]);\nconst historyRows = shallowRef<HistoryTableRow[]>([]);\nconst selectedActiveRow = shallowRef<TaskTableRow[]>([]);\nconst selectedHistoryRow = shallowRef<HistoryTableRow[]>([]);\n\nconst listPolling = usePolling(refreshLists, 1000);\nlistPolling.start();\n\nfunction refreshLists(): void {\n  const snapshot = taskService.getSnapshot();\n  const active = snapshot.instances.filter(\n    (i) => !isTerminal(i.lifecycle),\n  );\n  activeRows.value = active.map(toActiveRow);\n\n  const terminated = snapshot.instances.filter((i) => isTerminal(i.lifecycle));\n  const historyIds = new Set(snapshot.history.map((i) => i.instanceId));\n  const merged = [...snapshot.history, ...terminated.filter((i) => !historyIds.has(i.instanceId))];\n  historyRows.value = merged.map(toHistoryRow);\n}\n\nfunction toActiveRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): TaskTableRow {\n  const displayStatus = resolveDisplayStatus(inst.lifecycle, inst.definitionRef);\n  const progress = inst.startedAt\n    ? (() => {\n        const stepsPerIteration = inst.definitionRef.steps.length;\n        const currentIterResults = inst.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === inst.currentIteration);\n        let stepsCompleted = 0;\n        for (const r of currentIterResults) {\n          if (r.appliedPolicy !== 'skip-step') stepsCompleted++;\n        }\n        return { completed: stepsCompleted, total: stepsPerIteration };\n      })()\n    : { completed: 0, total: inst.definitionRef.steps.length };\n  const pct = progress.total > 0 ? Math.round((progress.completed / progress.total) * 100) : 0;\n  return {\n    instanceId: inst.instanceId,\n    name: inst.definitionRef.name,\n    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,\n    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,\n    displayStatus,\n    progressPercent: pct,\n    progressLabel: `${progress.completed}/${progress.total}`,\n    _original: inst,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction toHistoryRow(inst: ReadonlyTaskInstanceState): HistoryTableRow {\n  const startedAt = inst.startedAt ? new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime() : 0;\n  const endRef = inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt;\n  const endTime = endRef ? new Date(endRef).getTime() : Date.now();\n  const elapsedMs = startedAt > 0 ? Math.max(0, endTime - startedAt) : 0;\n  return {\n    instanceId: inst.instanceId,\n    name: inst.definitionRef.name,\n    scheduleKind: inst.definitionRef.schedule.kind,\n    lifecycle: inst.lifecycle,\n    elapsedMs,\n    finishedAt: inst.completedAt ?? inst.stoppedAt ?? inst.failedAt ?? '',\n    _original: inst,\n  };\n}\n\nonUnmounted(() => {\n  listPolling.stop();\n});\n\n// ===== Selected instance for right panel =====\nconst selectedInstance = computed<ReadonlyTaskInstanceState | null>(() => {\n  if (activeTab.value === 'active' && selectedActiveRow.value.length > 0) {\n    return selectedActiveRow.value[0]._original;\n  }\n  if (activeTab.value === 'history' && selectedHistoryRow.value.length > 0) {\n    return selectedHistoryRow.value[0]._original;\n  }\n  return null;\n});\n\nconst selectedProgress = computed(() => {\n  if (!selectedInstance.value) return null;\n  const inst = selectedInstance.value;\n  const stepsPerIteration = inst.definitionRef.steps.length;\n  const currentIterResults = inst.stepResults.filter((r) => r.iteration === inst.currentIteration);\n  let stepsCompleted = 0;\n  for (const r of currentIterResults) {\n    if (r.appliedPolicy !== 'skip-step') stepsCompleted++;\n  }\n  return { completed: stepsCompleted, total: stepsPerIteration };\n});\n\nconst selectedDisplayStatus = computed(() => {\n  if (!selectedInstance.value) return '';\n  return resolveDisplayStatus(selectedInstance.value.lifecycle, selectedInstance.value.definitionRef);\n});\n\nconst selectedStatusInfo = computed(() => {\n  return TASK_STATUS_MAP[selectedDisplayStatus.value] ?? { label: selectedDisplayStatus.value, color: 'grey' };\n});\n\n// ===== Active tab actions =====\nfunction onActiveRowClick(row: TaskTableRow): void {\n  selectedActiveRow.value = [row];\n}\n\nfunction onActiveSelectionChange(selected: TaskTableRow[]): void {\n  selectedActiveRow.value = selected;\n}\n\nfunction onHistoryRowClick(row: HistoryTableRow): void {\n  selectedHistoryRow.value = [row];\n}\n\nfunction onHistorySelectionChange(selected: HistoryTableRow[]): void {\n  selectedHistoryRow.value = selected;\n}\n\n// ===== Task lifecycle actions =====\nasync function onStart(id: string): Promise<void> {\n  await executeAction(`start-${id}`, async () => {\n    taskService.startTask(id);\n    notify.success('任务已启动');\n    refreshLists();\n  });\n}\n\nasync function onPause(id: string): Promise<void> {\n  await executeAction(`pause-${id}`, async () => {\n    taskService.pauseTask(id);\n    notify.info('任务已暂停');\n    refreshLists();\n  });\n}\n\nasync function onResume(id: string): Promise<void> {\n  await executeAction(`resume-${id}`, async () => {\n    taskService.resumeTask(id);\n    notify.success('任务已恢复');\n    refreshLists();\n  });\n}\n\nfunction onStop(id: string): void {\n  $q.dialog({\n    title: '确认停止',\n    message: '确定要停止该任务吗？',\n    cancel: true,\n    persistent: false,\n  }).onOk(async () => {\n    await executeAction(`stop-${id}`, async () => {\n      taskService.stopTask(id);\n      notify.info('任务已停止');\n      refreshLists();\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nasync function onRemove(id: string): Promise<void> {\n  $q.dialog({\n    title: '确认删除',\n    message: '确定要删除该任务吗？此操作不可撤销。',\n    cancel: true,\n    persistent: false,\n  }).onOk(async () => {\n    await executeAction(`remove-${id}`, async () => {\n      taskService.removeTask(id);\n      notify.success('任务已删除');\n      selectedActiveRow.value = [];\n      selectedHistoryRow.value = [];\n      refreshLists();\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nasync function onRetry(id: string): Promise<void> {\n  await executeAction(`retry-${id}`, async () => {\n    const result = taskService.retryTask(id);\n    if (result) {\n      notify.success('任务已重新执行');\n      refreshLists();\n    } else {\n      notify.error('重新执行失败');\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nasync function onStopAll(): Promise<void> {\n  $q.dialog({\n    title: '确认全部停止',\n    message: '确定要停止所有运行中的任务吗？',\n    cancel: true,\n    persistent: false,\n  }).onOk(async () => {\n    await executeAction('stopAll', async () => {\n      const count = taskService.stopAll();\n      notify.info(`已停止 ${count} 个任务`);\n      refreshLists();\n    });\n  });\n}\n\n// ===== Editor dialog =====\nlet nextStepId = 1;\n\nfunction onNewTask(): void {\n  editor.openNew();\n}\n\nfunction onEditTask(): void {\n  if (!selectedInstance.value) return;\n  if (selectedInstance.value.lifecycle !== 'created') {\n    notify.warning('只能编辑待启动状态的任务');\n    return;\n  }\n  editor.openEdit(selectedInstance.value.instanceId);\n}\n\nfunction onEditorHide(): void {\n  editor.close();\n}\n\nfunction onSave(): void {\n  const id = editor.save();\n  if (id) {\n    notify.success('任务已保存');\n    refreshLists();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onSaveAndStart(): void {\n  const id = editor.saveAndStart();\n  if (id) {\n    notify.success('任务已保存并启动');\n    refreshLists();\n  }\n}\n\nfunction onAddStep(kind: string): void {\n  let step: TaskStepDefinition;\n  switch (kind) {\n    case 'send':\n      step = createSendStep(\n        { frameId: '', targetId: '' },\n        { id: `step-${nextStepId++}`, name: '发送步骤' },\n      );\n      break;\n    case 'wait-condition':\n      step = createWaitConditionStep(\n        { conditions: [], onTimeout: 'fail' } as WaitConditionConfig,\n        { id: `step-${nextStepId++}`, name: '等待条件' },\n      );\n      break;\n    case 'delay':\n      step = createDelayStep(1000, { id: `step-${nextStepId++}`, name: '延时' });\n      break;\n    default:\n      return;\n  }\n  editor.addStep(step);\n}\n\n// ===== Formatting helpers =====\nfunction formatElapsed(ms: number): string {\n  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;\n  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);\n  if (s < 60) return `${s}s`;\n  const m = Math.floor(s / 60);\n  const rs = s % 60;\n  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ${rs}s`;\n  const h = Math.floor(m / 60);\n  const rm = m % 60;\n  return `${h}h ${rm}m`;\n}\n\nfunction formatTime(iso?: string): string {\n  if (!iso) return '--';\n  const d = new Date(iso);\n  return d.toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });\n}\n\n// ===== Step editor helpers =====\nfunction getFrameName(frameId: string): string {\n  if (!frameId) return '--';\n  const frame = frameService.getFrame(frameId);\n  return frame?.name ?? frameId;\n}\n\nfunction getFrameFields(frameId: string) {\n  if (!frameId) return [];\n  const frame = frameService.getFrame(frameId);\n  return frame?.fields ?? [];\n}\n\nfunction updateSendStepField(index: number, key: keyof SendStepConfig, value: unknown): void {\n  const step = editor.steps[index];\n  if (step.kind !== 'send') return;\n  const config = { ...step.config, [key]: value } as SendStepConfig;\n  editor.updateStep(index, { ...step, config });\n}\n\nfunction updateDelayStepDuration(index: number, durationMs: number): void {\n  const step = editor.steps[index];\n  if (step.kind !== 'delay') return;\n  editor.updateStep(index, { ...step, config: { durationMs } });\n}\n\nfunction updateWaitConditionConfig(index: number, config: WaitConditionConfig): void {\n  const step = editor.steps[index];\n  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;\n  editor.updateStep(index, { ...step, config });\n}\n\nfunction addWaitConditionTerm(stepIndex: number): void {\n  const step = editor.steps[stepIndex];\n  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;\n  const config = step.config as WaitConditionConfig;\n  const conditions = [...config.conditions, { frameId: '', fieldId: '', operator: 'eq' as const, threshold: '' }];\n  updateWaitConditionConfig(stepIndex, { ...config, conditions });\n}\n\nfunction removeWaitConditionTerm(stepIndex: number, termIndex: number): void {\n  const step = editor.steps[stepIndex];\n  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;\n  const config = step.config as WaitConditionConfig;\n  const conditions = config.conditions.filter((_, i) => i !== termIndex);\n  updateWaitConditionConfig(stepIndex, { ...config, conditions });\n}\n\nfunction updateWaitConditionTerm(stepIndex: number, termIndex: number, term: ConditionTerm): void {\n  const step = editor.steps[stepIndex];\n  if (step.kind !== 'wait-condition') return;\n  const config = step.config as WaitConditionConfig;\n  const conditions = config.conditions.map((c, i) => (i === termIndex ? term : c));\n  updateWaitConditionConfig(stepIndex, { ...config, conditions });\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <q-page class=\"task-page\">\n    <!-- Toolbar -->\n    <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rw-divider-b\">\n      <span class=\"rw-text-value text-h6\">任务管理</span>\n      <div class=\"flex-1\" />\n      <q-btn unelevated no-caps color=\"primary\" icon=\"o_add\" label=\"新建任务\" @click=\"onNewTask\" />\n      <q-btn flat no-caps icon=\"o_stop_circle\" label=\"全部停止\" :disable=\"activeRows.length === 0\" @click=\"onStopAll\" />\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Main layout: list + right panel -->\n    <div class=\"flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n      <!-- Left: task list with tabs -->\n      <div class=\"flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden\">\n        <TableToolbar\n          v-model:search-model-value=\"searchText\"\n          search-placeholder=\"搜索任务名称...\"\n        />\n\n        <q-tabs v-model=\"activeTab\" dense inline-label class=\"q-px-4\">\n          <q-tab name=\"active\" label=\"活动任务\" no-caps />\n          <q-tab name=\"history\" label=\"历史记录\" no-caps />\n        </q-tabs>\n\n        <!-- Active tab -->\n        <div v-show=\"activeTab === 'active'\" class=\"flex-1\">\n          <DataTable\n            :columns=\"taskColumns\"\n            :rows=\"activeRows\"\n            row-key=\"instanceId\"\n            selection=\"single\"\n            :selected=\"selectedActiveRow\"\n            container-height=\"calc(100vh - 240px)\"\n            @row-click=\"(_row: TaskTableRow) => onActiveRowClick(_row)\"\n            @update:selected=\"onActiveSelectionChange\"\n          >\n            <template #no-data>\n              <div class=\"text-center w-full q-pa-lg rw-text-label\">暂无活动任务</div>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-scheduleKind=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <q-chip\n                  dense\n                  outline\n                  :color=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[props.row.scheduleKind]?.color ?? 'grey'\"\n                  :label=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[props.row.scheduleKind]?.label ?? props.row.scheduleKind\"\n                  class=\"q-ma-none\"\n                />\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-status=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <StatusBadge :status=\"props.row.displayStatus\" :status-map=\"TASK_STATUS_MAP\" />\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-progress=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                  <q-linear-progress\n                    :value=\"props.row.progressPercent / 100\"\n                    color=\"primary\"\n                    size=\"4px\"\n                    class=\"flex-1\"\n                    rounded\n                  />\n                  <span class=\"rw-text-desc text-xs min-w-[32px] text-right\">{{ props.row.progressLabel }}</span>\n                </div>\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-_actions=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <div class=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-1\">\n                  <q-btn\n                    v-if=\"props.row.lifecycle === 'created'\"\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_play_arrow\" size=\"sm\" color=\"positive\"\n                    :loading=\"isOperating(`start-${props.row.instanceId}`)\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onStart(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn\n                    v-if=\"props.row.lifecycle === 'running'\"\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_pause\" size=\"sm\" color=\"warning\"\n                    :loading=\"isOperating(`pause-${props.row.instanceId}`)\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onPause(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn\n                    v-if=\"props.row.lifecycle === 'paused'\"\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_play_arrow\" size=\"sm\" color=\"primary\"\n                    :loading=\"isOperating(`resume-${props.row.instanceId}`)\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onResume(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn\n                    v-if=\"props.row.lifecycle === 'running' || props.row.lifecycle === 'paused'\"\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_stop\" size=\"sm\" color=\"negative\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onStop(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn\n                    v-if=\"props.row.lifecycle === 'created'\"\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_edit\" size=\"sm\" color=\"primary\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onEditTask()\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn\n                    v-if=\"isTerminal(props.row.lifecycle)\"\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_delete\" size=\"sm\" color=\"negative\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onRemove(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                </div>\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n          </DataTable>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- History tab -->\n        <div v-show=\"activeTab === 'history'\" class=\"flex-1\">\n          <DataTable\n            :columns=\"historyColumns\"\n            :rows=\"historyRows\"\n            row-key=\"instanceId\"\n            selection=\"single\"\n            :selected=\"selectedHistoryRow\"\n            container-height=\"calc(100vh - 240px)\"\n            @row-click=\"(_row: HistoryTableRow) => onHistoryRowClick(_row)\"\n            @update:selected=\"onHistorySelectionChange\"\n          >\n            <template #no-data>\n              <div class=\"text-center w-full q-pa-lg rw-text-label\">暂无历史记录</div>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-scheduleKind=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <q-chip\n                  dense\n                  outline\n                  :color=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[props.row.scheduleKind]?.color ?? 'grey'\"\n                  :label=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[props.row.scheduleKind]?.label ?? props.row.scheduleKind\"\n                  class=\"q-ma-none\"\n                />\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-result=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <StatusBadge :status=\"props.row.lifecycle\" :status-map=\"TASK_STATUS_MAP\" />\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-elapsed=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <span class=\"rw-text-value text-xs\">{{ formatElapsed(props.row.elapsedMs) }}</span>\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-finishedAt=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <span class=\"rw-text-value text-xs\">{{ formatTime(props.row.finishedAt) }}</span>\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n\n            <template #body-cell-_actions=\"props\">\n              <q-td :props=\"props\">\n                <div class=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-1\">\n                  <q-btn\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_replay\" size=\"sm\" color=\"primary\"\n                    :loading=\"isOperating(`retry-${props.row.instanceId}`)\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onRetry(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn\n                    flat round dense icon=\"o_delete\" size=\"sm\" color=\"negative\"\n                    @click.stop=\"onRemove(props.row.instanceId)\"\n                  />\n                </div>\n              </q-td>\n            </template>\n          </DataTable>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <!-- Right panel: 360px -->\n      <div class=\"w-[360px] flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto rw-divider-l\">\n        <template v-if=\"selectedInstance\">\n          <!-- Summary for created state -->\n          <template v-if=\"selectedInstance.lifecycle === 'created'\">\n            <div class=\"p-4 rw-divider-b\">\n              <div class=\"mb-3\">\n                <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">任务名称</span>\n                <div class=\"rw-text-value\">{{ selectedInstance.definitionRef.name }}</div>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"mb-3\">\n                <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">调度类型</span>\n                <div class=\"mt-1\">\n                  <q-chip\n                    dense\n                    outline\n                    :color=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind]?.color ?? 'grey'\"\n                    :label=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind]?.label ?? selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind\"\n                  />\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div>\n                <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">步骤数</span>\n                <div class=\"rw-text-value\">{{ selectedInstance.definitionRef.steps.length }}</div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"p-4\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <q-btn\n                  unelevated no-caps color=\"primary\"\n                  icon=\"o_edit\" label=\"编辑\"\n                  :loading=\"isOperating('edit')\"\n                  @click=\"onEditTask\"\n                />\n                <q-btn\n                  unelevated no-caps color=\"positive\"\n                  icon=\"o_play_arrow\" label=\"启动\"\n                  :loading=\"isOperating(`start-${selectedInstance.instanceId}`)\"\n                  @click=\"onStart(selectedInstance.instanceId)\"\n                />\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </template>\n\n          <!-- Execution detail for running/paused -->\n          <template v-else-if=\"!isTerminal(selectedInstance.lifecycle)\">\n            <div class=\"p-4\">\n              <TaskExecutionDetail\n                :instance=\"selectedInstance\"\n                :progress=\"selectedProgress\"\n                :display-status=\"selectedDisplayStatus\"\n                :status-info=\"selectedStatusInfo\"\n                @pause=\"onPause(selectedInstance!.instanceId)\"\n                @resume=\"onResume(selectedInstance!.instanceId)\"\n                @stop=\"onStop(selectedInstance!.instanceId)\"\n              />\n            </div>\n          </template>\n\n          <!-- Terminal state summary -->\n          <template v-else>\n            <div class=\"p-4 rw-divider-b\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-3\">\n                <StatusBadge :status=\"selectedDisplayStatus\" :status-map=\"TASK_STATUS_MAP\" />\n                <span class=\"rw-text-value\">{{ selectedInstance.definitionRef.name }}</span>\n              </div>\n              <div class=\"mb-2\">\n                <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">调度类型</span>\n                <div class=\"mt-1\">\n                  <q-chip\n                    dense\n                    outline\n                    :color=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind]?.color ?? 'grey'\"\n                    :label=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_MAP[selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind]?.label ?? selectedInstance.definitionRef.schedule.kind\"\n                  />\n                </div>\n              </div>\n              <div v-if=\"selectedInstance.error\" class=\"rw-text-error text-xs mt-2\">\n                {{ selectedInstance.error }}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div class=\"p-4\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <q-btn\n                  flat no-caps icon=\"o_replay\" label=\"重新执行\" color=\"primary\"\n                  :loading=\"isOperating(`retry-${selectedInstance.instanceId}`)\"\n                  @click=\"onRetry(selectedInstance.instanceId)\"\n                />\n                <q-btn\n                  flat no-caps icon=\"o_delete\" label=\"删除\" color=\"negative\"\n                  @click=\"onRemove(selectedInstance.instanceId)\"\n                />\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </template>\n        </template>\n\n        <!-- Empty state -->\n        <div v-else class=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center flex-1 rw-text-desc\">\n          <q-icon name=\"o_assignment\" size=\"48px\" color=\"grey\" class=\"q-mb-sm\" />\n          <p>请选择一个任务</p>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Task editor dialog -->\n    <q-dialog v-model=\"editor.isEditing.value\" @hide=\"onEditorHide\">\n      <q-card class=\"rw-dialog-xl\">\n        <q-card-section>\n          <div class=\"text-h6\">{{ editor.editingInstanceId.value ? '编辑任务' : '新建任务' }}</div>\n        </q-card-section>\n\n        <q-card-section class=\"q-pt-none rw-dialog-scroll-body\">\n          <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">\n            <!-- Basic info -->\n            <q-input\n              v-model=\"editor.taskName.value\"\n              dense outlined\n              label=\"任务名称\"\n              :rules=\"[val => !!val || '请输入任务名称']\"\n            />\n\n            <!-- Schedule type -->\n            <div>\n              <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">调度类型</span>\n              <q-select\n                v-model=\"editor.scheduleKind.value\"\n                :options=\"SCHEDULE_KIND_OPTIONS\"\n                emit-value\n                map-options\n                outlined dense\n                class=\"mt-1\"\n              />\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Timer config -->\n            <template v-if=\"editor.scheduleKind.value === 'timer'\">\n              <q-input\n                v-model.number=\"editor.timerIntervalMs.value\"\n                dense outlined\n                type=\"number\"\n                label=\"间隔 (ms)\"\n                :rules=\"[val => val > 0 || '间隔必须大于0']\"\n              />\n              <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n                <q-toggle v-model=\"editor.timerInfinite.value\" label=\"无限循环\" />\n                <q-input\n                  v-if=\"!editor.timerInfinite.value\"\n                  v-model.number=\"editor.timerIterations.value\"\n                  dense outlined\n                  type=\"number\"\n                  label=\"执行次数\"\n                  :rules=\"[val => val > 0 || '次数必须大于0']\"\n                  class=\"w-40\"\n                />\n              </div>\n            </template>\n\n            <!-- Event config -->\n            <template v-if=\"editor.scheduleKind.value === 'event'\">\n              <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n                <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n                  <span class=\"rw-text-label text-xs\">触发条件</span>\n                  <q-btn flat dense no-caps icon=\"o_add\" label=\"添加条件\" size=\"sm\" color=\"primary\" @click=\"editor.addEventCondition()\" />\n                </div>\n                <div\n                  v-for=\"(cond, ci) in editor.eventConditions.value\"\n                  :key=\"ci\"\n                  class=\"flex items-center gap-2\"\n                >\n                  <q-select\n                    :model-value=\"cond.operator\"\n                    :options=\"['eq', 'neq', 'gt', 'lt', 'gte', 'lte', 'contains', 'change', 'any']\"\n                    outlined dense\n                    emit-value\n                    class=\"w-24\"\n                    @update:model-value=\"editor.updateEventCondition(ci, { ...cond, operator: $event as ConditionTerm['operator'] })\"\n                  />\n                  <q-input\n                    :model-value=\"String(cond.threshold)\"\n                    outlined dense\n                    placeholder=\"阈值\"\n                    class=\"flex-1\"\n                    @update:model-value=\"editor.updateEventCondition(ci, { ...cond, threshold: $event })\"\n                  />\n                  <q-btn flat round dense icon=\"o_close\" size=\"xs\" color=\"negative\" @click=\"editor.removeEventCondition(ci)\" />\n                </div>\n                <div v-if=\"editor.eventConditions.value.length === 0\" class=\"rw-text-desc text-xs\">至少添加一条触发条件</div>\n              </div>\n            </template>\n\n            <q-separator />\n\n            <!-- Steps editor -->\n            <div class=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n              <div class=\"flex items-center   <q-list>\n                    <q-item\n                      v-for=\"opt in ADD_STEP_OPTIONS\"\n                      :key=\"opt.value\"\n                      clickable\n - `rewrite/src/features/task/components/task-columns.ts` — 活动任务列定义 (D-T3)
- `rewrite/src/features/task/components/history-columns.ts` — 历史记录列定义 (D-T3)
- `rewrite/src/features/task/composables/use-task-list.ts` — mode: active|history, shallowRef + selector
- `rewrite/src/features/task/composables/use-task-editor.ts` — 完整编辑表单 + validate + save/create/update
